### [1] [HiFi-BRep: High-Fidelity Latent Representation for Robust B-Rep Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16485)
*Junhao Hou,Chenqi Luo,Pufan Wang,Jiaying Lu,Yusheng Liu,Feiwei Qin,Meie Fang,Kun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiFi-BRep是一个用于计算机辅助设计中边界表示（B-Rep）生成的新框架，通过拓扑感知编码器（使用可学习查询消除padding并采用拓扑引导注意力防止特征污染）和单阶段并行解码器（嵌入核心流形约束为可微学习目标）同时解决表示脆弱性和生成脆弱性问题，在结构有效性和几何保真度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-Rep生成是计算机辅助设计中的基础任务，但直接合成高保真且结构有效的B-Rep仍具挑战。现有深度生成方法存在两类脆弱性：表示脆弱性（由padding噪声和潜在空间特征污染引起）和生成脆弱性（由序列化错误传播及不可微的有效性约束导致的训练-推理不匹配引起）。

Method: HiFi-BRep提出两个协同贡献：(1) 拓扑感知编码器，通过可学习查询消除padding、以拓扑引导注意力防止特征污染，构建高保真潜在表示；(2) 单阶段解码器，并行联合预测几何与拓扑，将核心流形约束嵌入为可微学习目标，实现几何与拓扑的相互引导并避免级联错误。

Result: 大量实验表明，HiFi-BRep在结构有效性和几何保真度两方面均显著优于最先进方法，为高质量B-Rep合成提供了稳健解决方案。代码和模型已公开。

Conclusion: HiFi-BRep通过同时解决表示层面的padding噪声/特征污染和生成层面的级联误差/训练-推理不匹配问题，实现了几何与拓扑的协同建模，为高保真B-Rep生成提供了可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-Rep) generation is a fundamental task in computer-aided design, yet the direct synthesis of high-fidelity and structurally valid B-Reps remains a major challenge. Existing deep generative methods suffer from two forms of brittleness: representation brittleness, caused by padding noise and feature contamination in the latent space, and generation brittleness, stemming from sequential error propagation and a train-inference mismatch due to non-differentiable validity enforcement. We propose HiFi-BRep, a novel framework that addresses these limitations through two synergistic contributions. First, a topology-aware encoder constructs a high-fidelity latent representation by eliminating padding via learnable queries and preventing feature contamination with topology-guided attention. Second, a single-stage decoder jointly predicts geometry and topology in parallel, embedding core manifold constraints as a differentiable learning objective. This design ensures mutual guidance between geometry and topology while avoiding cascaded errors. Extensive experiments show that HiFi-BRep significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both structural validity and geometric fidelity, providing a robust solution for high-quality B-Rep synthesis. Code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/1nnoh/HiFi-BRep.

</details>


### [2] [Self-Routed Tensor Adapters for Parameter-Efficient Universal Visual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16384)
*Suraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self-Routed Tensor Adapters (SRTA)，一种紧凑的多域视觉适配框架。通过低秩投影、可学习域矩阵计算路由权重，并混合共享Tucker核的切片生成样本特定适配矩阵，无需外部门控网络。在五个多域视觉分类基准上以显著更少的可训练参数达到与MoE式PEFT相当或更强的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉表示需要一个跨越异质域的自适应机制，避免将知识碎片化为域专用模块。现有的低秩适配器对所有输入使用固定子空间，在域风格、背景和语义上下文差异较大时具有限制性；而MoE式适配器虽通过多条专家路径提升专业化，但依赖外部路由器和大规模专家库，增参数并割裂路由与适配过程。

Method: SRTA将每个输入投影到低秩空间，利用可学习域矩阵从该表示计算路由权重，再以这些权重混合共享Tucker核的切片，生成样本特定的适配矩阵，整个过程无需外部门控网络。此外引入渐进式深度加权路由目标，跨适配层监督路由决策以强化路径学习。

Result: 在五个异质多域视觉分类基准上，SRTA达到与MoE风格PEFT基线竞争或略强的平均准确率，同时使用显著更少的可训练参数。rank 64时，4域设置下SRTA用2.77M参数对比MoLoRA的9.52M；6域设置下用3.00M对比14.31M。

Conclusion: SRTA提供了有效的准确率-参数权衡，使冻结视觉基础模型能向通用多域表示适配，兼具参数效率与域感知专业化能力。

Abstract: Universal visual representations require adaptation mechanisms that adapt across heterogeneous domains without fragmenting knowledge into domain-specific modules. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts frozen visual foundation models efficiently, but standard low-rank adapters use a fixed subspace for all inputs, which can be restrictive when domains differ in style, background, and semantic context. MoE-based adapters improve specialization through multiple expert pathways, but often rely on external routers and large expert banks, adding parameters and separating routing from adaptation. We propose \textbf{Self-Routed Tensor Adapters}, a compact framework for multi-domain visual adaptation. SRTA projects each input into a low-rank space, computes routing weights from this representation using a learnable domain matrix, and uses these weights to blend slices of a shared Tucker core. This produces a sample-specific adaptation matrix without an external gating network, allowing shared visual factors to be reused while supporting domain-aware specialization. To strengthen pathway learning, we introduce a progressive depth-weighted routing objective that supervises routing decisions across adapter layers. Across five heterogeneous multi-domain visual classification benchmarks, SRTA achieves competitive or slightly stronger average accuracy than MoE-style PEFT baselines while using substantially fewer trainable parameters. At rank 64, SRTA uses 2.77M parameters in the 4-domain setting compared with 9.52M for MoLoRA, and 3.00M in the 6-domain setting compared with 14.31M. Overall, SRTA offers an effective accuracy-parameter trade-off for adapting visual foundation models toward universal multi-domain representations. \href{https://github.com/surajyadav-research/SRTA}{GitHub}

</details>


### [3] [A Plug-and-Play 2D Motion Interface for Real-World Motion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15984)
*Kaname Yokoyama,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种即插即用的2D运动接口（2D Motion Interface），使3D预训练的Motion Language Models（MoLMs）能够直接接受2D运动输入，无需修改或微调原模型，从而解决了从单目视频获取3D运动数据困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoLMs通常需要将3D运动进行tokenization后再输入语言模型处理，但从单目视频中获取准确的3D运动数据非常困难，这严重限制了MoLMs在真实世界场景中的适用性。

Method: 提出一个即插即用的2D Motion Interface，其核心思路是让3D预训练的MoLMs无需任何修改或微调即可接受2D运动输入；同时构建了一个单目真实世界视频运动评估数据集，并引入了一个real-video adapter来适配该接口。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多个MoLMs上达到了与3D运动输入相当的性能，并且优于从头开始在2D运动上训练的MoLMs。此外，在构造的单目真实世界视频评估数据集上，结合real-video adapter，在单目姿态估计设置下2D运动相比3D运动表现出更好的实用性。

Conclusion: 2D运动为MoLMs在真实世界运动理解场景中的部署提供了一种实用且有效的接口，既能保持3D预训练模型的性能优势，又解决了3D运动数据获取困难的实际问题。

Abstract: Motion Language Models (MoLMs) typically understand human motions by tokenizing 3D motion and processing the resulting tokens using a language model. However, obtaining accurate 3D motions from monocular videos is challenging, limiting their real-world applicability. To address this issue, we introduce a plug-and-play 2D Motion Interface that enables 3D-pretrained MoLMs to accept 2D motion inputs without modifying or fine-tuning the original models.
  Experiments on public datasets show that our method achieves performance comparable to 3D motion inputs across multiple MoLMs and outperforms training MoLMs from scratch on 2D motions. We further construct a monocular real-world video motion evaluation dataset and introduce a real-video adapter, demonstrating the usefulness of 2D motions over 3D motions under the evaluated monocular pose-estimation setting. These results suggest that 2D motion provides a practical interface for deploying MoLMs in real-world motion understanding settings. Code is available at https://github.com/irajisamurai/2D-Motion-Interface.

</details>


### [4] [OvDSGG: End-to-End Open-Vocabulary Dynamic Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14835)
*John Helsby,Yi Yang,Bodo Rosenhahn,Michael Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OvDSGG，首个用于开放词汇动态场景图生成（open-vocabulary DSGG）的端到端框架。它基于开放词汇的空间骨干和时间骨干，通过三元组特征提取模块和视觉-语言对齐模块实现开放词汇识别，在零样本Recall@K上比次优基线高10.0-20.4个百分点，同时在闭集DSGG上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端动态场景图生成（DSGG）方法是闭集模型，只能识别固定训练词汇表中的对象和谓词，且难以应对稀有概念的长尾分布问题，严重限制了其现实应用。已有的开放词汇模型通常继承预训练大语言模型，导致多阶段训练和推理，成本高昂。因此需要一种高效、端到端的开放词汇DSGG方法。

Method: OvDSGG构建在开放词汇空间骨干（Spatial Backbone）和时间骨干（Temporal Backbone）之上，提出两个关键模块：(1) 三元组特征提取模块（Triplet Feature Extraction Module），用于桥接空间和时间骨干；(2) 视觉-语言对齐模块（Visual-Language Alignment Module），通过联合视觉-语言特征空间中学习自适应决策边界来保持开放词汇识别能力，避免了现有方法中昂贵的知识蒸馏。

Result: 在从Action Genome改编的开放词汇DSGG基准上，OvDSGG在所有指标上显著优于开放词汇基线，零样本Recall@K分数比次优基线高10.0-20.4个百分点；在闭集DSGG任务上，OvDSGG与现有最先进模型保持竞争力。此外，开放了代码和基准。

Conclusion: OvDSGG证明了端到端的开放词汇DSGG是可行的且高效，无需昂贵的知识蒸馏或多阶段训练，同时在开放词汇和闭集设置下均表现出优异的性能，为动态场景图生成的现实世界应用提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic scene graphs (DSGs) capture spatio-temporal interactions across videos as $\langle$subject, predicate, object$\rangle$ triplets, and underpin downstream tasks such as video captioning, video question answering, and action analysis. However, end-to-end dynamic scene graph generation (DSGG) methods are closed-set: they recognize only objects and predicates from a fixed training vocabulary and struggle with the long-tailed distribution of rare concepts, severely limiting their real-world applicability. Existing open-vocabulary models typically inherit pretrained large language models, resulting in multi-stage training and inference with substantial cost. We introduce OvDSGG, the first end-to-end framework for open-vocabulary DSGG. OvDSGG builds on top of an open-vocabulary Spatial Backbone and a Temporal Backbone; we further propose a Triplet Feature Extraction Module that bridges them, and a Visual-Language Alignment Module that preserves open-vocabulary recognition by learning an adaptive decision boundary in the joint visual-language feature space, without expensive knowledge distillation in existing methods. We further introduce a rigorous open-vocabulary DSGG benchmark adapted from Action Genome, with disjoint Base/Novel splits for both objects and predicates. OvDSGG significantly outperforms open-vocabulary baselines across all metrics, with zero-shot Recall@$K$ scores 10.0--20.4 percentage point higher than the next-best baseline, while on closed-set DSGG remaining competitive with state-of-the-art models. Code and benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/jhelsby/OvDSGG/.

</details>


### [5] [HOIMask: Towards Generative Masked Modeling for Human Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15141)
*Yihong Ji,Jinsong Zhang,He Hu,Hongbo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HOIMask，首个用于人-物交互（HOI）运动生成的生成式掩码框架，在离散空间中建模HOI运动，通过HOI向量量化、生成式掩码建模和接触感知重建引导，超越了基于扩散的SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的HOI生成方法虽能利用接触融合信号引导生成，但由于迭代去噪过程中的误差累积，常导致大量伪影和不稳定的交互质量。需要一种避免迭代误差累积、同时保持接触信号引导能力的生成方案。

Method: 1) HOI向量量化（VQ）：将运动序列和接触感知信号编码为离散的2D人和物体token图，保留细粒度时空结构，优于传统1D表示；2) 生成式掩码建模框架：利用Transformer架构联合捕获人-物交互动力学，建模复杂时空与交互依赖；3) 接触感知重建引导：在推理阶段的离散空间中引入引导策略，融合接触信号优化HOI tokens，使生成运动具有更高时空一致性。

Result: HOIMask在HOI运动生成任务上超越了基于扩散的SOTA方法，生成更真实且语义对齐的HOI运动，有效消除了扩散方法的伪影和不稳定交互质量。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的运动交互token、专用架构和引导策略，离散空间的生成式掩码建模为HOI运动生成提供了优于扩散式生成范式的新思路，兼顾了交互质量的真实性与语义对齐性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have dominated the HOI generation, as they enable critical contact fusions or signals to guide the diffusion process. However, they often result in high artifacts and unstable interaction quality due to error accumulation during iterative denoising. In this work, we propose HOIMask, the first generative masked framework for modeling HOI motion in discrete space. HOIMask first encodes both motion sequences and contact-aware signals into discrete 2D human and object token maps via HOI Vector Quantization (VQ), preserving fine-grained spatial-temporal structure beyond conventional 1D representations. On this basis, a generative masked modeling framework is employed to jointly capture human-object interaction dynamics, leveraging a transformer architecture designed to model complex spatial-temporal and interaction dependencies. To generate more coherent and physically plausible motions, we further introduce a novel contact-aware reconstruction guidance in discrete space during inference, which fuses contact signals to optimize HOI tokens that forces the generated motion with higher spatio-temporal consistency. With craftily designed motion interaction tokens, dedicated architecture and guidance strategy, HOIMask outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion-based methods, generating more realistic and semantically aligned HOI motions. Please refer to https://jyhflash.github.io/HOIMask/ for more results.

</details>


### [6] [PaSTel: Anchoring Histology in Spatial Transcriptomics via Multi-Scale Hierarchical Bio-Prior Contrastive Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14924)
*Azim Dehghani Amirabad,Junchao Zhu,Pushpak Pati,Walid Abdelmoula,Tommaso Mansi,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaSTel是一个分层多模态预训练框架，通过在三层（spot级、功能级、区域级）引入生物学先验来改进空间转录组学中组织学图像与基因表达的对齐，克服了现有方法中管家基因主导和缺乏空间依赖性建模的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组学多模态预训练方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 空间信息基因选择常被广泛表达的管家基因主导，导致判别性不足的表征；(2) 独立点-斑块对齐无法捕捉对组织结构至关重要的空间依赖关系。

Method: PaSTel提出层次化多模态预训练框架，在三个层面整合生物学先验：spot层面使用TF-IDF重加权识别空间信息基因；功能层面以策展的KEGG通路作为锚点编码全局生物学语义；区域层面通过空间聚类聚合邻近spot来建模中尺度组织结构。

Result: 在多个下游任务中，PaSTel一致优于现有的视觉和视觉-组学编码器，证明了引入多尺度生物学先验能够为空间转录组学产生更具信息量和可迁移性的表征。

Conclusion: 整合多尺度生物学先验的层次化预训练框架能有效克服维度灾难和空间关系缺失问题，获得更优的空间转录组学表征。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) links tissue morphology with molecular programs, motivating multimodal pretraining methods that align histology images with gene expression. However, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations: spatially informative gene selection is often dominated by ubiquitous housekeeping genes, leading to weakly discriminative representations, and independent spot-patch alignment fails to capture spatial dependencies that are critical for tissue organization. To address these challenges, we introduce PaSTel, a hierarchical multimodal pretraining framework that integrates biological priors at three levels. At the spot level, TF-IDF reweighting is used to identify spatially informative genes; at the functional level, curated KEGG pathways serve as anchors for encoding global biological semantics; and at the regional level, spatial clustering aggregates neighboring spots to model meso-scale tissue structure. Across multiple downstream tasks, PaSTel consistently outperforms existing vision and vision-omics encoders, demonstrating that incorporating multiscale biological priors yields more informative and transferable representations for spatial transcriptomics.

</details>


### [7] [Training-Free Reconstruction-Based AI-Generated Image Detectors Are Inherently Vulnerable to Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16646)
*Roman Demchenko,Jonas Ricker,Asja Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种针对基于自编码器重建误差的AI生成图像检测器的对抗攻击方法，通过构造人眼不可感知的对抗样本人为增大重建误差，使伪造图像被错误分类为真实图像。实验表明在三种SOTA生成器和三种检测器上检测性能显著下降，且攻击在经历真实世界退化后依然有效，对抗样本可跨检测器自然迁移，揭示重建型检测器的固有脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像质量日益逼真且广泛传播，需要可靠稳健的检测方法。重建型检测器因其透明性（无需训练）和可解释性成为有前景的方向，但其工作原理与传统基于分类器的检测器截然不同，其对抗鲁棒性几乎未知。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究重建型检测器在面对对抗攻击时的脆弱性。

Method: 提出两种针对利用自编码器重建误差的检测器的对抗攻击方法：通过构造人眼不可感知的对抗扰动，人为增大原始图像与重建图像之间的距离（重建误差），从而使伪造图像被错误分类为真实图像。评估涵盖三种SOTA生成器和三种检测器，并考虑图像经历真实世界退化后的攻击效果，同时考察对抗样本跨检测器的迁移性。

Result: 在三种SOTA生成器和三种检测器的评估中，所提攻击方法显著降低了检测性能，且攻击效果在图像经受真实世界退化处理后依然保持。关键发现是对抗样本在不同检测器之间自然迁移，因为它们共享相同的重建检测原理，这表明重建型检测器存在固有的安全脆弱性。

Conclusion: 重建型检测器虽然在透明性和免训练方面具有优势，但其基于重建距离的决策机制本质上易受对抗攻击。攻击的跨检测器迁移性表明这种脆弱性是原理性的而非个别实现问题，需要在设计下一代检测器时加以重视。

Abstract: The impressive visual quality and ubiquity of AI-generated images call for reliable and robust detection methods. Reconstruction-based detectors have emerged as a promising direction for transparent and training-free identification of synthetic images. However, due to their fundamentally different mode of operation (compared to standard, classifier-based methods), little is known about their adversarial robustness. In this work, we propose two novel attack methods targeted at detectors that leverage autoencoder reconstruction error. We find that by constructing imperceptible adversarial examples, the distance between original and reconstruction can be artificially increased, causing fake images to be wrongly classified as real. Our evaluation including images from three state-of-the-art generators and three detectors demonstrates that detection performance is significantly decreased, even if attacked images additionally undergo real-world degradations. Critically, our adversarial examples naturally transfer across detectors, as they all share the same principle, pointing towards an inherent vulnerability of reconstruction-based detectors.

</details>


### [8] [UC-VLM: Consistency-Driven Learning for AI-Generated Image Detection with Vision-Language Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15238)
*Lei Tan,Shuwei Li,Mohan Kankanhalli,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UC-VLM是一个统一的多阶段框架，用于AI生成图像检测，仅依赖二元监督标签，通过视觉判别目标和标签条件生成目标同时训练视觉通路和语言输出，减少对人工提示和标注推理的依赖，在GenImage和Chameleon基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLLM的AIGI检测器主要微调语言侧，对低层视觉取证线索关注不足，且依赖人工设计的提示或人工标注的推理链，限制了可扩展性。

Method: UC-VLM提出统一多阶段框架：(1) 自动识别有效指令变体；(2) 在视觉判别目标中强化对非语义取证线索的敏感度；(3) 采用标签条件生成目标，用二元标签监督文本输出。该设计将弱二元监督转化为视觉通路和语言输出共享的监督信号，无需人工推理或手工提示。

Result: 在GenImage上达到96.1%的平均准确率，超过最强先前结果4.6%；在Chameleon上ProGAN/SDV1.4训练下达到69.6%/77.9%准确率，分别超过最佳基线11.2%/15.3%。

Conclusion: UC-VLM验证了通过自动优化指令和统一二元监督信号，可以有效增强VLLM对低层视觉取证线索的敏感性，同时降低对人工提示的依赖，为AIGI检测提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Large Models (VLLMs) are promising for AI-generated image (AIGI) detection because they can produce both a prediction and a natural-language output. However, most existing VLLM-based detectors primarily fine-tune the language side while giving limited attention to low-level visual forensic cues. They also often depend on manually crafted prompts or human-annotated rationales, which limits scalability.We present UC-VLM, a unified multi-stage framework for AIGI detection that relies solely on binary supervision. UC-VLM first identifies effective instruction variants automatically. It then reuses the same binary label within a multi-stage training framework: (i) a visual discrimination objective that strengthens sensitivity to non-semantic forensic cues, and (ii) a label-conditioned generation objective that uses the binary label to supervise textual outputs. This design turns weak binary supervision into a shared supervision signal for both the visual pathway and the language output. Our key novelty is a unified multi-stage binary-supervised framework that consistently reuses the same authenticity labels for visual adaptation and label-conditioned text generation, while leveraging automatically optimized instructions to reduce prompt sensitivity without requiring human-written rationales or hand-crafted prompts.Experiments show that UC-VLM achieves 96.1% average accuracy on GenImage, exceeding the strongest prior result by 4.6%, and obtains 69.6% / 77.9% accuracy on Chameleon under ProGAN / SDV1.4 training, surpassing the best baseline by 11.2% / 15.3%, respectively.

</details>


### [9] [What You Ask is What You Ground: Bridging Question Intent to Temporal Evidence for Grounded VideoQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15708)
*Jinhwan Seo,Kyubeom Han,Jumin Lee,Junhyug Noh,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GroundFormer，解决Grounded VideoQA中"问题不变定位"（question-invariant grounding）的失效模式——模型对不同问题预测出几乎相同的时间片段。通过可学习的通信token在定位前用问题语义调制视频特征，并采用因子化MIL交叉注意力、高斯平滑和分层多模态对比损失，在NExT-GQA和STAR上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出Grounded VideoQA中存在一个被忽视的关键失效模式：question-invariant grounding，即模型对同一视频的不同问题会预测出近乎相同的时间段，缺乏问题区分性。该问题源于两类结构性缺陷：(i) 模态隔离——视频特征在接收问题语义之前就被固定；(ii) 定位模块内部的问题注入过于薄弱。

Method: GroundFormer包含三部分核心设计：1) 通过可学习的通信token在定位前用问题意图调制视频特征，实现有向的视觉-语言交互；2) 基于问题条件化特征的因子化MIL（多示例学习）交叉注意力，在候选级监督下将答案选择与时间证据耦合；3) 高斯平滑将峰值注意力转换为时间上连贯的片段；此外引入分层多模态对比损失，通过两阶段训练对齐视频、问题和答案的嵌入。

Result: GroundFormer在NExT-GQA和STAR基准上取得了Grounded VideoQA的最优（SOTA）性能，显著提升了具有问题区分性的时间定位能力。

Conclusion: 作者通过直接针对问题不变定位这一缺陷进行方法设计，验证了"定位前进行问题条件化视频特征调制"这一范式的有效性，表明增强问题意识是提升Grounded VideoQA时间定位质量的关键。

Abstract: We study a critical yet overlooked failure mode in Grounded Video Question Answering: question-invariant grounding, where models predict nearly identical temporal segments for different questions about the same video. We trace this behavior to two structural limitations in prior common designs: (i) modality isolation that fixes video representations before they receive question semantics, and (ii) weak question injection inside the grounding module. To address this, we propose GroundFormer, which conditions video features on question intent before localization via learnable communication tokens that mediate directed visuo-lingual interaction. On top of the question-conditioned features, a factorized MIL cross-attention couples answer selection with temporal evidence under candidate-level supervision, while Gaussian smoothing converts peaked attention into temporally coherent segments. We further introduce a hierarchical multi-modal contrastive loss that aligns video, question, and answer embeddings across a two-pass training pipeline. GroundFormer achieves state-of-the-art grounded VideoQA performance on NExT-GQA and STAR, substantially improving question-discriminative temporal grounding.

</details>


### [10] [PersonaDrive: Controllable Trajectory Prediction with Multi-Dimensional Driving Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15230)
*Chan Lee,Kimin Yun,Yuseok Bae,Seong Tae Kim,Jung Uk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双轴（时间紧迫性与乘坐舒适性）九宫格人格条件轨迹数据集PCT，以及PersonaDrive框架，通过语言学习驾驶人格并生成对应轨迹，实验超越多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测和端到端自动驾驶方法虽在城市场景中鲁棒性提升，但缺乏有意义的可控性。已有基准要么缺少人格条件标注，要么仅支持单一紧迫性谱系，无法区分共享同一紧迫性等级但需要不同驾驶动力学的人格。

Method: 提出(i)PCT数据集，将驾驶人格沿时间紧迫性与乘坐舒适性两个轴分解，各组合三个等级形成九种人格，并配自然语言描述与轨迹；(ii)PersonaDrive框架，包含Persona-Conditioned Anchor Transform (PCAT)沿两轴分层重构锚点，以及Persona-Conditioned Multi-Modal Fusion (PCMF)做BEV级人格融合。训练由Hierarchical Guide Loss强制轴对齐物理排序，以及Axis-Decomposed Diversity Loss防止对角模态坍缩监督。

Result: 实验表明PersonaDrive在多维场景中一致优于对比基线。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive能够从语言学习驾驶人格并生成人格特定的轨迹，提升了自动驾驶的可控性和可区分性，代码与PCT数据集已公开。

Abstract: Although recent trajectory prediction and end-to-end autonomous driving methods improve robustness in urban environments, they still lack meaningful controllability. Existing benchmarks either provide no persona-conditioned annotations or support only a single urgency spectrum (i.e., emergency, normal, relaxed), which cannot distinguish personas that share the same urgency level but require different driving dynamics. To address this, we propose (i) the Persona-Conditioned Trajectory (PCT) dataset, which decomposes driving personas along two axes, Temporal Urgency and Ride Comfort, and combines three levels of each to form a grid of nine personas, each paired with natural-language descriptions and trajectories, and (ii) PersonaDrive, a framework that can learn driving personas from language and can generate persona-specific trajectories. PersonaDrive incorporates Persona-Conditioned Anchor Transform (PCAT), which hierarchically reshapes anchors along both axes, and Persona-Conditioned Multi-Modal Fusion (PCMF) for BEV-level persona fusion. Training is supervised by a Hierarchical Guide Loss enforcing axis-aligned physical orderings and an Axis-Decomposed Diversity Loss preventing diagonal mode collapse. Experimental results show that PersonaDrive consistently improves over the compared baselines across multi-dimensional scenarios. The code and PCT dataset are available at https://github.com/VisualAIKHU/PersonaDrive

</details>


### [11] [Towards Real-Time and Adaptable LiDAR Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16490)
*Azhar Hussian,Martin Vossiek,Vasileios Belagiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RapidLiDAR，一种无需手动噪声调参、以学习方式初始化场景的LiDAR场景补全方法。通过自适应初始化模块预测逐点空间位移，结合多尺度体素和BEV特征细化模块，在0.1秒内完成整场景补全，比现有最快方法快2.3倍，达到典型车载LiDAR 10 Hz采集速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR场景补全方法遵循初始化-细化范式：生成式方法通过迭代去噪随机高斯噪声实现补全，速度慢；非生成式方法用固定噪声尺度扰动部分场景，难以覆盖大间隙和遮挡区域，且需针对不同传感器配置手动重新校准噪声参数。

Method: RapidLiDAR包含两个核心模块：(1) 自适应初始化模块——对每个局部输入点预测空间变化的位移，将部分观测扩展为适应局部几何的粗场景初始化，无需手动噪声调参；(2) 多尺度细化模块——通过查询从输入扫描构建的多尺度3D体素和2D BEV特征图来进一步细化点位置。架构用体素和BEV特征提取替代最远点采样和k近邻搜索等点邻域算子，使其更快且天然适配不同输入分辨率。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和KITTI-360数据集上，RapidLiDAR达到与最先进方法相当的补全性能，同时以0.1秒完成全场景补全，比最快的现有方法快2.3倍，匹配典型车载LiDAR传感器的10 Hz采集速率。

Conclusion: RapidLiDAR通过将初始化本身视为可学习的数据驱动组件，消除了手动噪声调参需求，同时以体素/BEV特征提取替代昂贵的点邻域算子，在保证补全性能的前提下实现了实时LiDAR场景补全，向真实自动驾驶场景中的实时3D感知迈进一步。

Abstract: LiDAR scene completion is a key component of 3D perception in autonomous driving, where the scene must be completed in real time to be usable in downstream tasks. Existing approaches typically follow an initialize-and-refine paradigm, in which a coarse initialization of the scene is first constructed, then refined into complete 3D geometry. Generative models are slower because they iteratively refine random Gaussian noise into the scene, while non-generative methods perturb the partial scene with a fixed noise scale, which limits coverage of large gaps and occluded regions and requires manual recalibration for each new sensor configuration. We present RapidLiDAR, a LiDAR scene completion method that treats the initialization itself as a learned, data-driven component. We propose an adaptive initialization module that predicts a spatially varying displacement for each partial input point, expanding the partial observations into a coarse scene initialization adapted to the local geometry, without requiring manual noise tuning. To refine this coarse initialization into a complete and coherent scene, we additionally propose a multi-scale reconstruction module that further refines point positions by querying multi-scale 3D voxel and 2D BEV feature maps constructed from the input scan. By replacing point-neighborhood operators such as farthest point sampling and $k$-nearest neighbor search with voxel- and BEV-based feature extraction, our architecture is faster and can handle different input resolutions by design. Experiments on SemanticKITTI and KITTI-360 show that our method achieves completion performance on par with the state of the art while completing a full scene in 0.1 seconds, which is 2.3 times faster than the fastest prior method. This matches the 10 Hz acquisition rate of typical automotive LiDAR sensors, taking a step toward real-time LiDAR scene completion.

</details>


### [12] [NARRATE: A Multimodal Real-World Australian Driving Dataset for Human-Centred Explanations in Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14767)
*Ashkan Yousefi Zadeh,Zishuo Zhu,Xiaomeng Li,Andry Rakotonirainy,Sebastien Glaser,Ronald Schroeter,Patricia Delhomme,Zahra Mehraban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍NARRATE，一个多模态真实世界澳大利亚驾驶数据集，包含来自35名经验丰富司机和驾驶教练的2,050个注释事件，每个事件配有同步视觉、定位、运动、LiDAR流和车内/驾驶后自由文本解释。该数据集提供动作标签、6个高层次和32个细粒度场景上下文标签，以及跨度级情境感知(SA)注释。四个基准任务表明该结构可从驾驶员语言中学习，但细粒度上下文识别和解释生成仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言注释驾驶数据集多为观察者撰写、事后构建、基于模拟或由传感器输入生成，而非从执行动作的驾驶员处直接获取。这使得自动驾驶解释难以真正反映驾驶员的理解和决策过程，缺乏人本中心的解释模型基础。

Method: 构建NARRATE多模态驾驶数据集：在公共道路上采集35名驾驶员和驾驶教练的驾驶数据，同步视觉、定位、运动、LiDAR多模态流；采用车内和/或驾驶后的自由文本解释采集方式；提供动作标签、6个高层次和32个细粒度场景上下文标签，以及对驾驶员解释中感知(Perception)、理解(Comprehension)和预测(Projection)三个层面的SA注释。

Result: 四个基准任务（SA、场景上下文、驾驶员动作分类和解释生成）的评估表明该数据结构可从驾驶员语言中学习。但细粒度上下文识别和解释生成仍具有挑战性，说明现有方法在这两个任务上还有提升空间。

Conclusion: NARRATE为构建更以人为中心、领域感知的自动驾驶解释模型铺平了道路，弥补了驾驶解释数据来自真实驾驶员而非观察者或仿真的空白。

Abstract: Automated vehicles must explain their decisions in ways that passengers can understand, monitor, and trust. Existing language-annotated driving datasets are mostly observer-written, post-hoc, simulation-based, or generated from sensor inputs, rather than elicited from the driver performing the action. We introduce NARRATE, a multimodal real-world Australian driving dataset comprising 2,050 annotated events from 35 experienced drivers and driving instructors on public roads. Each event is grounded in synchronised visual, localisation, motion, and LiDAR streams and paired with in-vehicle and/or post-drive free-text explanations. NARRATE provides action labels, scenario-context labels spanning six high-level and 32 fine-grained categories, and span-level Situational Awareness (SA) annotations over driver explanations for Perception, Comprehension and Projection. Four benchmark tasks (SA, scenario-context, driver-action classification, and explanation generation) show that this structure is learnable from driver language, while fine-grained context recognition and explanation generation remain challenging. NARRATE paves a path towards more human-centred and domain-aware explanation models for automated driving.

</details>


### [13] [Beyond Single Object: Learning 3D Relations with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15710)
*Kohsuke Ide,Ryousuke Yamada,Yue Qiu,Xianzheng Ma,Yoshihiro Fukuhara,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Yutaka Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Multi-3DLLM框架，解决3D-LLMs在多物体细粒度比较推理上的空白，包含数据、模型和评测基准三部分，在MO3D基准上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-LLMs主要聚焦于单物体/单场景描述，缺乏跨物体的细节比较推理能力。作者旨在填补这一空白，使模型能够进行跨多物体的细粒度物体级推理。

Method: 提出三组件框架：(1) MO3D指令数据集，要求细粒度多物体比较；(2) Multi-3DLLM模型，采用最小化的Patch-Interaction Transformer (PIT)建模物体间/物体内关系同时保留局部几何；(3) Mini-apps，包含Shape Mating和Change Captioning两个应用驱动的基准，用于测试几何理解的实际用途。

Result: 近期3D-LLMs和2D-VLMs在MO3D任务上表现不佳，缺乏比较中心设计和几何感知能力。Multi-3DLLM在混合数据上训练后学会了几何推理，在MO3D上超越所有基线，并对单物体分类任务产生正向迁移。

Conclusion: Multi-3DLLM通过比较中心的建模和混合数据训练，成功实现了多物体细粒度几何推理，并证明该能力可正向迁移至单物体任务，为3D-LLMs的多物体理解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We address a fundamental gap in 3D-LLMs: existing models focus on single-object/scene description, struggling with detailed, inter-object comparison. We propose a framework for detailed object-level reasoning across multiple objects with three components: (1) MO3D (Multi-Object in 3D), an instruction dataset requiring fine-grained multi-object comparison; (2) Multi-3DLLM, using a minimal Patch-Interaction Transformer (PIT) that models inter-/intra-object relationships while preserving local geometry; (3) Mini-apps, two application-driven benchmarks (Shape Mating, Change Captioning) that probe geometric understanding for practical use. Recent 3D-LLMs and 2D-VLMs perform poorly on these tasks, lacking both comparison-centric design and geometric awareness. In contrast, Multi-3DLLM trained on our mixture data learns geometric reasoning, surpasses all baselines on MO3D, and provides positive transfer to single-object classification.

</details>


### [14] [JoLT: Joint Latent Trajectories for Context-Guided High-Resolution Tiled Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15395)
*Mathis Koroglu,Guillaume Jeanneret,Hugo Caselles-Dupré,Matthieu Cord,Arnaud Dapogny*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoLT是一个用于生成高分辨率详细图像的文本到图像生成方法，通过双流联合去噪低分辨率和高分辨率潜在图像来实现，其中LR潜在控制全局布局，HR潜在控制细节，两个分支互连以整合信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成模型在生成密集细节的高分辨率图像时表现不佳，当前采用低到高分辨率的级联方法（先生成LR图像再上采样）存在局限性。

Method: JoLT提出联合潜在轨迹方法，采用双流架构在每个采样步骤同时去噪LR和HR潜在图像。LR潜在流控制整体布局，HR潜在流控制细节，两个分支相互连接以整合信息，替代传统先生成LR再上采样的级联方式。

Result: 广泛验证表明JoLT优于现有竞争基线，生成图像不仅细节丰富而且视觉上令人愉悦。

Conclusion: JoLT为高分辨率文本到图像生成提供了新途径，生成的高质量图像为艺术创作打开了新的可能性。

Abstract: Although text-to-image generative models produce impressive results, they struggle to generate densely detailed, high-resolution (HR) images. Current literature addresses this issue with a low-to-high-resolution approach. First, a low-resolution (LR) image is generated. Then, an upsampled version is generated using the LR image as an additional cue. In this paper, we present Joint Latent Trajectories (JoLT). To generate an image, JoLT uses two streams that jointly denoise LR and HR latent images at each sampling step. The LR latent controls the overall layout, while the HR latent controls the details. We interconnect both branches to jointly integrate their information. We extensively validate our method, demonstrating its advantages over competing baselines. The resulting images are not only richly detailed but also visually pleasing, opening new avenues for artistic creation.

</details>


### [15] [CrossView: Can Vision-Language Models Reason Across Cameras?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15539)
*Sahil Shah,S P Sharan,Harsh Goel,Manvik Pasula,Adithya Hebbalae,Minkyu Choi,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrossView，一个多视角视频问答基准，覆盖自动驾驶、安防监控、自我中心/外部中心视频和机器人领域。评估GPT-5.2和Qwen3-VL等模型发现准确率普遍较低，开源模型大幅落后，模型联合处理多视角的能力与性能强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准大多聚焦单摄像头场景，但现实世界（自动驾驶、安防系统、机器人）本质上是多摄像头网络。多摄像头推理与单摄像头问题存在本质差异，需要处理随视角数量增长的上下文、跨视角遮挡、视角重要性判断以及跨视角证据整合。然而现有模型在这些挑战上表现不佳，且缺乏系统针对该问题的基准。

Method: 构建CrossView多摄像头视频问答基准，涵盖自动驾驶、安防监控、自我中心/外部中心视频、机器人四大领域，系统评估专有模型（如GPT-5.2）和开源模型（如Qwen3-VL）的多视角推理能力。

Result: 专有和开源模型在CrossView上准确率普遍较低，开源模型与专有模型差距明显。模型性能与其联合处理多视角的能力呈强正相关关系。

Conclusion: CrossView作为多摄像头视频理解的严格基准，揭示了现有模型在该任务上的不足，并为该方向提供了系统化的评估工具和开源数据集。

Abstract: Video understanding benchmarks have long centered on single-camera settings, where modern multi-modal language models achieve strong performance across image and video tasks. Yet, the real world runs on multi-camera networks: autonomous vehicles, security systems, and robots all gather data across many simultaneous views. We argue that this is not simply "more" of the single-camera problem; it is fundamentally different. Multi-camera reasoning requires handling context that scales with the number of views, resolving occlusions visible from only a subset of cameras, judging which views matter, and integrating evidence across perspectives that may overlap or diverge. Current models struggle with exactly these challenges, yet no benchmark systematically targets them. We introduce CrossView, a multi-camera video question-answering benchmark spanning autonomous driving, security surveillance, egocentric/exocentric video, and robotics. Evaluation of proprietary models, such as GPT-5.2, and open-source models, like Qwen3-VL, reveals consistently low accuracy, with open-source models trailing by a wide margin. Performance scales strongly with a model's ability to jointly process multiple viewpoints, positioning CrossView as a rigorous benchmark for multi-camera video. We open-source our code and dataset at https://utaustin-swarmlab.github.io/CrossView.

</details>


### [16] [EgoGazeLite: On-Device Egocentric Gaze Prediction for Token-Efficient Multimodal LLM Video Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15614)
*Matteo Stoiber,Niels Buus Lassen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoGazeLite是一个轻量级双过程注视预测器，以15.7M参数、6.71 GFLOPs的规模在消费级加速硬件上实时（21.6ms/帧）运行，无需眼动追踪硬件即可实现基于注视裁剪的多模态LLM第一视角视频理解。在两种MLLM、三种自动化指标和两种LLM评判下，预测注视裁剪与真实注视裁剪在全部十种情况中均确认无显著差异，消除了硬件眼动追踪的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM用于可穿戴设备的自我中心视频理解受限于token预算，高分辨率视频的视觉token数量导致内存和计算成本过高。先前工作GazeLLM通过围绕注视点裁剪视频将视觉token减少约十倍，但依赖专用眼动追踪硬件，在消费级智能眼镜上不可用。需要在设备端运行的软件替代方案，需同时满足准确性和轻量性双重约束。

Method: 提出EgoGazeLite，一个轻量级双过程（dual-process）注视预测器，用于预测自我中心视频中的注视点，以替代硬件眼动追踪。该方法将预测注视点用于视频裁剪，生成稀疏的视觉token输入给MLLM，在不依赖专用硬件的情况下实现GazeLLM式的token压缩收益。

Result: 在两种MLLM、三种自动化指标和两种LLM评判的评估设置下，预测注视裁剪与真实注视裁剪无显著差异，十种情况全部确认等价。模型仅15.7M参数、6.71 GFLOPs，在消费级加速硬件上端到端实时运行（21.6ms/帧）。

Conclusion: EgoGazeLite成功以纯软件方式替代了眼动追踪硬件，实现了token高效的、基于注视条件的自我中心视频理解，在保证下游生成质量的同时满足设备端的计算和能耗约束，消除了对专用eye-tracking硬件的依赖。

Abstract: The use of multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) for egocentric video understanding with wearable devices is constrained by the token budget. Memory and compute cost scale with the number of visual tokens, and high-resolution video quickly becomes expensive to transmit and process at scale. Prior work (GazeLLM) addresses this by cropping the video around the camera wearer's gaze. This reduces the number of visual tokens by about tenfold while maintaining or improving the quality of full-resolution descriptions. However, this compression strategy depends on dedicated eye-tracking hardware, which is unavailable on consumer smart glasses. Building a software-only substitute poses a joint constraint: the predictor must be accurate enough to preserve downstream description quality, yet light enough to run on-device, within the power and compute budget of a smartphone. We address this with EgoGazeLite, a lightweight dual-process gaze predictor for egocentric video. Across two MLLMs, three automated metrics, and two LLM judges, predicted-gaze crops show no significant difference from ground-truth-gaze crops. Equivalence is confirmed in all ten cases. EgoGazeLite achieves this at 15.7M parameters, 6.71 GFLOPs, and runs the full gaze-and-crop pipeline end-to-end in real time (21.6 ms/frame) on consumer accelerator hardware. Together, these results remove the need for eye-tracking hardware for token-efficient, gaze-conditioned egocentric video understanding with MLLMs.

</details>


### [17] [Depth-guided Multi-view Exposure Bracketing for HDR Robot Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16014)
*Jinnyeong Kim,Juhyung Choi,Woohyeok Kim,Sunghyun Cho,Seung-Hwan Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向多传感器机器人系统的单次曝光HDR成像基准数据集，包含121个真实场景和20个CARLA仿真视频序列，并提出了基于深度引导的多视角曝光括架（DMEB）参考方法，在极端光照条件下实现了鲁棒的HDR感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端光照条件下的可靠单次HDR成像长期是一大挑战，但现有研究缺乏针对多传感器机器人系统的全面HDR感知基准，无法公正评估不同方法在该场景下的表现。

Method: （1）构建大规模数据集：通过定制机器人视觉平台和iPhone 13 Pro采集121个真实世界场景（覆盖中等及超高动态范围），并从CARLA仿真器生成20个合成视频序列。（2）提出DMEB参考管道：利用多视角低比特深度相机分布差异化曝光，并通过深度引导的置信度感知融合进行单次HDR重建。

Result: 在自建数据集上的评估表明，DMEB方法建立了强参考基准，验证了该传感器配置（多相机+深度传感器）在多样化环境下实现鲁棒HDR感知的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多传感器机器人系统中HDR感知基准的空白，所提出的数据集和DMEB参考方法为后续研究提供了可比较的评价平台和技术基线，展示了多视角曝光括架结合深度信息在极端光照下HDR重建的可行性。

Abstract: Achieving reliable single-shot high dynamic range (HDR) imaging under extreme illumination conditions remains a long-standing challenge, yet no comprehensive benchmark exist for evaluating HDR perception in multi-sensor robotic systems. To fill this gap, we introduce a large-scale dataset collected via a custom robotic vision platform and an iPhone 13 Pro: 121 real-world scenes spanning modest and ultra-high dynamic range conditions, alongside 20 synthetic video sequences from the CARLA simulator. As a reference pipeline for this dataset, we propose Depth-guided Multi-view Exposure Bracketing (DMEB), a single-shot HDR method that distributes drastically different exposures across multi-view low-bit-depth cameras and fuses them via depth-guided confidence-aware fusion. Evaluations on our dataset show that DMEB establishes a strong reference point and highlight the promise of this sensor configuration for robust HDR perception in diverse multi-camera and depth sensor system.

</details>


### [18] [Periocular Soft Biometrics: A Survey and Applications to Multimedia Forensics and Disinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14701)
*Fernando Alonso-Fernandez,Kevin Hernandez-Diaz,Josef Bigun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对基于眼周（periocular）图像的人口统计属性（性别、年龄、种族）估计进行了系统性综述，涵盖公开数据集、从手工特征到深度学习的方**法发展趋势、当前技术水平以及多媒体取证和虚假信息检测等应用场景，并讨论了数据集偏差、跨域泛化、公平性和伦理等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无法进行完整身份识别的情况下，软生物特征（如性别、年龄、种族）可为取证调查、身份验证、监控及合成媒体检测提供重要的辅助证据。眼周区域作为面部中常被遮挡但仍可见的区域，是可靠的软生物特征来源，但目前缺乏针对该方向的系统性综述。

Method: 采用综述（survey）方法，对眼周图像人口统计属性估计领域进行系统梳理：涵盖公开数据集、方法论演化趋势（从手工特征描述子到深度学习架构）、性别/年龄/种族预测的当前技术水平，并讨论相关应用场景与开放挑战。

Result: 综述了眼周图像人口统计属性估计的最新进展，明确了其在多媒体取证、虚假信息检测（如监控视频人口统计过滤、年龄验证、合成数据中人口统计学不一致检测）中的应用价值，并识别了数据集偏差、跨域泛化、公平性、伦理问题及缺乏取证导向基准等关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 眼周软生物特征在人口统计属性估计中具有重要应用潜力，但该领域仍面临数据偏差、跨域泛化、公平性与伦理等核心挑战，亟需构建专门的取证导向基准数据集以推动技术发展。

Abstract: Soft-biometric attributes such as gender, age, and ethnicity provide valuable ancillary evidence when full identity recognition is not feasible, supporting applications in forensic investigation, identity verification, surveillance, or detection of synthetic and manipulated media. Among biometric modalities, the periocular region is a robust source of soft-biometric cues, as it often remains visible when other parts of the face are occluded, a frequent condition in forensic evidence and surveillance footage, and can be captured across a wide range of acquisition conditions. In this paper, we provide a survey of demographic attribute estimation from periocular images, covering publicly available datasets, methodological trends from handcrafted descriptors to deep learning architectures, and the state of the art in gender, age, and ethnicity prediction. We discuss use cases relevant to multimedia forensics and disinformation-detection applications, including demographic filtering in surveillance footage, age verification, and the detection of demographic inconsistencies in synthetic data. We also highlight open challenges, including dataset bias, cross-domain generalisation, fairness, ethical aspects, and the lack of forensic-oriented benchmarks.

</details>


### [19] [SIGMA-Lane: Scale-pyramId Gated MAmba for Temporally Consistent Video Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16338)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Mengmeng Wang,Yan Gao,Xiangjie Kong,Guojiang Shen,Jiaxin Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIGMA-Lane，一种针对视频车道线检测中遮挡导致的状态污染问题的SSM时域建模方法，通过在SSM写入和残差融合路径上放置遮挡感知门控来控制观测进入时序记忆的方式，并结合结构空间检索从历史先验中恢复缺失的车道结构，在VIL-100和OpenLane-V上验证了遮挡下的时序稳定性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频车道线检测需要在帧间保持预测稳定，但严重的车辆遮挡会破坏时序线索。在流式循环模型中，被污染（损坏）的观测可能进入隐状态，导致错误持续传播到后续帧。现有遮挡感知方法通常仅将障碍物掩码作为辅助输入，未能直接保护状态更新路径，缺乏对状态污染这一失效模式的针对性处理。

Method: 将遮挡导致的失效模式建模为SSM时域建模中的状态污染问题。具体地，在SSM的写入路径和残差融合路径上放置遮挡感知门控，控制当前观测如何进入时序记忆以及在时序传播后如何融合回来。模型包含两条互补路径：(1) 经过坐标一致性仿射对齐后，使用SSM一致的遮挡感知双门控进行时序滤波；(2) 结构空间检索（SSR）从对齐的历史先验中恢复被遮挡缺失的车道结构。

Result: 在VIL-100和OpenLane-V数据集上的实验表明，SIGMA-Lane在重度遮挡条件下显著提升了时序稳定性，同时取得了具有竞争力的F1和mIoU分数。

Conclusion: 通过区分"观测受遮挡"与"状态被污染"这两个层面，直接在SSM状态更新路径上施加遮挡感知门控，有效缓解了遮挡导致的错误在时序上的持续传播，在时序稳定性与空间精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Video lane detection requires predictions that remain stable across frames, yet severe vehicle occlusions can break temporal cues. In streaming recurrent models, corrupted observations may enter the hidden state and produce errors that persist into later frames. Existing occlusion-aware refinements usually provide obstacle masks as auxiliary inputs, so the state-update path is only indirectly protected. We propose SIGMA-Lane, which treats this failure mode as state contamination in State Space Model (SSM)-based temporal modeling. SIGMA-Lane places occlusion-aware gates on the SSM write and residual-fusion paths, controlling how current observations enter temporal memory and are fused back after temporal propagation. After coordinate-consistent affine alignment, the model combines two complementary paths: SSM-consistent dual-gating for temporal filtering and Structural Spatial Retrieval (SSR) for recovering missing lane structure from aligned historical priors. Experiments on VIL-100 and OpenLane-V show improved temporal stability under heavy occlusion, with competitive F1 and mIoU scores.

</details>


### [20] [LightLoc++: Sensor-Robust Representation Learning for Efficient Outdoor LiDAR Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15317)
*Wen Li,Shangshu Yu,Dunqiang Liu,Qiming Xia,Sheng Ao,Siqi Shen,Chenglu Wen,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightLoc++是一个面向户外LiDAR定位的传感器鲁棒且高效的场景坐标回归框架。作者发现现有的解耦式SCR方法在不同LiDAR传感器配置下精度明显下降，因此提出通过跨传感器一致性学习来预训练传感器鲁棒骨干网络，并引入样本分类引导和冗余样本下采样以提高新场景训练效率。配套发布了包含32/64/128线LiDAR的同步城市多LiDAR数据集SULID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SCR在户外LiDAR定位中性能优异但需要数天的场景专属训练，限制了实际部署。近期的解耦方法（场景无关骨干+场景特定轻量头部）虽然提升了训练效率，但高度依赖预训练骨干，当新场景的LiDAR传感器与预训练时配置不同时精度显著下降。作者认为高效LiDAR定位需要跨LiDAR配置稳定的场景几何表征，但目前缺乏相应的数据和方法。

Method: 1) 构建SULID数据集：包含32、64、128线旋转LiDAR的同步城市多LiDAR数据集，具有广泛的跨传感器重叠和多样城市场景；2) 使用跨传感器一致性学习预训练传感器鲁棒的骨干网络；3) 引入样本分类引导以降低回归歧义；4) 引入冗余样本下采样以减少大规模户外场景中的计算冗余。

Result: 在多个户外LiDAR定位基准上，LightLoc++达到最先进的定位精度，同时新场景训练成本是最低的。实验验证了传感器鲁棒性设计对跨LiDAR配置泛化能力的提升。

Conclusion: LightLoc++证明了通过跨传感器一致性预训练和高效训练策略，可以在保持SOTA定位精度的同时显著降低新场景训练成本，且对不同的LiDAR传感器配置具有鲁棒性，推动了SCR在户外LiDAR定位中的实际部署。

Abstract: Scene coordinate regression (SCR) achieves strong performance in outdoor LiDAR localization, but it usually requires scene-specific training that can take days, limiting practical deployment. Recent works improve training efficiency by decoupling SCR into a scene-agnostic backbone and scene-specific prediction heads, where the backbone is pretrained on source datasets and frozen for new scenes, and only lightweight heads are optimized. However, we find that this paradigm heavily depends on the pretrained backbone. Existing decoupled methods can match conventional SCR methods fully optimized for each new scene when LiDAR configurations are similar to those used during backbone pretraining, but their accuracy drops noticeably on datasets collected with different LiDAR sensors. This suggests that efficient LiDAR localization requires representations that capture stable scene geometry across LiDAR configurations. Motivated by this observation, we propose LightLoc++, a sensor-robust and efficient outdoor LiDAR localization framework. To support sensor-robust representation learning, we introduce SULID, a synchronized urban multi-LiDAR dataset with representative 32-, 64-, and 128-beam rotating LiDARs, extensive cross-sensor overlap, and diverse urban scenes. Using SULID, we pretrain a sensor-robust backbone through cross-sensor consistency learning. LightLoc++ further preserves efficient new-scene learning by incorporating sample classification guidance and redundant sample downsampling, which reduce regression ambiguity and computational redundancy in large-scale outdoor scenes. Extensive experiments on multiple outdoor LiDAR localization benchmarks demonstrate that LightLoc++ achieves state-of-the-art localization performance with the lowest new-scene training cost among compared methods. Code and dataset will be made available at https://github.com/liw95/LightLoc-PlusPlus.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond Independence: Learning Correlated Views for Variational Incomplete Multi-View Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15757)
*Zheming Xu,Aiyue Tang,Shidi Chen,Xuechao Zou,Congyan Lang,Rogelio A. Mancisidor,Michael Kampffmeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ACOVA，一个突破条件独立性假设的可变分非完整多视图聚类框架。通过利用后验估计误差的协方差结构显式建模跨视图相关性，并使用归一化Cholesky分解参数化相关矩阵以保证正定性，实现统一的可变分目标联合训练。实验表明该方法在多个IMVC基准上持续优于SOTA方法，且仅引入极少量可学习参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于变分推断的免插补（imputation-free）不完全多视图聚类方法虽然对缺失视图具有鲁棒性，但在后验聚合阶段普遍依赖跨视图条件独立性假设，无法捕捉多视图数据固有的结构化关联和相关性，限制了聚类效果的提升。

Method: 提出一个显式超越独立性假设的可变分框架。核心是：1）在聚合阶段利用后验估计误差的协方差结构来显式建模并学习视图间的相关性；2）通过归一化Cholesky分解参数化相关矩阵，确保正定性并支持端到端训练；3）整个模型通过统一的可变分目标函数联合优化。

Result: 在多个IMVC基准数据集和多样的缺失视图设置下，该方法一致性地超越了现有最先进的聚类方法，同时仅引入可忽略数量的可学习参数，验证了自适应相关建模在变分IMVC中的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了在非完整多视图聚类中超越条件独立性假设的必要性，所提出的自适应相关性建模方法显著提升了变分IMVC的性能，为未来的相关工作提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Incomplete multi-view clustering (IMVC) aims to uncover shared cluster structures from data with partially observed views. Although recent imputation-free methods based on variational inference demonstrate robustness to missing views, they commonly rely on a conditional independence assumption across views in the posterior aggregation stage, which fails to capture the inherently structured and potentially correlated nature of multi-view data. In this paper, we propose a variational framework that explicitly goes beyond this assumption by introducing a learnable cross-view correlation structure. Specifically, we explicitly model and learn correlations between views by utilizing the covariance structure of posterior estimation errors during aggregation. To facilitate robust and efficient learning, the correlation matrix is parameterized through a normalized Cholesky decomposition, ensuring positive definiteness and enabling the entire model to be trained jointly through a unified variational objective. Extensive experiments on multiple IMVC benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across diverse missing-view settings while introducing only a negligible number of learnable parameters. These results highlight the effectiveness of adaptive correlation modeling in variational IMVC, demonstrating the need to go beyond the independence assumption in IMVC. The code is available at https://github.com/zmxu196/ACOVA.

</details>


### [22] [Bridging the Gap between Labeled and Unlabeled Data via Unified Flow with Feature Memory Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16681)
*Shanwen Wang,Xin Sun,Danfeng Hong,Junyu Dong,Patrick Le Callet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UFFM方法，通过统一流（UF）与特征记忆库（FMB）解决遥感半监督语义分割中标记数据主导、伪标签质量低的问题。UF结合外部视觉基础模型与遥感域教师生成低偏差伪标签并统一优化目标；FMB动态更新类特定特征并对齐类特征以减少标记与未标记数据的特征差异。实验表明该方法优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督语义分割（S^4）方法独立训练标记与未标记数据，导致标记数据在网络中占据主导地位，严重降低了伪标签质量。这一现象在遥感（RS）领域尤为突出，因此需要一种新的训练机制来平衡两类数据、提升伪标签质量。

Method: UFFM包含两个核心创新：（1）统一流（UF）：一种新的训练流程，将外部视觉基础模型（VFM）与遥感域教师相结合以生成偏差更小的伪标签，并在统一的训练目标下联合优化标记与伪标记数据；（2）特征记忆库（FMB）：一种新颖的S^4记忆模块，训练过程中动态更新类特定特征，通过类特征对齐缩小标记与未标记数据之间的特征差异。

Result: 在遥感数据集上的大量实验表明，UFFM方法在性能上优于现有SOTA半监督语义分割方法，并验证了其在弥合标记与未标记数据之间优化与特征表示差距方面的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: UFFM通过统一训练流程和类特征对齐机制，有效解决了遥感半监督语义分割中标记数据主导和伪标签质量退化的问题，为领域内提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Although semi-supervised semantic segmentation ($\text{S}^4$) utilizes abundant unlabeled data to reduce manual labeling burdens, independent training of labeled and unlabeled data causes the former to dominate, which severely degrades pseudo-label quality. To address this challenges, we propose a novel remote sensing (RS) $\text{S}^4$ method via unified flow with feature memory bank (UFFM). Specifically, UFFM comprises two key innovations: unified flow (UF) and feature memory bank (FMB). The UF is a new training flow that generates less biased pseudo-labels by combining an external visual foundation model (VFM) with an RS domain teacher, and jointly optimizes labeled and pseudo-labeled data under a unified training objective. The FMB is a novel memory module for $\text{S}^4$ that dynamically updates class-specific features during training and reduces the feature discrepancy between labeled and unlabeled data through class-feature alignment. To verify the effectiveness of our model, we conduct extensive experiments on RS datasets. The experimental results show the superiority of our method over SOTA $\text{S}^4$ methods. Moreover, the results demonstrate the effectiveness of our contributions in bridging the optimization and feature representation gap between labeled and unlabeled data. Our code is released at \href{https://github.com/wangshanwen001/RS-UFFM}{https://github.com/wangshanwen001/RS-UFFM}.

</details>


### [23] [Memory-Bounded Continuation of Greedy Sampling for Continual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15277)
*Yoon Gyo Jung,Jaewoo Park,Kuan-Chuan Peng,Seongdeok Bang,Octavia Camps*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ContCore是一种面向连续异常检测的固定内存核心集方法，通过"贪心扩展+贪心合并"的两阶段策略持续贪心采样，在严格内存限制下保持核心集代表性，理论上保证与oracle核心集的差距有界，并在MVTecAD和VisA等基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续异常检测中任务依次到达，贪心采样在无内存限制下可简单扩展，但实际部署需要固定内存（核心集大小不随任务数增长）。已有神经方法易遭灾难性遗忘，朴素核心集累积需要无限内存，缺乏兼顾固定内存与代表性的解决方案。

Method: 提出ContCore框架：首先通过贪心扩展在新任务特征上增加候选样本，然后通过贪心合并（consolidation）在保证代表性的前提下将核心集压缩回固定大小预算。该两阶段过程本质上是"持续贪心"——反复对先前贪心采样得到的集合迭代执行贪心选择，并给出理论证明表明所得核心集与oracle核心集的差距有界（graceful degradation而非灾难性退化）。

Result: 在MVTecAD和VisA的11种任务调度上达到SOTA性能，并在在线连续异常检测场景中有效扩展，而先前方法在该场景下性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 持续贪心采样即使每步丢弃数据以满足内存约束，核心集质量仍能优雅退化而非灾难性崩溃，从而在固定内存条件下实现可靠的跨任务异常检测。ContCore兼具固定内存、理论保证与实证优越性，为实用化的连续异常检测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Greedy sampling produces a compact yet representative summary of normal data, which is essential for reliable anomaly detection that relies on measuring distance from normality. For continual anomaly detection where tasks arrive sequentially, extending greedy sampling is straightforward with unbounded memory through coreset accumulation. However, practical deployment requires fixed memory where the coreset size remains constant regardless of task count. We observe that continued greedy sampling, which iteratively applies greedy selection over previously greedy-sampled sets, effectively preserves representativeness under strict memory limits. Despite discarding data at each step to satisfy the memory constraint, coreset quality degrades gracefully rather than catastrophically, enabling reliable anomaly detection across the tasks. We provide theoretical justification by showing that resulting greedy-continued coreset approximates the oracle coreset within a bounded gap. We instantiate this principle in ContCore, which constructs a greedy-continued coreset through greedy expansion on new task features followed by greedy consolidation to enforce the memory budget. Unlike neural methods susceptible to catastrophic forgetting or naive coreset accumulation requiring unbounded memory, ContCore maintains fixed memory with theoretical guarantees. Empirically, ContCore achieves state-of-the-art performance across 11 task schedules on MVTecAD and VisA, and extends effectively to online continual AD settings where prior methods degrade significantly. Code: https://github.com/jungyg/ContCore

</details>


### [24] [Multi-scale Decomposed Convolution Refinement Network for Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16015)
*Mingsheng Zheng,Zirui Jiang,Bo Liu,Yupeng Chen,Jun Zhang,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MDCRNet，一种基于多尺度分解卷积细化网络的方法，用于解决可见光-红外跨模态行人重识别中的跨模态差异和判别能力不足问题。通过层级学习模块和联合判别度量损失，在SYSU-MM01和RegDB数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外行人重识别（VI-ReID）面临跨模态差异和判别能力有限两大挑战，现有方法在语义挖掘、跨模态融合和特征约束方面存在局限，导致识别性能不佳。

Method: 提出MDCRNet（多尺度分解卷积细化网络），包含两个核心组件：1) 层级学习模块（HLM），由四个层级分解卷积注意力（HDCA）模块组成，每个模块配备轻量级通道注意力和多尺度空间感知块以捕获多尺度空间依赖；2) 联合判别度量损失（JDML），引入粒度判别损失（GDL）同时优化类内紧致性和跨模态类间可分性。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01和RegDB两个标准数据集上的大量实验表明，MDCRNet在两个基准上都达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: MDCRNet通过多尺度分解卷积细化网络有效增强了跨模态特征学习和判别度量学习，验证了其在VI-ReID任务上的有效性，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Visible-infrared person re-identification (VI-ReID) suffers from cross-modal discrepancies and limited discriminative capabilities, leading to suboptimal recognition performance. Current approaches exhibit limitations in semantic mining, cross-modal fusion and feature constraints. To tackle these challenges, we propose MDCRNet, a Multi-scale Decomposed Convolution Refinement Network that enhances cross-modal feature learning and discriminative metric learning. Specifically, we introduce a Hierarchical Learning Module (HLM) containing four Hierarchical Decomposed Convolution Attention (HDCA) modules, each equipped with lightweight channel attention and multi-scale spatial perception blocks to capture multi-scale spatial dependencies. Moreover, we develop a Joint Discriminative Metric Loss (JDML) incorporating a novel Granularity Discriminative Loss (GDL) that simultaneously optimizes intra-identity compactness and inter-identity separability across modalities. Extensive experiments on SYSU-MM01 and RegDB datasets demonstrate that MDCRNet achieves state-of-the-art performance on both benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Kevin-zms/MDCRNet.

</details>


### [25] [PoseAdapter: Dual-Stream 2.5D Controllable Image Generation for Complex Multi-Object Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15583)
*Yufeng Chi,Huimin Ma,Fan Gao,Zhice Niu,Keqin Li,Jianmin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PoseAdapter是一个轻量级2.5D可控图像生成框架，通过高效的"个体物体标注+2D边界框+3D角度"条件布局替代昂贵的密集3D映射，并结合Context-Aware Dual-Stream Representation解决属性泄漏与全局一致性的权衡问题，在空间准确性、方向精度和多物体视觉保真度上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然T2I扩散模型取得了显著成功，但在多物体场景中实现精确的空间和方向控制仍是持续挑战。现有方法要么依赖计算昂贵的稠密3D图，要么遭受严重的属性泄漏和"剪切粘贴"伪影问题。

Method: PoseAdapter采用轻量级条件布局（个体物体标题、2D边界框、3D角度）建立精确的空间角度锚点；引入Context-Aware Dual-Stream Representation，通过并行掩码和非掩码路径向现代MM-DiT架构的视觉流中注入局部物体令牌和关系增强场景令牌，以消除属性泄漏同时保持物体间关系和场景级一致性；并构建了OrientLayout高质量数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明PoseAdapter在空间准确度、方向精度和多物体视觉保真度方面均优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: PoseAdapter证明了利用轻量级2.5D条件布局和双流表示可以同时解决多物体可控生成中的属性泄漏与全局一致性问题，为可控图像生成提供了高效且实用的新范式。

Abstract: While Text-to-Image (T2I) diffusion models have achieved remarkable success, precise spatial and orientational control in multi-object scenes remains a persistent challenge. Existing methods either rely on computationally expensive dense 3D maps or suffer from severe attribute leakage and "cut-and-paste" artifacts. To address these limitations, we propose PoseAdapter, a lightweight framework for high-fidelity 2.5D controllable image generation. Instead of dense spatial maps, it establishes precise spatial-angular anchors using an efficient condition layout: individual object captions, 2D bounding boxes, and 3D angles. To resolve the generative trade-off between strict instance isolation and global coherence, we introduce a Context-Aware Dual-Stream Representation. By injecting local object tokens and relation-enriched scene tokens into the visual stream of modern MM-DiT architectures via parallel masked and unmasked pathways, PoseAdapter eliminates attribute leakage while preserving natural inter-object relationships and scene-level coherence. To support this paradigm, we construct OrientLayout, a high-quality dataset featuring standardized 2.5D annotations and instance-level decoupled semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PoseAdapter outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in spatial accuracy, orientational precision, and multi-object visual fidelity. Code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/cyf23/PoseAdapter.

</details>


### [26] [Ultra: Unsupervised Cross-Task Optimization for Reliable Restoration Segmentation Collaboration under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16589)
*Shiqin Wang,Zhiqian Li,Haoyuan Du,Junming Chen,Jiayuan Li,Tianrun Xu,Haoyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ultra框架，通过方向选择与因果效应估计重新构建退化天气下的无监督恢复-分割协同学习，消除跨任务幻觉传播，在三个基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UDA-ASS方法隐含假设恢复与分割能互相提供有益指导，但在严重退化且无目标域监督的情况下，跨任务优化方向的有效性无法被识别，导致由幻觉驱动的错误传播。

Method: 提出Ultra框架，将跨任务交互重构为不确定下的方向选择与因果效应估计。具体包含两个模块：CTDN利用互补视觉结构和语义信息生成候选优化方向并进行协同方向选择；CMIL将跨任务信息传递从基于相关性传播重构为因果效应评估，抑制幻觉传播。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的UDA-ASS基准上取得最先进的语义分割性能；在无监督恢复任务上优于现有UDA-ASS恢复方法，并泛化到无监督恢复与目标检测协同任务。

Conclusion: 通过将跨任务协作从隐式信任的关联传播转变为可识别的方向选择与因果引导，Ultra能够实现可靠且泛化的退化天气下跨任务协同学习。

Abstract: Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Adverse Weather Semantic Segmentation (UDA-ASS) aims to transfer semantic knowledge from labeled normal-weather images to unlabeled adverse environments. Existing approaches implicitly assume that restoration and segmentation provide mutually beneficial guidance. However, under severe degradation and without target-domain supervision, the validity of cross-task optimization directions becomes fundamentally unidentifiable, leading to hallucination-driven error propagation. In this work, we propose a novel Unsupervised Restoration-Segmentation Collaborative Learning Framework (Ultra), which reframes cross-task interaction as direction selection under uncertainty and causal effect estimation, enabling reliable collaboration through candidate direction generation and intervention-based filtering. In detail, we propose CTDN and CMIL. The former exploits complementary visual structures and semantic information to generate candidate optimization directions and performs cooperative direction selection between restoration and segmentation. The latter reformulates cross-task information transfer from correlation-based propagation into causal effect assessment, suppressing hallucination propagation. Extensive experiments on three widely used UDA-ASS benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Beyond segmentation, our framework achieves better unsupervised restoration results than existing UDA-ASS restoration methods and generalizes to unsupervised restoration and object detection collaboration tasks. Code and models will be available at https://github.com/Wang-Shiqin/Ultra.

</details>


### [27] [Xemo-Talker: Unlock Emotions Explicitly for Audio-Driven Talking Portrait Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14700)
*Chaolong Yang,Yinuo Guo,Kai Yao,Yuyao Yan,Jie Sun,Guangliang Cheng,Shibin Wu,Bin Dong,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Xemo-Talker，一种音频驱动的说话人头部生成方法。核心发现是情绪特征分布于整个运动空间，但其判别性监督应集中于非主成分子空间（因为主成分主要编码高能量的发音和姿态变化），这样能在唇形同步和细粒度情绪控制间取得更好平衡。方法先学习中性的语音到运动映射保证稳定发音和唇形同步，再引入由低主成分子空间监督引导的轻量级情绪分支，并结合由类间分离、类内紧凑和低主成分对比学习组成的三重损失来增强情绪控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动说话人头部生成系统中，情绪控制依赖隐式情绪调节机制，导致控制不直接且不充分；同时，如果对整个运动空间施加显式情绪相关损失训练，会面临精确唇形同步与细粒度情绪控制之间的固有权衡难题。

Method: Xemo-Talker包含两部分：1) 先学习中性的语音到运动映射以获得稳定的发音和唇形同步；2) 引入一个轻量级情绪分支，由低主成分子空间监督引导。同时设计Tri-Loss，包含类间分离、类内紧凑和低主成分对比学习三种损失，用于增强情绪控制能力。

Result: 给定音频输入、参考图像和情绪标签，Xemo-Talker在情绪分类准确率上达到最先进水平，同时保持具有竞争力的唇形同步能力和高推理效率，其性能接近在真实视频上测量的结果。

Conclusion: 将判别性监督集中在运动空间的低主成分子空间能够有效解决唇形同步与情绪控制之间的权衡问题，Xemo-Talker验证了该思路的有效性，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Precise emotion control in audio-driven talking heads remains a challenge due to the reliance on implicit emotion regulation in existing systems, which often leads to indirect and insufficient control. Additionally, training with explicit emotion-related losses across the entire motion space poses significant difficulties due to the inherent trade-off between accurate lip synchronization and fine-grained emotion control. In this paper, we reveal a key finding: although emotional cues are distributed throughout the motion space, concentrating discriminative supervision on less-principal components achieves a better emotion-lip synchronization balance, as principal components mainly encode high-energy articulation and pose variations. Building on this insight, we propose Xemo-Talker, which first learns a neutral speech-to-motion mapping for stable articulation and lip synchronization, and then introduces a lightweight emotion branch guided by less-principal subspace supervision. To enhance emotion control, we design a Tri-Loss consisting of inter-class separation, intra-class compactness, and less-principal contrastive learning. Given an audio input, a reference image, and an emotion label, Xemo-Talker achieves state-of-the-art emotion classification accuracy while maintaining competitive lip synchronization and high inference efficiency, with performance approaching that measured on real videos.The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/chaolongy/Xemo-Talker.

</details>


### [28] [Frequency and Edge-Guided Segment Anything Model for Remote Sensing Image Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15054)
*Feng Gao,Zizhe Pan,Haoting Wang,Ruzhuang Hua,Jingchao Cao,Junyu Dong,Qian Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FE-SAM（Frequency and Edge-guided SAM），一种面向遥感图像语义分割的可扩展高效框架。通过引入频域调制适配器（FMA）自适应地分解和调制频域特征，以及边缘增强细化器（EGRefiner）集成多尺度边缘信息，解决了SAM在遥感分割中特征适配不足和边界语义模糊两大问题。在三个基准数据集上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM虽在遥感分割中表现出了强大的分割能力和泛化性，但存在两个关键局限：(1) SAM的特征无法充分适应多样化土地利用类型的差异特征；(2) 地物边界存在语义模糊问题，难以精确勾画。这两大问题阻碍了SAM在细粒度遥感语义分割中的性能上限。

Method: 提出FE-SAM框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Frequency-Modulated Adapter (FMA)——基于输入数据自适应地分解和调制频域特征，选择性地增强对应于不同土地利用类型的信息丰富的低频和高频分量；(2) EGRefiner——集成从输入图像提取的多尺度边缘增强信息，提升SAM对细粒度细节的捕捉能力。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，FE-SAM在遥感图像语义分割任务中优于现有最先进方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: FE-SAM通过频域调制适配和边缘增强细化两条技术路径，有效解决了SAM在遥感图像语义分割中的特征适配不足与边界模糊问题，验证了频域与边缘信息融合对提升基础模型在遥感领域的适配性的有效性。

Abstract: Remote sensing image semantic segmentation (RSISS) has attracted significant attention due to the growing demand for fine-grained land cover information. The Segment Anything Model (SAM), proposed as a foundation vision model, offers strong segmentation performance and generalization capabilities for RSISS tasks. However, existing SAM-based approaches face two limitations: (1) Insufficient adaptation of SAM's features to the diverse characteristics of land cover types. (2) Semantic ambiguity at object boundaries, which hinders accurate delineation. To address these limitations, we propose Frequency and Edge-guided SAM (FE-SAM), a scalable and efficient framework for RSISS. Specifically, we introduce a Frequency-Modulated Adapter (FMA) that adaptively decomposes and modulates frequency-domain features based on the input data. It selectively enhances informative high- and low-frequency components corresponding to different land cover types. Furthermore, to improve SAM's ability to capture fine-grained details, we design EGRefiner, which integrates multi-scale edge-enhanced information extracted from the input image. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that FE-SAM outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The source codes are available at: https://github.com/oucailab/FE-SAM.

</details>


### [29] [SOS! : A Streamlined Object-Conditional Transformer for Model-free Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15295)
*Jiaqi Hu,Junwen Huang,Hongli Xu,Peter KT Yu,Nassir Navab,Benjamin Busam,Slobodan Ilic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SOS（Streamlined Object-conditional Transformer），一种无模型的目标物体分割框架，仅需单张参考图像即可对未见物体进行精确分割，废除对3D模型先验的依赖，将掩码生成与目标识别统一为单次前向传播，在多个基准上达到最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础分割模型虽能生成高质量、类别无关的掩码，但无法将这些候选掩码与特定目标物体关联起来，这一语义鸿沟严重阻碍了其在机器人操作等下游任务中的应用。现有方法过度依赖详尽的3D物体模型先验，带来了巨大的计算开销和复杂多阶段流程。

Method: 提出SOS框架，完全消除对3D模型的依赖，每个目标物体仅需一张参考图像。核心是新颖的目标条件Transformer（Object-Conditional Transformer），通过学习身份锚定查询（identity-anchored queries），将掩码生成与目标识别统一为单一的前馈传播过程，在结构和计算效率上均有大幅提升。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量评估表明，SOS在无模型未见物体分割任务上建立了新的最先进水平，兼具有高精度和高效率的性能表现。

Conclusion: SOS通过流线型的目标条件Transformer设计，成功解决了基础分割模型的语义关联难题，为机器人操作等下游应用提供了一种轻量、准确且高效的无模型分割方案。

Abstract: Foundation segmentation models excel at generating high-quality, class-agnostic masks, but they struggle to associate these proposals with specific target objects. This semantic gap severely hinders their deployment in downstream applications like robotic manipulation, which demand precise unseen objects segmentation. Existing approaches attempt to resolve this by relying on exhaustive 3D object model priors, inherently introducing prohibitive computational overhead and complex, multi-stage pipelines. To address these limitations, we propose SOS (Streamlined Object-conditional Transformer for model-free Segmentation). SOS completely eliminates the reliance on 3D models, requiring only a single reference image per target object. Central to our framework is a novel Object-Conditional Transformer that learns identity-anchored queries, unifying mask generation and target identification into a single feed-forward pass. This streamlined design drastically improves both structural and computational efficiency. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SOS establishes a new state-of-the-art for model-free unseen objects segmentation, delivering accurate and high-efficiency performance. The project page and code are available at https://sos-seg.github.io/.

</details>


### [30] [Path2ST: Hierarchical Cell-Tissue Grounded Cross-Modal Translation for Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14710)
*Ruochen Liu,Wei Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Path2ST 是一个层次化自回归框架，利用H&E染色图像的生物学层次结构（细胞类型→组织微环境→基因表达程序）进行跨模态语义翻译，通过层次化细胞-组织条件机制、尺度自适应自回归生成和全频谱损失函数，在三个数据集上达到SOTA性能，实现准确且空间连贯的转录组图谱预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有H&E到空间转录组的预测方法将H&E图像视为通用视觉输入，忽略了其内在的生物学层次结构——空间组织的细胞类型共同形成功能性组织微环境，从而决定局部的基因表达程序。这导致现有方法无法充分利用生物学先验知识，预测精度和空间连贯性受限。

Method: Path2ST包含三个核心组件：(i) 层次化细胞-组织条件机制，融合显式和隐式细胞特征与组织级语义表征构建层次化条件信号；(ii) 尺度自适应自回归生成过程，在层次化语义词汇表上进行由粗到细、生物学一致的表达合成；(iii) SpectraLoss全频谱目标，同时强制执行序数保真度、建模转录爆发并让语义结构对齐细胞类型。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验表明Path2ST取得了最先进的性能，能够生成高度准确且空间连贯的转录组图谱。

Conclusion: Path2ST通过将H&E到ST预测重新定义为跨模态语义翻译任务，并利用生物学层次结构，有效弥合了H&E图像与空间转录组之间的语义鸿沟，验证了层次化条件生成范式在空间基因表达预测中的优越性。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from hematoxylin and eosin (H\&E)-stained images offers a cost-effective alternative to spatial transcriptomics (ST). However, existing methods treat H\&E images as generic visual inputs and ignore their intrinsic biological hierarchy, where spatially organized cell types collectively form functional tissue microenvironments that govern local gene expression programs. To bridge this gap, we formulate H\&E-to-ST prediction as a cross-modal semantic translation task and propose Path2ST, a hierarchically grounded autoregressive framework featuring three key components: (i) a Hierarchical Cell-Tissue Conditioning mechanism that fuses explicit and implicit cellular features with tissue-level semantic representations to construct hierarchical conditioning signals; (ii) a Scale-Adaptive Autoregressive Generation process over a hierarchical semantic vocabulary, enabling coarse-to-fine, biologically consistent expression synthesis; and (iii) SpectraLoss, a full-spectrum objective that jointly enforces ordinal fidelity, models transcriptional bursts, and aligns semantic structures with cell types. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, validating that Path2ST generates highly accurate and spatially coherent transcriptomic profiles. The related code is released at https://github.com/RuochenLiu23/Path2ST.

</details>


### [31] [Emergent 3D Instance Segmentation from Self-Supervised Point Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15796)
*Ted Lentsch,Santiago Montiel-Marín,Holger Caesar,Julian F. P. Kooij*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TokenGraph3D，一个无需训练的3D实例分割方法，证明冻结的自监督点云Transformer内部表示（特别是注意力查询和键）已包含足够的实例结构信息，无需任何手工几何先验即可实现实例分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无监督3D实例分割依赖于手工设计的几何先验（如密度聚类、运动线索或2D检测投影）。作者质疑这些先验的必要性，探究冻结的自监督点云Transformer是否内部已蕴含足够的实例结构信息，以实现无需任何手工几何先验的实例分割。

Method: 作者使用冻结的自监督点云Transformer作为纯特征提取器，在SemanticKITTI、nuScenes和Waymo数据集上探测其内部表示。基于四个核心洞察——实例信号集中在注意力查询和键中、输出特征发生语义坍缩、实例信号在深度上呈双峰分布（最浅和最深编码器阶段最强）、该信号主要由旋转位置编码（RoPE）驱动——设计了TokenGraph3D：一个训练自由的实例分割器，通过键相似性图上的连通分量进行点分组，不依赖密度聚类或邻近性先验。

Result: 在完全相同的无先验条件下，TokenGraph3D显著优于基于输出特征的基线方法，使从自监督Transformer中涌现的3D实例结构变得可见，并在三个大规模数据集上取得了更强的实例分割性能。

Conclusion: 自监督点云Transformer中的查询和键表示隐式编码了实例级结构信息，且该信息主要源于旋转位置编码。这表明无需外部几何先验，仅利用Transformer内部表征即可进行有效的无监督3D实例分割，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Unsupervised 3D instance segmentation of outdoor LiDAR scans has traditionally relied on handcrafted geometric priors such as density-based clustering, motion cues, or projected 2D detections. In this work, we investigate whether a frozen, self-supervised point transformer already contains the structural information required to isolate object instances without any handcrafted geometric prior. Using this transformer purely as a feature extractor, we probe its internal representations across the SemanticKITTI, nuScenes, and Waymo Perception datasets. Our analysis yields four core insights: (1) the instance signal concentrates in the attention queries and keys rather than in the values or final output features; (2) output features semantically collapse, merging adjacent same-class objects that the queries and keys keep distinct; (3) this instance signal is bimodal in depth, strongest at the shallowest and deepest encoder stages; and (4) this signal is driven predominantly by the rotary position encoding (RoPE), whose removal collapses its advantage. We put these findings into our method TokenGraph3D, a training-free segmenter that groups points via connected components on a key-similarity graph, using neither density-based clustering nor proximity priors. Under identical prior-free conditions, we substantially outperform output-feature baselines, making the emergent 3D instance structure visible.

</details>


### [32] [Zero-MELO: Test-Time Evidence Calibration with Multimodal LLMs for Zero-Shot Micro-Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14854)
*Chengyan Wang,Hanliang Xie,Yueyi Yang,Haoyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-MELO，一个针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在微手势识别（MGR）任务上的测试时证据校准框架。通过树搜索机制获取局部细粒度视觉证据，结合测试时校准模块缓解分数偏差，以及多线索融合模块整合多源证据，在iMiGUE和MA-52数据集上显著超越Qwen2.5-VL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在通用视频理解上表现优异，但在微手势识别这类细粒度、运动中心的判别性任务上性能不佳。主要原因在于MLLM固有的单次前向推理限制了其运动敏感表征能力，导致两个瓶颈：局部证据不足以及由语言和运动无关外观导致的严重分数偏差。

Method: 提出测试时证据校准框架，包含三个模块：1）树搜索机制，逐步获取局部化细粒度视觉证据；2）测试时校准模块，缓解语言和外观带来的分数偏差；3）多线索融合模块，整合多个线索而非依赖单一线索进行最终预测。诊断过程基于负对数似然（NLL）空间分析零样本MGR错误。

Result: 在iMiGUE数据集上取得26.84%的平均类别准确率，在MA-52上取得22.10%，显著优于Qwen2.5-VL基线的16.15%和10.20%。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的测试时引导可以有效增强MLLM在细粒度运动感知任务上的能力，证明了运动敏感表征的限制源于单次前向推理而非模型参数本身，测试时证据校正是弥补这一差距的有效途径。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in general video understanding, their capability in fine-grained and motion-centric tasks remains limited. This limitation is particularly critical in micro-gesture recognition (MGR), where micro-gestures (MGs) - subtle, short-duration, and spatially localized human movements - serve as key discriminative signals for implicit affective analysis, yet are easily neglected following common prompting practices. Although MGR has been intensively studied by many discriminative approaches, the use of MLLMs for MGR is underexplored, with notably poor performance. We hypothesize that the motion-sensitive representation ability of MLLMs is constrained by their inherent single-pass forward inference, which can be substantially enhanced through carefully designed test-time guidance. Motivated by this, building on our prior findings regarding temporal insensitivity in Video LLMs, we diagnose zero-shot MGR errors in the Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) space. We observe that MLLMs suffer from two bottlenecks: 1) insufficient localized evidence and 2) severe score biases driven by language and motion-agnostic appearances. Thus, we propose a novel test-time evidence calibration framework that improves both reasoning details and prediction reliability. Specifically, we introduce a tree search mechanism to progressively acquire localized, fine-grained visual evidence, coupled with a test-time calibration module to mitigate score biases. The multi-cue fusion module then integrates evidence from multiple cues without relying on a single cue for final prediction. Our framework achieves mean-class accuracies of 26.84\% on iMiGUE and 22.10\% on MA-52, significantly outperforming the Qwen2.5-VL baseline, which produces 16.15\% and 10.20\%, respectively. The code will be available at https://zero-melo.github.io/Zero-MELO.

</details>


### [33] [Beyond Similarity Matching: Structured Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary Referring Segmentation in 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16103)
*Yizhao Wang,Xinfa Wang,Jingbo Wang,Jingbo Wang,Guantao Zhang,Yafeng Han,Guohong Gao,Yuhe Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QAGaussian是一个面向3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)开放词汇指称分割的查询自适应神经推理框架。它通过查询条件化的多尺度高斯槽、关系感知槽图推理和粒度自适应路由，在仅用Mosaic3D-5.6M预训练、无目标数据集微调的情况下，在多个独立基准上取得了47.2 Avg. mIoU和63.2 Avg. F1的性能，超越最强基线2.7/2.9个点，并显著改善了目标-参照混淆、部分-整体泄漏和关系违规等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3DGS指称分割方法通常依赖全局文本-区域相似度匹配，这在涉及属性、参照目标、空间关系和细粒度部件的查询中表现薄弱，导致目标-参照混淆、粒度不匹配、部分-整体泄漏和关系违规等问题。作者旨在提出一种能够根据查询自适应调整推理过程的神经框架，以解决这些挑战。

Method: QAGaussian包含三个核心组件：(1) 查询条件化多尺度高斯槽学习——将高斯原语组织为差分候选，其感受野由输入表达式塑造；(2) 关系感知槽图——通过语言条件化边权重传播目标-参照、属性、部分-整体及上下文证据；(3) 粒度自适应路由——软性组合区域级、对象级、部件级、属性感知和关系感知的掩码分支，随后进行关系约束的细化以保证空间、部分-整体、属性和几何一致性。预训练仅在Mosaic3D-5.6M上进行。

Result: 在无需目标数据集微调的情况下，QAGaussian在独立基准上取得了47.2 Avg. mIoU和63.2 Avg. F1，比最强的3DGS指称基线分别高出2.7 mIoU和2.9 F1。Part-mIoU从38.6提升到43.4，Rel-mIoU从44.4提升到50.8，目标-参照混淆率从10.8降低到7.4。

Conclusion: 查询条件化槽学习、关系感知图推理和自适应路由为3DGS中的开放词汇指称分割提供了一种有效的神经建模策略，显著优于依赖全局文本-区域相似度的现有方法，且具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary referring segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) requires a neural model to select Gaussian primitives according to free-form language expressions. Existing 3DGS-based methods usually rely on global text-region similarity, which is weak for queries involving attributes, reference objects, spatial relations, and fine-grained parts. This often causes target-reference confusion, granularity mismatch, part-whole leakage, and relation violations. We propose QAGaussian, a query-adaptive neural reasoning framework for language-guided Gaussian primitive selection. QAGaussian first learns query-conditioned multi-scale Gaussian slots as differentiable candidates whose receptive fields are shaped by the input expression. It then builds a relation-aware slot graph with language-conditioned edge weighting to propagate target-reference, attribute, part-whole, and contextual evidence. A granularity-adaptive router softly combines region-level, object-level, part-level, attribute-aware, and relation-aware mask branches, followed by relation-constrained refinement for spatial, part-whole, attribute, and geometric consistency. QAGaussian is pretrained only on Mosaic3D-5.6M for Gaussian-text alignment and evaluated on independent benchmarks without target-dataset fine-tuning. It achieves 47.2 Avg. mIoU and 63.2 Avg. F1, outperforming the strongest 3DGS referring baseline by 2.7 mIoU points and 2.9 F1 points. It also improves Part-mIoU from 38.6 to 43.4, Rel-mIoU from 44.4 to 50.8, and reduces target-reference confusion from 10.8 to 7.4. These results demonstrate that query-conditioned slot learning, relation-aware graph reasoning, and adaptive routing provide an effective neural modeling strategy for open-vocabulary referring segmentation in 3DGS. The code is available at https://github.com/zqeslwyz/QAGaussian.

</details>


### [34] [TEA: Text Encoder Alignment for Robust Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15341)
*Alireza Dehghanpour Farashah,Zhuan Shi,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TEA框架，将概念擦除形式化为文本表示空间中的域对齐问题，仅微调文本编码器而保持生成骨干网络冻结，在零推理开销下实现对黑盒和白盒对抗攻击的最先进擦除鲁棒性，并扩展到SD v3.5等Rectified Flow Transformer架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型容易被对抗性或改写式提示绕过安全机制生成有害内容。现有概念擦除方法存在对对抗提示鲁棒性有限、损害良性生成质量或依赖推理时干预带来持续计算开销等问题。

Method: 将概念擦除形式化为文本表示空间中的域对齐问题，提出轻量级文本编码器对齐框架TEA。仅微调文本编码器而保持生成骨干网络完全冻结。给定概念-锚定提示对，训练判别器区分含概念提示与安全锚定提示的token级表示，同时更新文本编码器使这些表示无法区分。

Result: TEA零推理开销，仅需少量微调步骤即可大规模部署。在Stable Diffusion v1.4上对黑盒和白盒对抗攻击达到最先进的擦除鲁棒性，同时保持良性提示生成质量。在Stable Diffusion v3.5上取得最低攻击成功率，将概念擦除扩展到T5条件化的Rectified Flow Transformer架构。

Conclusion: TEA通过仅微调文本编码器实现高效、模型无关的概念擦除，兼顾鲁棒性、生成质量与部署效率，填补了此前在SD v3.5等新架构上概念擦除研究的空白。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can be misused to generate harmful content through adversarial or paraphrased prompts that bypass built-in safety mechanisms. Existing concept erasure methods often suffer from limited robustness against adversarial prompts, degradation of benign generation quality, or reliance on inference-time interventions that introduce persistent computational overhead. To address these limitations, we formulate concept erasure as a domain alignment problem in the text representation space. We propose a lightweight Text Encoder Alignment framework (TEA) that fine-tunes only the text encoder while keeping the generative backbone fully frozen. Given concept--anchor prompt pairs, our method trains a discriminator to distinguish token-level representations of concept-containing prompts from those of safe anchor prompts, while updating the text encoder to make these representations indistinguishable. TEA introduces zero inference-time overhead and requires only a small number of fine-tuning steps, making it highly efficient to deploy at scale. Despite this efficiency, TEA achieves state-of-the-art erasure robustness against black-box and white-box adversarial attacks on Stable Diffusion v1.4, while preserving generation quality on benign prompts. Furthermore, TEA is model-agnostic and achieves the lowest attack success rate on Stable Diffusion v3.5, extending concept erasure to a Rectified Flow Transformer architecture with T5 conditioning where prior methods remain largely unexplored. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/alirezafarashah/TEA.git}{https://github.com/alirezafarashah/TEA.git}

</details>


### [35] [MOSS-VL Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15045)
*Pengyu Wang,Chenkun Tan,Shaojun Zhou,Qirui Zhou,Yanxin Chen,Xingyang He,Huazheng Zeng,Jijun Cheng,Chenghao Wang,Xiaomeng Qian,Pengfei Wang,Zhan Huang,Shanqing Gao,Wei Huang,Longjun Cao,Wu Ran,Jie Liu,Changtai Zhu,Hongkai Wang,Yixian Tian,Chenghao Liu,Zhen Ye,Xinghao Wang,Botian Jiang,Guoguo Feng,Zhaoye Fei,Ruixiao Li,Mingshu Chen,Yang Gao,Qinyuan Cheng,Shimin Li,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOSS-VL是一个开源视觉语言模型家族，将实时交互（即说即看）作为一等能力，通过门控交叉注意力架构、合成交互语料监督和分段式课程训练实现。离线版本在时序推理基准上具有竞争力，在线版本在四个流式基准中的三个上取得最好成绩，尤其在与预动性行为相关的子集上大幅领先。模型仅11.3B参数但视觉token不进入解码序列，时间到首词延迟优势随视觉上下文增长从2.8x扩大至5.1x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
