Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型大多在离线场景下工作，即先看完整输入再生成回复，无法在生成过程中实时感知新到达的视觉帧。作者希望将实时交互（感知与说话同时进行）作为模型的核心能力，而非事后加工，并通过端到端的系统级设计（架构、数据、训练课程）来实现这一目标。

Method: 三方面协同设计：(1) 架构上，语言解码器仅通过门控交叉注意力（gated cross-attention）关注视觉信息，使得模型在生成文本的同时能自然地接收新帧；(2) 数据上，构建合成交互语料库监督模型何时说话、何时保持沉默、何时修正回答；(3) 训练上，采用分段式课程（staged curriculum），将实时特定训练集中在一个轻量的最终阶段，建立在一个强大的离线基础之上。

Result: MOSS-VL-Instruct离线版在相似规模下具有竞争力，并在时序推理视频数据集上领先。在线版MOSS-VL-Realtime在四个流式基准中的三个上取得最优平均成绩，且横扫三个直接测试主动行为（proactive behavior）的子集——OmniMMI主动告警子集上以66.0大幅超越最佳基线的37.5。在11.3B参数下，由于视觉token不在解码序列中，其时间到首词（TTFT）优势相比同骨干的Qwen3-VL-8B从2.8x扩大到5.1x（随视觉上下文增长）。

Conclusion: MOSS-VL证明通过全栈协同设计——门控交叉注意力架构、交互语料监督和分段课程训练——视觉语言模型可以在保持离线强基础的同时获得出色的实时交互能力。所有五个checkpoint、训练课程和实时推理代码均已开源。

Abstract: We present MOSS-VL, an open vision-language model family that treats real-time interaction -- perceiving while it speaks -- as a first-class capability. It is co-designed across the stack: the language decoder attends to vision only through gated cross-attention, so the model can naturally see incoming frames while generating; a synthesized interaction corpus supervises when to speak, when to stay silent, and when to revise; and a staged curriculum concentrates all real-time-specific training in one light final stage over a strong offline foundation. Offline, MOSS-VL-Instruct is competitive at comparable scale and leads temporal-reasoning video sets. Across four streaming benchmarks, MOSS-VL-Realtime posts the best average on three (second on the fourth) among open-source streaming models, sweeping the three subsets that squarely test proactive behavior -- 66.0 vs. 37.5 for the best baseline on OmniMMI Proactive Alerting. With 11.3B parameters but visual tokens outside the decoded sequence, MOSS-VL widens its time-to-first-token advantage over same-backbone Qwen3-VL-8B from 2.8x to 5.1x as visual context grows. We release all five checkpoints, the training curriculum, and the real-time inference code at https://github.com/OpenMOSS/MOSS-VL.

</details>


### [36] [VGGT-Align: Bridging Local Reconstruction and Global Consistency for Long-Sequence 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15260)
*Wei Zhang,Yihang Wu,Songhua Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Scene Geometric Invariant Anchoring (SGIA)框架，利用场景几何不变量在跨时间片段的一致性来约束Sim(3)对齐中的尺度自由度，将7-DoF对齐退化为6-DoF刚体变换，切断链式尺度误差传播；同时引入轻量级测试时自适应策略微调归一化层参数。实验表明在长序列3D重建基准上将绝对轨迹误差降低最多32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列3D重建中，分块推理管线的顺序Sim(3)对齐中尺度自由度未被约束，导致估计误差复合累积（乘法式放大），严重扭曲全局轨迹和点云几何，这是最关键的失效模式——尺度漂移。

Method: 1) SGIA核心：利用结构化环境（如驾驶场景）中环境规则性派生的几何量在时间上不变，通过粗到精鲁棒估计从每个块的预测点云中提取主导几何不变量，利用其跨块一致性建立独立于点云配准的尺度约束，将7-DoF Sim(3)对齐退化为6-DoF刚体变换。2) 轻量级测试时自适应：仅微调归一化层参数，通过多目标自监督逐序列改进块内预测。两模块均为即插即用，无需离线重训。

Result: 在多个长序列基准上达到最先进性能，绝对轨迹误差降低最多32%，在轨迹稳定性和重建质量上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过锚定场景几何不变量来显式消除尺度自由度，从源头切断链式尺度误差传播，结合轻量级测试时自适应，提供了一种无需离线重训的即插即用尺度一致性增强方案。

Abstract: Maintaining global geometric consistency is a central challenge in long-sequence 3D reconstruction, with scale drift being the most critical failure mode. In chunk-based inference pipelines, the scale degree of freedom in sequential Sim(3) alignment is left unconstrained, causing estimation errors to compound multiplicatively and distort global trajectories and point cloud geometry. We present a scale-consistency enhancement framework built on a key insight: in structured environments such as driving scenes, geometric quantities arising from environmental regularity remain inherently invariant across temporal segments, and discrepancies in their per-chunk measurements directly expose inter-chunk scale drift. We propose Scene Geometric Invariant Anchoring (SGIA), which extracts dominant geometric invariants from each chunk's predicted point cloud via coarse-to-fine robust estimation and exploits their cross-chunk consistency to establish scale constraints independent of point cloud registration, explicitly degenerating 7-DoF Sim(3) alignment into 6-DoF rigid-body transformation and severing chain-wise scale error propagation at its source. We further introduce a lightweight test-time adaptation strategy that fine-tunes only normalization-layer parameters via multi-objective self-supervision, progressively improving intra-chunk predictions along the sequence. Both modules are plug-and-play and require no offline retraining. Experiments on multiple long-sequence benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing absolute trajectory error by up to 32% with significant gains in trajectory stability and reconstruction quality. Code: https://github.com/WZ-CS/VGGT-Align

</details>


### [37] [KeyID: Decoupled Drafting and Keyframe Editing for Identity-Preserving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16154)
*Jianjie Luo,Yiming Zhong,Haoming Shen,Yupeng Xiao,Zhenguo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KeyID是一个免训练的IPVG框架，通过将视频动态合成与身份注入解耦，实现了身份保持的视频生成。它包含参考感知视频生成和身份保持关键帧编辑两个组件，将稠密帧级监督改为稀疏关键帧级精炼，解决了提示遵循与身份保真之间的容量冲突，无需额外训练即可扩展到多主题和复杂顺序动作生成，在ACM Multimedia 2026挑战赛中获亚军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IPVG方法受限于高调优成本或有限的输入级增强，难以在复杂长序列动作中保持刚性身份一致性，需要一种训练免费、能同时兼顾文本提示遵循与身份保真的解决方案。

Method: KeyID将视频生成解耦为两个模块：(1) Reference-Aware Video Generation，生成与多参考对齐的身份无关视频草稿；(2) Identity-Preserved Keyframe Editing，通过稀疏关键帧修正和后续运动插值整合目标身份。通过从稠密帧级监督转向稀疏关键帧级精炼来解决容量冲突。

Result: KeyID在官方挑战基准上通过了自动化和人工评估，性能优于先前方法，并在ACM Multimedia 2026 IPVG大挑战赛Track 2（顺序动作）中荣获亚军。

Conclusion: 模块化设计使KeyID无需额外训练即可无缝扩展到多主题参考和复杂顺序动作生成，验证了将身份注入从动态合成中解耦的有效性，为IPVG提供了一种低成本高效率的范式。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation (IPVG) requires synthesizing videos that are faithful to both reference subjects and text prompts. Existing methods are often hindered by high tuning costs or limited input-level enhancements, struggling to maintain rigid identity consistency during complex, long-sequence actions. To address these limitations, we propose KeyID, a training-free IPVG framework that decouples the synthesis of video dynamics from the injection of identity. Specifically, KeyID comprises two components: (1) Reference-Aware Video Generation, which produces an identity-agnostic video draft aligned with multiple references, and (2) Identity-Preserved Keyframe Editing, which integrates the target identity via sparse keyframe correction and subsequent motion interpolation. By shifting from dense frame-level supervision to sparse keyframe-level refinement, KeyID effectively resolves the capacity conflict between prompt adherence and identity fidelity. Crucially, our modular design allows seamless extension to multi-subject references and complex sequential action generation without additional training. KeyID outperforms prior works and is validated by automatic and human evaluations on the official challenge benchmark, ultimately securing the runner-up position in the Track 2 (Sequential Action) of the ACM Multimedia 2026 IPVG Grand Challenge. Source code is available at https://github.com/WISLab-GDUT/KeyID.

</details>


### [38] [PixRestore: Unified Image Restoration via Pixel Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16793)
*Lingchen Sun,Rongyuan Wu,Xiangtao Kong,Jixin Zhao,Qiaosi Yi,Yujing Sun,Shuaizheng Liu,Zhengqiang Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixRestore是一个无需VAE的像素空间扩散Transformer（DiT）统一图像修复模型，完全从零训练，不依赖T2I预训练。它直接在patchified像素上进行flow matching，通过LQ-HQ DINO特征相似度预测层特征可靠性并自适应融合/监督，最终微调为单步生成器。仅约50M参数和单步推理即在保真度、感知质量和鲁棒性上超越现有UIR模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一图像修复（UIR）方法多基于大型预训练文生图（T2I）潜空间扩散模型，但存在两个关键问题：一是潜空间中的VAE可能丢失对修复至关重要的细节信息；二是T2I模型的开放式合成先验可能引入与内容不一致的伪影（内容不一致性）。因此本文旨在设计一种不依赖T2I预训练、直接作用于像素空间的方法，以兼顾细节保留与内容一致性。

Method: PixRestore采用无VAE的像素空间Diffusion Transformer（DiT）架构，扩散骨干完全从头训练。具体包括：1）直接在patchified像素上进行flow matching，保留细粒度细节同时控制token序列规模；2）利用LQ-HQ DINO特征相似度预测各层特征可靠性，将高可靠层特征作为稠密条件融合，对低可靠层施加更强的HQ特征监督以促进退化去除；3）在大规模多样化场景与退化语料上训练；4）使用DINO-based对抗目标微调为单步生成器以实现高效推理。

Result: 在公开基准和真实世界测试集上，PixRestore仅以约50M参数和单步推理，即在总体保真度、感知质量和退化鲁棒性上取得最佳效果，同时效率远高于竞品。更大的PixRestore变体可进一步提升性能，验证了像素空间设计的可扩展性。

Conclusion: PixRestore证明了不依赖T2I预训练、直接以像素空间DiT进行flow matching的方案在统一图像修复上是可行且高效的，能够同时兼顾细节保真、内容一致性与计算效率。其可扩展性表明像素空间设计具备进一步增长的潜力。

Abstract: Unified image restoration (UIR) aims to recover high-quality (HQ) content from low-quality (LQ) images with different degradations using a single model. Most recent methods adapt large pretrained text-to-image (T2I) latent diffusion models for their strong capacity and generative priors. However, the variational autoencoder (VAE) in latent T2I models may discard restoration-sensitive details, while the open-ended synthesis prior can introduce content-inconsistent artifacts. We present PixRestore, a VAE-free pixel-space Diffusion Transformer (DiT) for UIR, where the diffusion backbone is trained entirely from scratch, without relying on T2I pretraining. PixRestore performs flow matching directly on patchified pixels, preserving fine-grained details while keeping the token sequence tractable. To adapt to different degradations, PixRestore learns to predict the reliability of layer features using LQ--HQ DINO feature similarity. Features from more reliable layers are fused as dense conditioning, while less reliable layers receive stronger HQ-feature supervision to encourage degradation removal. We train PixRestore on a large-scale corpus of diverse scenes and degradations, and further finetune it into a one-step generator using DINO-based adversarial objectives for efficient inference. Experiments on public benchmarks and real-world test sets show that, with only about 50M parameters and single-step inference, PixRestore achieves the best overall fidelity, perceptual quality, and robustness to degradations among competing UIR models while being far more efficient. Larger PixRestore variants can further boost performance, demonstrating the scalability of our pixel-space design. Code and the curated benchmark can be found at https://github.com/csslc/PixRestore.

</details>

*Tomasz Stanczyk,Seongro Yoon,Francois Bremond*

TL;DR: McByte++是一个无需训练的跟踪-by-检测框架，用于体育场景下的长时多目标跟踪。它通过轻量级掩码传播、条件相机运动补偿和在线重识别集成在统一流程中，相比原版McByte在SoccerNet-tracking和SportsMOT基准上实现了高达+3.0 HOTA和+6.1 IDF1的提升，同时速度提升可达一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育场景中的长时多目标跟踪因频繁遮挡、快速相机运动和运动员反复重现而极具挑战性，现有方法在这些条件下难以兼顾跟踪精度与计算效率。

Method: McByte++采用训练-free的跟踪-by-检测框架，集成三大核心组件：轻量级掩码传播（替代重分割组件）、条件相机运动补偿（优化运动建模）、在线重识别。整个系统在统一流程中运行，无需检测器重训练或数据集特调。

Result: 在SoccerNet-tracking和SportsMOT基准上，McByte++相比原版McByte在在线设置下实现最多+3.0 HOTA和+6.1 IDF1的提升；结合离线全局关联可进一步获得增益；速度提升最高可达一个数量级。

Conclusion: McByte++通过轻量化组件替换和运动建模优化，在显著提升运行效率的同时增强了身份保持能力，证明了无需训练即可在体育多目标跟踪中达到先进水平的可行性。

Abstract: Long-term multi-object tracking in sports remains challenging due to frequent occlusions, rapid camera motion, and repeated player reappearances. We introduce McByte++, a training-free tracking-by-detection framework that integrates lightweight mask propagation, conditional camera motion compensation, and online re-identification within a unified pipeline. Compared to its predecessor, McByte++ substantially improves runtime efficiency while enhancing identity preservation. On SoccerNet-tracking and SportsMOT benchmarks, McByte++ achieves up to +3.0 HOTA and +6.1 IDF1 improvements over the original McByte in the online setting, with further gains when combined with offline global association. Replacing heavy segmentation components and optimizing motion modeling yields up to an order-of-magnitude speed increase. All results are obtained without detector retraining or dataset-specific tuning. Code will be made available at https://github.com/tstanczyk95/McBytePlusPlus.

</details>


### [40] [MegaParts: Scaling Part-Aware 3D Object Generation to 300 Parts via Token-Efficient Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14783)
*Manwen Liao,Xinyu Lian,Jian Mao,Kaixu Chen,Li Luo,Jinghao Yan,Wanshui Gan,Qiao Yu,Weitian Zhang,Chunhua Shen,Guang Chen,Bo Dai,Xudong Xu,Zhaoyang Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MegaParts 提出一种可扩展的自回归 3D 生成框架，通过结合结构化序列建模和 token 高效的向量量化形状分词器，支持多达 300 个部件和 256k token 序列长度的复杂物体生成，在网格质量上优于基线的自回归和扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的部件感知 3D 生成方法在部件数量增加时，因 token 长度和内存开销过大而难以扩展到高复杂度的物体，难以在保持组成结构的同时实现细粒度部件级控制。

Method: MegaParts 由两部分组成：1) 一个 token 高效的向量量化形状分词器，通过最小化 token 使用量同时保证高保真重建，实现基于几何复杂度的自适应长度 token 化；2) 基于该紧凑表示，训练一个大语言模型在统一结构化序列中生成物体包围盒、部件包围盒和部件形状 token，并配合高效的长上下文训练策略。

Result: MegaParts 可扩展到最多 300 个部件和 256k token 的序列长度，显著扩展了部件感知 3D 生成的规模；在网格质量上优于基线自回归模型和扩散模型，证明压缩的离散部件 token 不仅提升可扩展性，还改善生成几何的保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于 LLM 原生 token 高效的自回归建模是大规模部件感知 3D 生成中扩散模型的一个有力替代方案，为可控建模、编辑和关节化等图形应用提供了更可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D object generation is essential for graphics applications such as controllable modeling, editing, and articulation, where objects are represented as coherent assemblies of semantic parts. However, existing part-aware generation methods, do not scale well to highly complex objects. As the number of parts increases, generating detailed geometry becomes prohibitively expensive in token length and memory. We introduce MegaParts, a scalable autoregressive 3D generation framework to address this challenge by combining structured sequence modeling with a token-efficient vector-quantized shape tokenizer. Our tokenizer learns discrete latent representations for part-level geometry by minimizing token usage subject to high-fidelity reconstruction, enabling adaptive-length tokenization based on geometric complexity. On top of this compact representation, we train a large language model to generate object bounding boxes, part bounding boxes, and part shape tokens within a unified structured sequence. Combined with efficient long-context training strategy, our token-efficient formulation scales to objects with up to 300 parts and sequence lengths up to 256k tokens. This substantially extends the scale of part-aware 3D generation while preserving compositional structure and enabling fine-grained part-level control. Our method achieves higher mesh quality than baseline autoregressive and diffusion models, showing that compressed discrete part tokens improve not only scalability but also the achievable fidelity of generated geometry. These results suggest that LLM native token-efficient autoregressive modeling is a compelling alternative to diffusion for large-scale part-aware 3D generation. The project page is available at https://expmaster.github.io/megaparts_webpage.

</details>


### [41] [CBX-Bench: A Human-Aligned MLLM Council for Benchmarking Concept Bottleneck Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15404)
*Yusuf Meric Karadag,Gulay Oklan,Seref Baris Cagliyan,Umut Ozdemir,Emre Akbas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CBX-Bench，一个基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）评审团的概念瓶颈模型（CBM）可解释性量化评估基准。通过人类标注研究建立解释质量参考标准，并用五模型MLLM评审团恢复70%以上的人类偏好排名（一致同意项达83%），最终形成公开基准和排行榜供CBM作者提交解释进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）以可解释性为核心动机，但其评估仍主要依赖下游分类精度和零散的定性示例，缺乏量化且可扩展的可解释性度量标准。大规模真实概念标注不可行，且概念列表缺乏共识，形成开放式问题，亟需一种人类对齐的、可规模化的解释质量评估方法。

Method: （1）建立人类研究：在CUB-200、ImageNet-100、Places365三个数据集上，针对900个图像对比项收集2700条人类判断，让标注者比较LF-CBM、VLG-CBM、CBM-Suite两两解释的实用性，判定"更好/同样好/同样差"；（2）构建五模型MLLM评审团（Council），由开放权重的多模态大语言模型组成，对给定图像及其CBM解释生成解释质量分数；（3）通过人类参考标准校验评审团有效性；（4）发布CBX-Bench公开基准和排行榜，接收新CBM解释并维护数据集级别的解释质量排名。

Result: 五模型MLLM评审团在人类参考标准上恢复了超过70%的严格人类偏好排名；在人类标注完全一致的条目上该比例提升至83%。

Conclusion: CBX-Bench提供了人类对齐、可扩展的CBM解释质量评估方案，超越了仅依赖准确率和孤立定性示例的传统评估方式，为推动CBM可解释性的量化评估建立了公共基础设施。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) are designed to make visual classification interpretable by expressing predictions through human-understandable concepts. Although interpretability is the central motivation for CBMs, they are still largely evaluated as predictive models by downstream classification accuracy, supplemented by isolated qualitative examples. This highlights a pressing need for quantitative measures, a challenge complicated by the infeasibility of ground-truth concept annotation at scale and the open nature of concept lists due to a lack of consensus. To fill this gap, we develop a multimodal large language model (MLLM) council that, given an image and its CBM explanation, produces an explanation quality score. To ground and validate the council, we first conduct a human study to establish a ground-truth reference for CBM explanation quality: for an image, annotators compare explanations from two of LF-CBM, VLG-CBM, and CBM-Suite and choose the more useful one, or mark them as equally good or equally bad, yielding 2700 judgments over 900 image-comparison items on CUB-200, ImageNet-100, and Places365. Against this human reference, our five-model council, consisting of open-weight MLLMs, recovers over 70% of strict human preference rankings, rising to 83% on items where human annotators unanimously agree. Building on this validated council, we introduce CBX-Bench, a public benchmark and leaderboard: authors of new CBMs can submit their model's explanations, and CBX-Bench scores them with the council and maintains dataset-level rankings of explanation quality. CBX-Bench thus provides a human-aligned, scalable evaluation of CBM explanations beyond accuracy and isolated qualitative examples. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/meric-karadag/cbx-bench.

</details>


### [42] [ConceptFormer: Learning Adaptive Latent Concepts for Query-Document Alignment in Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15698)
*Peng Chunyi,Xu Zhipeng,Yan Yukun,Liu Zhenghao,Yu Shi,Mei Sen,Sun Yubo,Zhang Yongheng,Zhou Jie,Gu Yu,Yu Ge,Sun Maosong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConceptFormer，一种用于视觉文档检索的潜在概念表示学习框架。它通过连续、查询条件的潜在概念令牌桥接局部视觉证据与语义相关性，无需文本中间表示或直接依赖原始视觉标注，在多个基准上分别比最强视觉检索基线和OCR文本检索基线相对提升16.7%和22.1%的平均NDCG@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索的细粒度监督主要依赖文本描述或局部视觉区域作为证据代理，但这些信号要么忽视复杂的视觉结构，要么提供不完整、不准确的证据表示，无法充分捕捉跨文本、布局、图表和视觉结构分布的查询相关证据。

Method: ConceptFormer将查询相关证据建模为连续的、查询条件的潜在概念，显式桥接局部视觉证据与语义相关性。训练时使用强视觉语言模型动态确定潜在概念令牌的数量，并将这些概念作为中间表示来弥合查询与文档之间的语义差距，引导嵌入空间的学习。

Result: 在多样化的视觉文档检索基准上，ConceptFormer在平均NDCG@10上相对于最强视觉检索基线获得16.7%的相对提升，相对于最强OCR文本检索基线获得22.1%的相对提升。分析表明潜在概念能有效连接局部视觉证据与语义相关性，同时捕捉细粒度文本线索和复杂的文档级视觉结构。

Conclusion: 潜在概念表示能够有效替代文本中间表示和原始视觉标注作为监督信号，在视觉文档检索中同时兼顾细粒度证据和整体视觉结构，实现更强的检索对齐效果。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval is a critical component of multimodal retrieval-augmented generation, aiming to identify query-relevant pages from document collections where evidence is distributed across text, layout, charts, and visual structures. Recent efforts toward finer-grained supervision primarily rely on textual descriptions or localized visual regions as evidence proxies. However, such supervision signals may either overlook complex visual structures or provide incomplete and inaccurate representations of the underlying evidence. To address these limitations, we propose ConceptFormer, a latent concept representation learning framework for visual document retrieval. ConceptFormer models query-relevant evidence as continuous, query-conditioned latent concepts that explicitly bridge localized visual evidence and semantic relevance, without requiring either textual intermediate representations or direct reliance on raw visual annotations. During training, ConceptFormer employs a strong vision-language model to dynamically determine the number of latent concept tokens and uses these concepts as an intermediate representation to bridge the semantic gap between queries and documents, thereby guiding the learning of the embedding space. Experiments on diverse visual document retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that ConceptFormer achieves 16.7\% and 22.1\% relative improvements in average NDCG@10 over the strongest visual retrieval baseline and the strongest OCR-based text retrieval baseline, respectively. Further analysis reveals that latent concepts effectively connect localized visual evidence with semantic relevance, enabling the retriever to capture both fine-grained textual cues and complex document-level visual structures while preserving strong retrieval alignment. Codes and data are available at https://github.com/Neuir/ConceptFormer.

</details>


### [43] [Geometry-Calibrated Closed-Form Shrinkage for SAR Despeckling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15028)
*Xuran Hu,Mingzhe Zhu,Djordje Stanković,Yujie Zhu,Zhenpeng Feng,Yifang Ban,Ljubiša Stanković*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了一种基于非局部稀疏估计的SAR图像去斑方法。该方法利用log-Yeo-Johnson变换、相似斑块分组、左奇异基编码和系数收缩。作者证明了三个通常被视为可调参数的量实际上由构造本身固定：群字典是正交的（使加权Lasso有精确的逐系数软阈值闭式解）、字典估计导致的正则化常数可通过随机矩阵论转化为几何校准修正、奇异投影使系数噪声近似高斯。最终得到一个确定性、无需训练的估计器，在24项PSNR/SSIM比较中18项排名第一，并在六个真实SAR配置中取得最低比值图像均值偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR去斑是一个逆恢复问题，需要在抑制乘性非高斯噪声的同时保留散射结构。现有的非局部稀疏估计方法依赖于多个可调超参数（如加权矩阵、正则化常数、收缩尺度等），这些参数通常需要手动调整，缺乏理论依据，且在不同图像和传感器之间难以泛化。

Method: 该方法基于log-Yeo-Johnson变换、相似斑块分组、各组的左奇异正交基编码和系数收缩。核心贡献是三个理论发现：(1) 由于群字典是正交的，加权Lasso退化为精确的逐系数软阈值闭式解，两个加权矩阵实际上是一个阈值场的分子和分母；(2) 通过随机矩阵论，将基字典估计中正则化常数转化为与群纵横比γ=p²/K相关的几何校准修正，将斑块大小、组大小和收缩尺度合并为一个解析决定的自由度；(3) 奇异投影使系数噪声在任意视数下近似高斯，从而定位了精确散斑似然失效的临界点。

Result: 该估计器是确定性的、无需训练的，对每幅图像和每种传感器应用同一组解析确定的参数。在三个合成基准上，与十二种已发表方法进行24项PSNR/SSIM比较中，有18项排名第一；在来自五种传感器的六个真实SAR配置中，取得了比值图像相对理论散斑模型的最低均值偏差。

Conclusion: 通过揭示该非局部稀疏估计器中三个"看似可调实为固定"的量的内在结构，作者将多个人工调参环节统一为单一解析决定的自由度，显著简化了SAR去斑流程，同时实现了更优的性能，且无需训练、可跨传感器泛化。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) despeckling is an inverse-recovery problem in which multiplicative non-Gaussian noise must be suppressed without erasing scattering structures. We revisit a nonlocal sparse estimator that applies a log--Yeo--Johnson transformation, stacks similar patches into groups, codes each group on its own left singular basis, and shrinks the resulting coefficients. Three quantities usually treated as tunable are shown to be fixed by this construction. First, the group dictionary is orthonormal, so the weighted Lasso admits an exact coefficient-wise soft-threshold solution: the iterative inner solver is unnecessary, and the two apparent weighting matrices are the numerator and denominator of a single threshold field rather than independent modules. Second, because the dictionary is estimated from the noisy group itself, its retained subspace absorbs speckle in proportion to the group aspect ratio $γ=p^2/K$; a random-matrix argument converts the corresponding regularization constant into a geometry-calibrated correction and collapses patch size, group size, and shrinkage scale into one analytically determined degree of freedom. Third, singular projection makes the coefficient noise nearly Gaussian at every tested look number, which locates the point at which an exact speckle likelihood ceases to be informative. The resulting estimator is deterministic, training-free, and applies one set of analytically determined settings to every image and sensor. It ranks first in 18 of 24 PSNR/SSIM comparisons against twelve published methods on three synthetic benchmarks, and attains the lowest mean deviation of the ratio image from the theoretical speckle model over six real-SAR configurations from five sensors. Code is available \href{https://github.com/Teriri1999/Geometry-Calibrated-Closed-Form-Shrinkage-for-SAR-Despeckling}{here}.

</details>


### [44] [PixelControl: Fine-Grained Condition Fidelity in Text-to-Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15705)
*Xin Lin,Haodong Li,Zhifei Zhang,Yutong Yang,Haitian Zheng,Juanxi Tian,Zhe Lin,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PixelControl，一个基于像素空间的可控扩散生成框架，解决潜在空间扩散模型在细粒度条件（如物体边界、细轮廓、中等/小尺寸区域）上的保真度不足问题。它基于PixelDiT骨干架构，通过结构感知控制注入和多尺度金字塔循环损失两种互补设计，在深度、分割、边缘等条件控制任务上显著提升结构保真度和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控文本到图像扩散模型虽然能遵循空间条件的整体布局，但在细粒度结构（物体边界、细轮廓、中/小尺寸条件区域）上常出现违反。这一问题在基于VAE的潜在扩散中尤为突出，因为空间压缩会削弱高频和低面积条件信号，导致细粒度条件保真度不足。

Method: PixelControl基于PixelDiT风格骨干构建，避免潜在空间瓶颈，并引入两种互补设计：1）结构感知控制注入（Structure-Aware Control Injection），通过推导条件结构图来增强空间敏感区域周围的注入控制残差；2）多尺度金字塔循环损失（Multi-Scale Pyramid Cycle Loss），在多个分辨率下验证生成图像与条件结构的一致性，平衡全局布局一致性与局部边界及细节精度。此外支持深度、分割、边缘及其组合的模态专用控制分支与轻量级门控融合。

Result: 在深度、分割和边缘控制任务上的实验表明，PixelControl相比现有可控生成方法显著提升了结构保真度和视觉质量，尤其在边界以及中等/小尺寸条件区域上取得了突出增益。

Conclusion: PixelControl通过像素空间生成配合结构感知注入与多尺度一致性验证机制，有效解决了细粒度条件保真问题，为可控图像生成提供了一种更精确的结构控制方案。

Abstract: Controllable text-to-image diffusion models can often follow the global layout of spatial conditions, yet still violate fine-grained structures such as object boundaries, thin contours, and medium/small conditioned regions. This limitation is especially problematic for VAE-based latent diffusion, where spatial compression can weaken high-frequency and low-area condition signals. We propose PixelControl, a pixel-space controllable diffusion framework for fine-grained condition fidelity. Built on a PixelDiT-style backbone, PixelControl avoids the latent bottleneck and introduces two complementary designs. First, Structure-Aware Control Injection derives a condition structure map and uses it to strengthen injected control residuals around spatially sensitive regions. Second, Multi-Scale Pyramid Cycle Loss verifies generated images against condition-derived structures across multiple resolutions, balancing global layout consistency with local boundary and detail accuracy. PixelControl supports depth, segmentation, edge, and their combinations through modality-specific control branches with lightweight gated fusion. Experiments across depth, segmentation, and edge control show that PixelControl improves structural fidelity and visual quality over existing controllable generation methods, with especially strong gains on boundaries and medium/small conditioned regions. The project page can be found at: https://linxin0.github.io/pixelcontrol_homepage/pixelcontrol-site/

</details>


### [45] [CardiacMamba: Fair and Robust RGB-RF Fusion for Remote Heart Rate Estimation via State Space Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15831)
*Bo Zhao,Zheng Wu,Yiping Xie,Zitong YU*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CardiacMamba是一个融合RGB面部光学信号与射频（RF）心脏运动信号的远程光电容积描记（rPPG）框架，通过状态空间建模实现非接触式心率监测。在EquiPleth数据集上达到SOTA性能（MAE 0.96 bpm），并将深浅肤色间的MAE差距缩小至0.26 bpm，兼具公平性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯RGB的rPPG方法易受光照变化、运动伪影和肤色依赖的光学反射特性影响，导致心率监测精度和公平性受限。作者旨在提出一种融合光学与射频信号的方案，以同时提升准确性和跨肤色的公平性。

Method: 提出CardiacMamba框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）时间差分Mamba模块（TDMM），增强RF信号中细微的时间变化；（2）基于双向状态空间模型（SSM）的交互机制，对齐异质的RGB-RF动态特征；（3）通道级快速傅里叶变换（CFFT）模块，实现通道域的频谱精炼。

Result: 在EquiPleth数据集上取得MAE 0.96 bpm、RMSE 3.06 bpm、Pearson相关系数0.97的SOTA性能；深浅肤色MAE差距降至0.26 bpm；在RGB退化及RF缺失条件下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RGB-RF多模态融合结合状态空间建模能有效提升rPPG心率监测的精度、公平性与鲁棒性，为消除rPPG系统中肤色偏差提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact heart rate (HR) monitoring from facial videos, but RGB-only methods are vulnerable to illumination changes, motion artifacts, and skin-tone-dependent optical reflectance. We propose CardiacMamba, a fair and robust RGB-RF fusion framework that integrates optical facial cues and radio-frequency cardiac motion cues through state space modeling. CardiacMamba introduces a Temporal Difference Mamba Module (TDMM) to enhance subtle RF temporal variations, a bidirectional SSM-based interaction mechanism to align heterogeneous RGB-RF dynamics, and a Channel-wise Fast Fourier Transform (CFFT) module for channel-domain spectral refinement. On the EquiPleth dataset, CardiacMamba achieves state-of-the-art performance with 0.96 bpm MAE, 3.06 bpm RMSE, and 0.97 Pearson correlation, while reducing the observed light-dark skin-tone MAE gap to 0.26 bpm and maintaining robustness under RGB degradation and RF-missing conditions

</details>


### [46] [Depth-Dominant Skeleton Detection for Natural Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16367)
*Chengkun Rao,Yixuan Deng,Min Li,Yangjun Ou,Ye Li,Ziwei Luo,Zhaojing Wang,Junwei Tang,Bangchao Wang,Xiaoyun Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出以深度图像为主模态、RGB图像为辅模态的骨架检测新范式，并据此提出DDSkel模型。DDSkel采用非对称编码器设计将RGB信息融合到深度特征中（RGB分支参数量仅为深度分支的12%），结构简单，仅有当前最优方法36%的可训练参数，却在最具挑战性的SymPASCAL数据集上超越了所有现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然场景骨架检测均以RGB图像为唯一输入，在复杂内容图像上性能显著下降。作者观察到深度图像对颜色和纹理不敏感，能提供清晰的区域轮廓和区域间空间关系，有助于缓解复杂场景下骨架检测的困难，因此提出以深度为主的新范式。

Method: 提出DDSkel模型，采用深度图像为主模态、RGB为辅助模态的非对称编码器设计，将RGB信息融合到深度特征中。RGB模态分支参数量仅为深度模态分支的12%，整体模型结构简洁，无复杂设计。

Result: DDSkel仅使用当前最优方法36%的可训练参数，即在最具挑战性、包含大量复杂图像的SymPASCAL数据集上超越了所有现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了深度主导、RGB辅助的骨架检测范式是可行且高效的，为自然场景骨架检测提供了新方向，同时表明更少的参数和更简洁的结构也能取得更优性能。

Abstract: To date, all natural scene skeleton detection follows the paradigm of taking RGB images as the sole input; despite notable progress, methods under this paradigm suffer significant performance degradation on complex-content images. We observe that depth images are inherently insensitive to color and texture, and can provide clear regional contours and inter-region spatial relationships, which naturally alleviates the difficulty of skeleton detection in complex scenarios. Motivated by this observation, this paper proposes for the first time a novel skeleton detection paradigm where depth images serve as the dominant modality and RGB images act as the auxiliary, and accordingly presents a model DDSkel (short for Depth-Dominant Skeleton Detection) under this paradigm. DDSkel employs an asymmetric encoder design to fuse RGB information into depth features, with the RGB modality branch having only 12% the parameters of the depth modality branch. DDSkel has a simple structure without intricate designs. Nevertheless, with only 36% of the trainable parameters of the current best method, DDSkel outperforms all state-of-the-art approaches on SymPASCAL, the most challenging dataset with a large volume of complex images.

</details>


### [47] [EgoTac: In-the-wild Tactile Prediction from Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15060)
*Wenkang Zhang,Chengbo Yuan,Zicheng Zhang,Zhengxue Cheng,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoTac，一个从第一人称人类视频中直接预测丰富触觉信息的通用模型。该模型在超过570万图像-触觉对的大规模统一语料上训练，涵盖连续力测量和二元接触。实验表明，域内预测的平均力误差低于0.06N，在域外接触预测基准上持续优于最先进的接触估计器，并能对无约束的真实世界视频进行零样本预测，为触觉感知的机器人学习提供了可扩展的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对于灵巧操作至关重要，但用于机器人学习的大规模第一人称人类数据大多缺乏触觉信息。直接采集大规模触觉数据受限于传感器条件，而人类视频数据丰富、接触密集且易于扩展。因此作者探索一个自然问题：触觉信号能否仅从视觉中推断？

Method: EgoTac是一个从第一人称人类视频中预测丰富触觉信息的可泛化模型。它在一个统一的语料库上训练，包含超过570万对图像-触觉数据对，覆盖连续力测量和二元接触两种模态。通过学习这个多样化数据集，模型能够捕捉不同交互场景中细微的触觉动态。

Result: 域内预测的平均力误差低于0.06N；在域外接触预测基准上持续优于最先进的接触估计器；能捕捉真实触觉数据的上升和下降模式；支持对无约束真实世界视频的零样本预测。缩放分析显示数据多样性和数据量都能稳步提升性能。

Conclusion: EgoTac提供了一条从第一人称人类视频中提取触觉先验的可扩展途径，使广泛应用的触觉感知机器人学习成为可能。

Abstract: Touch is fundamental to dexterous manipulation, yet most egocentric human data increasingly used for robot learning lacks tactile information. Directly collecting large-scale tactile data is challenging due to sensor limitations, while human video data is abundant, contact-rich, and easily scalable. This motivates a natural question: can tactile signals be inferred purely from vision? To address this, we introduce EgoTac, a generalizable model that predicts rich tactile information directly from egocentric human videos. EgoTac is trained on a unified corpus of over 5.7M image-tactile pairs, covering both continuous force measurements and binary contacts. By learning from this diverse dataset, EgoTac captures nuanced touch dynamics across varied interactions. Experiments demonstrate strong performance: in-domain prediction achieves an average force error below 0.06N. On out-of-domain contact prediction benchmarks, EgoTac consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art contact estimator. It also captures the rise and fall patterns of real tactile data and enables zero-shot predictions on unconstrained real-world videos. Scaling analyses further reveal that both data diversity and volume improve performance steadily. Overall, EgoTac provides a scalable pathway to extract tactile priors from egocentric human videos, enabling broadly applicable tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [48] [FLEET: Token-Based Feature Extraction for Event Camera-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16523)
*Tristan Gottwald,Maximilian Schier,Melanie Schaller,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLEET是一种基于事件相机序列的特征提取器，利用随机傅里叶特征和交叉注意力将可变长度的事件流压缩为固定大小的潜在表示，解耦计算成本与传感器分辨率，无需预训练即可端到端学习，在新基准上超越SOTA并表现出对观测频率变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有异步、高频、稀疏且低延迟的优势，理论上非常适合控制策略设计，但现有强化学习方法未能充分利用这些特性：基于CNN的方法将事件聚合为稀疏网格，导致计算成本与传感器分辨率耦合且模糊了时间信息；而现有基于生成的方法依赖轨迹数据进行预训练。

Method: FLEET直接处理事件序列而非聚合网格，采用随机傅里叶特征结合交叉注意力机制，将可变长度的事件流压缩为固定大小的潜在表示，从而将特征提取器骨干网络的计算成本与传感器分辨率解耦，实现无需辅助损失的端到端学习。

Result: 在新建的高吞吐量基准上验证FLEET，其基于序列的方法在性能上超越SOTA，并对观测频率变化表现出更优的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FLEET证明了直接处理事件流（而非聚合网格）的有效性，其固定大小潜在表示的设计实现了计算效率与传感器分辨率解耦，为事件相机在强化学习中的进一步应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Event cameras generate asynchronous, high-frequency data streams offering spatially sparse information at lower latency than traditional cameras.In principle, these properties should be ideal for the design of control policies.However, reinforcement learning research in this field remains limited as existing approaches fail to fully exploit the sensor's properties.CNN-based methods negate the sensors benefits by aggregating events into sparse grids. This couples compute cost to sensor resolution and blurs the temporal information. Meanwhile, existing generative baselines rely on the availability of trajectory data to pretrain the model. We propose FLEET (Feature Learning from Events via Efficient Tokenization), a feature extractor that processes event sequences directly. Leveraging random Fourier features and cross-attention, our architecture compresses variable streams into fixed-size latent representations. This decouples inference cost of the feature extractor's backbone from the sensor's resolution, enabling end-to-end learning without auxiliary losses. We validate FLEET on a new, high-throughput benchmark. The results demonstrate that our sequence-based approach surpasses SOTA performance and exhibits superior robustness to variations in observation frequencies.

</details>


### [49] [GaussianDWM++: Language-Grounded 3D Gaussian Driving World Model for Unified Scene Understanding, Editing, and Multi-Modal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16234)
*Tianchen Deng,Xuefeng Chen,Shuang Wu,Qu Chen,Jiajun Zhu,Bo Dai,Jianfei Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于基础特征高斯的驾驶世界模型框架，通过将Qwen/SigLIP视觉语言特征蒸馏到3D高斯图元中，统一了场景理解、语言接地推理、可控4D编辑和多模态生成能力，并在多个驾驶基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型主要聚焦于条件场景生成，缺乏显式的3D场景理解、语言接地推理和可控4D编辑能力；同时常用的点云、占据栅格或BEV表示难以实现文本信息与底层3D场景结构的细粒度对齐。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 基础特征高斯分词器，将Qwen/SigLIP视觉语言特征直接蒸馏到3D高斯图元中，构建紧致的开词汇高斯语义场；(2) 几何感知高斯适配器，结合重要性感知分层选择与文本条件的Perceiver式交叉注意力，将密集高斯图元聚合为紧致的世界令牌；(3) KL散度基于的高斯-图像分布对齐目标，使高斯世界令牌与基础图像令牌对齐。基于此表示支持指令可控的场景编辑，如天气条件生成和动态车辆操控。

Result: 在多个驾驶基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法在场景理解、视觉接地、规划导向推理和可控4D生成任务上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉语言基础模型的特征蒸馏到3D高斯表示中，成功实现了驾驶场景中语言-3D结构细粒度对齐的统一框架，覆盖理解、推理、编辑与生成四大能力，并将开源代码与数据集。

Abstract: Driving World Models (DWMs) have recently advanced rapidly with generative models, yet most existing methods mainly focus on conditional scene generation and lack explicit 3D scene understanding, language-grounded reasoning, and controllable 4D editing capabilities. Moreover, commonly used point cloud, occupancy, or BEV representations make it difficult to achieve fine-grained alignment between textual information and the underlying 3D scene structure. To address these limitations, we propose a foundation-feature Gaussian driving world model that unifies scene understanding, language-grounded reasoning, controllable 4D editing, and multi-modal generation within a single framework. Specifically, we introduce a foundation-feature Gaussian tokenizer that directly distills Qwen/SigLIP visual-language features into 3D Gaussian primitives, building a compact open-vocabulary Gaussian semantic field. We further design a geometry-aware Gaussian adapter that combines importance-aware hierarchical selection with text-conditioned Perceiver-style cross-attention to aggregate dense Gaussian primitives into compact world tokens. To improve representation compatibility, we introduce a KL-based Gaussian--image distribution alignment objective that aligns Gaussian world tokens with foundation image tokens. Based on the aligned Gaussian representation, our framework further supports instruction-controllable scene editing, including weather-conditioned generation and dynamic vehicle manipulation. Extensive experiments on broader driving benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across scene understanding, visual grounding, planning-oriented reasoning, and controllable 4D generation tasks. We will release the code and datasets publicly on Github.

</details>


### [50] [MITE-Net: SWaP-Optimized 4K Video Tiny Target Perception for Embodied Edge SAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15830)
*Mingshuo Xu,Mu Hua,Jigen Peng,Qi Wang,Shigang Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MITE-Net，一种面向边缘设备（UAV）上4K高分辨率影像实时微小目标感知的SWaP优化级联架构，通过生物启发式的无学习区域提议网络（TTM-RPN）与超轻量R-CNN头部耦合，并构建SAR-Tiny数据集（SeaDroneSee-Tiny与UAVID-Tiny）及在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier上进行硬件级基准测试。在海上动态场景中，MITE-Net以30.33 FPS实现100%搜索成功率，仅消耗3.19W（9.51 FPS/W），远优于YOLO基线；但在复杂城市场景中暴露出无学习生物前端与超轻量头部的复合结构性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备（如UAV）在搜救任务中受严格的尺寸、重量与功耗（SWaP）约束，传统图像降采样导致微小目标特征严重丢失，而切块处理又带来不可接受的延迟，亟需一种兼顾实时性、目标召回率与能效的高分辨率微小目标感知方案。

Method: 提出MITE-Net，一种SWaP优化的级联架构，包含：(1) 生物启发、无学习的微小目标运动区域提议网络（TTM-RPN）作为前端；(2) 参数少于0.14M的类R-CNN轻量检测头。同时构建SAR-Tiny数据集（重标注两个UAV数据集：SeaDroneSee-Tiny对应64-256像素微小目标的海上动态场景，UAVID-Tiny对应小于64像素的极微小目标的杂乱城市场景），并在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier边缘设备上与YOLO系列模型进行基准对比。

Result: 在4K海上影像上，MITE-Net以30.33 FPS获得100%搜索成功率，功耗仅3.19W（能效9.51 FPS/W），在目标召回率和能效上大幅超越YOLO基线；在UAVID-Tiny上则发现无学习生物前端在复杂城市背景下失效，且超轻量头部表示能力不足，构成复合性结构局限。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种高效的机载感知范式和严格的基准，验证了级联架构在SWaP受限条件下的有效性，同时揭示了此类设计在城市复杂场景下的短板，为未来端到端搜救目标感知架构指明改进方向。

Abstract: Real-time tiny target perception in high-resolution imagery is critical for embodied Search-and-Rescue (SAR) missions. However, strict Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints on edge devices like UAVs create a bottleneck: traditional image downsampling causes severe feature loss, while slice-based processing incurs prohibitive latency. To address this gap, this paper introduces a comprehensive framework encompassing a novel architecture, specialized datasets, and hardware-level benchmarks. First, we propose MITE-Net, a SWaP-optimized cascaded architecture, which couples a bio-inspired, learning-free Tiny Target Motion-Based Region Proposal Network (TTM-RPN) with a sub-0.14M-parameter R-CNN-like head. Second, to standardize 4K tiny target evaluation, we construct the SAR-Tiny Datasets by relabeling two challenging UAV datasets: SeaDroneSee-Tiny (dynamic maritime scenes, tiny targets predominantly of 64-256 pixels ) and UAVID-Tiny (cluttered urban scenes, extremely tiny targets, less than 64 pixels). Third, we benchmark against state-of-the-art YOLO models on an edge device, NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier, where MITE-Net directly processes 4K maritime imagery, achieving a 100\% search success rate at 30.33 FPS. Consuming merely 3.19 W (9.51 FPS/W), MITE-Net vastly outperforms YOLO baselines in target recall and energy efficiency. Conversely, UAVID-Tiny evaluations expose a compound structural limitation: the learning-free bionic front-end struggles against urban backgrounds, while the ultra-lightweight head lacks representational capacity for complex features. Ultimately, this work delivers an efficient onboard perception paradigm and a rigorous baseline guiding future end-to-end SAR architectures.

</details>


### [51] [MODAL: Multi-Modal Object Re-ID via Model-Driven Sparse Decoupling and Text-Image Differential Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15096)
*Chengbo Huang,Jun-Jie Huang,Long Lan,Tianrui Liu,Xueqiong Li,Yuanxi Peng,Xinwang Liu,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MODAL，一种基于耦合稀疏编码理论和差分抑制原理的多模态目标重识别框架。其核心包括多模态特征稀疏解耦模块（通过模型驱动的深度展开方式实现单模态特异、双模态及三模态共享表示的分解）和文本-图像差分过滤模块（利用粗粒度文本语义抑制视觉表示中的任务无关响应）。在四个数据集上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态目标重识别方法缺乏原理性的特征解耦和一致的多模态融合策略，导致表示纠缠、跨模态冲突、判别线索模糊，且在模态缺失下遭受分布偏移问题。

Method: 提出MODAL框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 多模态特征稀疏解耦模块，基于多模态耦合稀疏编码以模型驱动的深度展开方式实现，显式将多模态特征分解为单模态特异、双模态和三模态共享表示；(2) 模态感知子空间激活机制，在模态不完整时选择性激活一致的共享子空间以缓解性能退化；(3) 文本-图像差分过滤模块，利用粗粒度文本语义自适应抑制解耦视觉表示中的任务无关响应。

Result: 在四个数据集上的大量实验表明，MODAL达到了最先进的性能，并具有优越的可解释性（透明性）。

Conclusion: MODAL通过原理性的特征解耦（耦合稀疏编码）和差分抑制，有效解决了多模态Re-ID中的表示纠缠和模态缺失问题，在性能和可解释性上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multi-modal object re-identification (Re-ID) aims to facilitate cross-camera object retrieval in complex environments by leveraging complementary information from visual (e.g., RGB, NIR, TIR) and textual modalities. However, existing approaches often lack principled feature disentanglement and coherent multi-modal integration, leading to entangled representations that introduce cross-modal conflicts, obscure discriminative cues, and suffer distribution shift under modality-missing conditions. To tackle these challenges, we propose MODAL, a novel multi-modal object re-identification framework, grounded in coupled sparse coding theory and differential suppression principles. A core component of MODAL is a Multi-modal Feature Sparse Decoupling module, developed in a model-driven deep unrolling manner based on multi-modal coupled sparse coding. It explicitly decomposes multi-modal features into uni-modal specific, bi-modal and tri-modal shared representations, thereby achieving more transparent and effective feature disentanglement. Benefiting from the principled feature disentanglement, MODAL naturally mitigates performance degradation in incomplete-modality scenarios via a Modality-Aware Subspace Activation that selectively activates only the consistently shared subspaces. Moreover, we propose a Text-Image Differential Filtering module that leverages coarse-grained textual semantics to adaptively suppress task-irrelevant responses in the decoupled visual representations, thereby enhancing discriminative information. Extensive experiments on four datasets demonstrate that MODAL achieves state-of-the-art performance with superior transparency.

</details>


### [52] [Spatial Temporal Synergy: Balancing Change and Invariance in Text Driven 3D Human Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16008)
*Shaohui Lin,Zhenwu Shi,Jingyu Gong,Jiao Xie,Yu Zhou,Baochang Zhang,Lizhuang Ma,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIME统一框架，将文本驱动人体动作编辑中的"变化"与"不变性"解耦为空间姿态和时间节奏两个维度，解决现有扩散模型在空间扭曲和物理节奏破坏上的问题，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式文本驱动动作编辑方法难以平衡文本响应的"变化"与原始动作的"不变性"：过于依赖粗糙的空间约束和僵化的均匀时间假设，导致空间动作扭曲和变长编辑中原生物理节奏被破坏。

Method: 提出CIME框架，包含两大模块：1) 空间姿态维度采用全监督正负学习机制，涵盖层次化回顾特征监督、细微动作保持和三元组语义对齐；2) 时间节奏维度引入黎曼非均匀积分流形映射（RNIMM）模块，通过运动学感知的非均匀时间戳实现编辑文本中物理节拍的高保真复现。

Result: 在MotionFix和STANCE Adjustment数据集上的大量实验表明，CIME在编辑对齐和结构保真度方面达到最先进的性能，验证了统一架构的有效性。

Conclusion: CIME通过将变化与不变性在空间和时间两个维度进行全面解耦，有效解决了扩散模型在文本驱动动作编辑中的空间失真和时间节奏破坏问题，实现了编辑响应与结构保持的更好平衡。

Abstract: Text-driven human motion editing aims to modify existing motion sequences according to natural language instructions while maintaining the structural consistency of the original motion. Existing diffusion-based approaches struggle to balance text-responsive "change" and inertial "invariance". They often rely on coarse spatial constraints and rigid uniform time assumptions, leading to spatial motion distortions and the destruction of intrinsic physical rhythms during variable-length editing. To handle these challenges, we propose Change and Invariance Motion Editing (CIME), a unified framework that comprehensively decouples change and invariance into spatial pose and temporal rhythm dimensions. For spatial poses, our method integrates an omni-supervised positive-negative learning mechanism comprising hierarchical retrospective feature supervision, subtle motion preservation, and triplet-based semantic alignment. For temporal rhythms, we introduce the Riemannian Non-uniform Integral Manifold Mapping (RNIMM) module, which achieves high-fidelity reproduction of physical beats in the edited text via kinematics-aware non-uniform timestamps. Extensive experiments on the MotionFix and STANCE Adjustment datasets demonstrate that CIME achieves state-of-the-art performance in editing alignment and structural fidelity, validating the effectiveness of our unified architecture. Our source codes and models have been released at: github.com/ZhenwuShi/CIME.git

</details>


### [53] [SpIn-ViT: Designing a Sparsity-Induced Vision Transformer That Is Mechanistically Interpretable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14922)
*Philip H. Lee,Parth Padalkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpIn-ViT框架，将预训练的ViT与改进的稀疏自编码器（SAE）端到端联合训练，使稀疏patch级表示直接与图像分类目标对齐，相比已有的事后SAE方法在分类精度、可解释性评分和神经符号规则集上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SAE的视觉Transformer可解释性方法多为事后工具，即在ViT已优化完成后于冻结表示上训练SAE，导致潜在特征与下游分类目标不对齐，限制了特征的可解释性与任务相关性。

Method: SpIn-ViT将预训练ViT与修改后的SAE端到端联合训练，使稀疏patch级表示直接与图像分类目标对齐，学习语义连贯的神经元激活以定位有意义的图像区域，并在此基础上提取SAE神经元构建神经符号规则集。

Result: 在九个图像分类基准上，SpIn-ViT相比最优事后SAE方法：分类精度平均提高8.84%，AI可解释性评分提高近4倍，人工评估评分提高2倍以上；基于SAE神经元构建的神经符号模型分类精度平均提高5.97%，且规则集规模减小58.8%。

Conclusion: 将SAE训练与ViT分类目标端到端对齐能够有效提升表示的可解释性与任务性能，并支持更紧凑、更准确的神经符号规则提取，为可解释视觉模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has recently expanded to Vision Transformers (ViTs), with Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) increasingly used as post-hoc tools to decompose internal representations into sparse and more interpretable features. However, because post-hoc SAEs are trained on frozen representations after the ViT has already been optimized, their latent features are not directly aligned with the downstream classification objective. We introduce SpIn-ViT, a framework that jointly trains a pretrained ViT and a modified SAE end-to-end, directly aligning sparse patch-level representations with image classification. SpIn-ViT learns semantically coherent neuron activations that localize meaningful image regions while maintaining competitive predictive performance. We evaluate SpIn-ViT across nine image-classification benchmarks using classification accuracy, quantitative interpretability metrics, AI-based and Human evaluations. Compared with the previous state-of-the-art post-hoc SAE method, SpIn-ViT achieves 8.84% higher average classification accuracy, an AI-based interpretability score nearly four times as high, and a human-evaluation score more than twice as high. We further extract interpretable rule-sets using the SAE neurons to create neurosymbolic models which achieve 5.97% higher average classification accuracy while requiring a 58.8\% smaller rule-set than the neurosymbolic models created from the SOTA post-hoc SAE method.

</details>


### [54] [SplatGuide: Geometric Priors from 3D Gaussians for Pose-Free Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16863)
*Yejun Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xu Ji,Yihao Wang,Yuxin Hou,Junyuan Fang,Juho-Matti Kilpeläinen,Arno Solin,Hamed Rezazadegan Tavakoli,Esa Rahtu,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatGuide提出了一种基于3DGS重建与多视图扩散相结合的无位姿新视角合成方法，通过对同一3DGS场景进行三重复用（像素渲染、逐高斯可见性投票图、重建token特征），弥合了重建与扩散之间的信息断层，在多个基准数据集上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将前馈3DGS重建与多视角扩散结合时，仅从重建中提取单一信号（像素渲染或学习特征），未能利用逐高斯的可见性信息进行遮挡感知的参考视图选择，导致渲染几何、可见性线索和学习特征被浪费，形成信息断层。

Method: SplatGuide将单一3DGS场景复用于三个互补角色：(1) 渲染图像提供像素级几何条件；(2) 逐高斯源视图索引渲染为目标视图投票图，实现遮挡感知的参考视图选择；(3) 重建token通过交叉注意力提供特征级引导。三种信号均来自同一次重建前向传播。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、DL3DV、Tanks-and-Temples和Mip-NeRF 360数据集上，SplatGuide达到最先进的无位姿新视角合成性能。在RealEstate10K上，使用中等数量输入视图时，甚至超越了使用真实位姿的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过充分利用3DGS重建中此前被忽视的可见性信息，并以统一方式融合渲染、几何与特征三种信号，SplatGuide有效解决了重建与扩散间的信息断层问题，验证了可见性线索在无位姿新视角合成中的关键价值。

Abstract: Generating photorealistic novel views from unposed images requires both 3D geometric understanding and the ability to synthesize unseen content. A natural strategy combines feed-forward 3DGS reconstruction with multi-view diffusion. Yet prior pipelines extract at most one signal from the reconstruction, either pixel rendering or learned features, while none exploits per-Gaussian visibility for occlusion-aware reference selection. This *information disconnect* leaves renderable geometry, visibility cues, and learned features unused. SplatGuide closes this disconnect by reusing a single 3DGS scene across three complementary roles. Rendered images provide pixel-aligned geometric conditioning. Per-Gaussian source-view indices are rendered into a target-view voting map for occlusion-aware reference selection. Reconstruction tokens supply feature-level guidance via cross-attention. All three signals derive from the same reconstruction forward pass. Across RealEstate10K, DL3DV, Tanks-and-Temples, and Mip-NeRF 360, SplatGuide achieves state-of-the-art pose-free novel view synthesis. On RealEstate10K, with a moderate number of input views, it surpasses the ground-truth-pose baseline.

</details>


### [55] [Steering the Flow: Inverting Face Recognition Models via Gradient-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16791)
*Ye Lu,Shen Wang,Zhaoyang Zhang,Yihan Yan,Li Liu,Runze Liu,Fanghui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMI（Steering Flow Model Inversion），一种两阶段白盒模型反演攻击方法，通过预训练无条件Flow Matching模型作为人脸先验，并在采样过程中使用渐进式引导调度器（PGS）注入时间依赖的目标特定梯度，将模型反演重构为轨迹引导任务。在CelebA数据集的ArcFace目标上达到0.9248的ACC、22.61的FID和0.3874的LPIPS，实现竞争性的攻击成功率与视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型反演攻击（MIA）方法依赖间接引导或高度随机的引导，难以稳定地将生成轨迹优化导向目标人脸图像，因此需要一种更可控、更稳定的反演方法。

Method: SFMI包含两个阶段：第一阶段预训练一个无条件Flow Matching模型，以编码人脸的流形作为稳健先验；第二阶段使用渐进式引导调度器（PGS），在采样过程中通过目标模型反向传播从中间生成状态获取梯度，并随采样时间逐步注入自适应的目标特定梯度信号到向量场中，将生成流从随机噪声逐步引导至目标类的高密度区域。

Result: 在CelebA数据集、身份不重叠的跨评估设置下，SFMI在ArcFace目标上达到ACC 0.9248、FID 22.61、LPIPS 0.3874；在多个目标模型上的广泛实验表明其在攻击成功率和视觉保真度方面达到竞争性的最先进水平。

Conclusion: SFMI通过将模型反演转化为轨迹引导任务，有效解决了现有方法优化不稳定的问题，在白盒协议下实现了攻击成功率和视觉质量的竞争性平衡。

Abstract: Model Inversion Attacks (MIAs) aim to reconstruct representative training samples of target identities from face recognition models, exposing critical security vulnerabilities. Existing methods typically rely on indirect guidance or highly stochastic guidance, making it difficult to stably optimize generation trajectories toward target facial images. In this paper, we propose Steering Flow Model Inversion (SFMI), a novel two-stage white-box model inversion method that reformulates inversion as a trajectory-steering task. Specifically, Step I, Learning a Generic Flow Matching Prior, pre-trains a generic unconditional Flow Matching model to encode the manifold of human faces as a robust prior. Step II, Attacking with Progressive Guidance Scheduler (PGS), injects time-dependent target-specific gradients during sampling. By backpropagating through the target model to obtain gradients from intermediate generated states, PGS progressively injects adaptive guidance signals into the vector field. This process effectively steers the current generative flow from random noise toward the high-density regions of the target class. Under an identity-disjoint cross-evaluation setting using the CelebA dataset, SFMI achieves an ACC of 0.9248, an FID of 22.61, and an LPIPS of 0.3874 on the ArcFace target. Extensive experiments on multiple target models demonstrate that SFMI achieves competitive state-of-the-art performance in attack success and visual fidelity under the evaluated white-box protocol.

</details>


### [56] [Unlocking Motion in Expressions: Temporal Calibration for Referring Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16332)
*Yiwen Jiang,Zhengtong Zhu,Ruixin Zhang,Jiaqing Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种表达式驱动的运动校准框架（EMC），通过从自然语言描述中显式提取运动语义信号，并在时间决策过程中动态校准运动信息的贡献，以解决指代视频目标分割中运动信息利用依赖的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RVOS方法在统一的跨模态时序建模框架中引入运动信息，但语言表达式对运动语义的依赖未被显式建模，导致无法根据不同语义需求自适应调整运动信息的使用。

Method: 提出EMC框架，包含三个模块：MSP（运动信号处理）从表达式中提取可解释的运动控制信号；MIC（运动影响校准）在时间决策中调整运动线索的贡献；STSC（语义时序阶段构建）构建与表达式相关的时序阶段，为运动校准提供紧凑的时间候选空间。

Result: 在六个标准基准上（Ref-YouTubeVOS、Ref-DAVIS17、MeViS valid/valid^u、A2D-Sentences、JHMDB-Sentences）进行了广泛评估，验证了方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模表达式中的运动语义并自适应校准运动信息贡献，EMC框架有效提升了指代视频目标分割的性能，代码将公开在GitHub上。

Abstract: Referring Video Object Segmentation (RVOS) aims to segment referred objects at the pixel level in video sequences based on natural language descriptions. Existing methods typically introduce motion information within a unified cross-modal temporal modeling framework, where language cues are used for target localization and segmentation. However, the dependency of expressions on motion semantics is not explicitly modeled, making it difficult to adaptively adjust the use of motion information according to different semantic requirements. To address these issues, we propose an Expression-driven Motion Calibration (EMC) framework for RVOS that explicitly unlocks and leverages the motion semantics within expressions. The proposed method extracts interpretable motion control signals from expressions via a Motion Signal Processing (MSP) module, and employs a Motion Influence Calibration (MIC) module to adjust the contribution of motion cues during temporal decision making. In addition, a Semantic Temporal Stage Construction (STSC) module is introduced to build expression-relevant temporal stages, providing a compact temporal candidate space for motion calibration. Through extensive evaluation on six standard benchmarks, including Ref-YouTubeVOS, Ref-DAVIS17, MeViS (valid/valid$^u$), A2D-Sentences, and JHMDB-Sentences, the superiority of our method is validated. We will release the code on https://github.com/Jeven7/EMC.

</details>


### [57] [VicEdit: Learning to Edit Videos from Visual In-Context Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16745)
*Yuji Wang,Teng Hu,Yuheng Chen,Ran Yi,Han Feng,Weijian Cao,Chengjie Wang,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉上下文编辑（Visual In-context Editing）新范式，将视频编辑从纯文本指令升级为多模态视觉引导（单图、图像对、视频对），并构建了首个大规模数据集VicEdit-400K（涵盖10类任务、40万样本）及统一框架VicEdit，通过模态自适应语义蒸馏和双上下文注入实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于指令的视频编辑虽然取得了进展，但纯文本指令本质上难以传达细粒度的纹理和复杂的动态信息，存在固有的感知鸿沟。为此需要引入视觉参考作为多模态引导，以弥补文本指令的信息不足。

Method: (1) 提出视觉上下文编辑新范式，支持单图像、图像对、视频对三种视觉引导形式；(2) 构建自动化数据管线，生成40万高质量样本并经过多维过滤保证视觉保真度和语义一致性，覆盖10种任务类型；(3) 设计VicEdit统一框架，其中Modality-Adaptive Semantic Distillation从异构视觉参考中适应性地提取模态特定语义token，再通过Dual-Context Injection将其与文本指令协同融合，使生成过程充分利用视觉和文本双信号。

Result: 在VicEditBench上的大量评估表明，VicEdit在基础指令编辑和视觉上下文编辑两类任务上均达到SOTA性能，验证了视觉上下文学习作为强大且可控的视频编辑范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 视觉上下文学习为视频编辑提供了一种新的可控范式，能有效弥合文本指令与视觉细节之间的鸿沟，为多模态引导的视频编辑建立了新方向。

Abstract: Despite progress in instruction-based video editing, unimodal textual instructions inherently struggle to convey fine-grained textures and complex dynamics. To bridge this perceptual gap, we propose Visual In-context Editing, a new paradigm elevating video editing from textual instructions to multi-modal visual guidance encompassing single image, image pair, and video pair. To facilitate this paradigm, we curate VicEdit-400K, the first large-scale dataset for visual in-context video editing. We develop an automated pipeline to generate 400K high-quality samples across ten task types, ensuring superior visual fidelity and semantic consistency through multi-dimensional filtering. Leveraging this foundation, we introduce VicEdit, a unified framework to bridge visual and textual contexts. To adaptively extract editing semantics from heterogeneous references, we design Modality-Adaptive Semantic Distillation, which produces modality-specific semantic tokens from visual references. These tokens are then synergistically integrated with textual instructions through Dual-Context Injection, enabling the generation process to benefit from both visual and textual signals. Extensive evaluations on VicEditBench demonstrate that VicEdit achieves state-of-the-art performance across both basic instruction editing and visual in-context editing tasks, establishing visual in-context learning as a powerful and controllable paradigm for video editing.

</details>


### [58] [YOLO26-RD: An End-to-End Road Damage Detection Network With Learnable Contrast Enhancement and Edge-Guided Downsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15713)
*Sompote Youwai,Pawarotorn Chaipetch,Hathairat Samaikul,Theerayut Yonseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YOLO26-RD，一个基于YOLO26的无NMS端到端路面病害检测器，包含两个轻量新颖模块（LearnableContrast和EdgeSPD）。通过对7,618张道路图像的数据优先审计，证实了"小目标"前提不成立（92%实例为COCO-large），并据此移除P2检测头而保留其特征融合路径，使mAP50提升2.8个点且训练时间减少8%。最佳配置在验证集上达到0.787 mAP50，超过项目基线0.771。残差误差归因分析表明裂纹类的困难主要来自分布偏移、亚像素宽度和标签不完整等数据层面因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路面病害检测被普遍视为小目标问题，促使研究者采用高分辨率P2/P4检测头和有无损下采样。作者希望通过数据优先（data-first）的审计方式检验这一前提是否成立，并主张用"测量驱动的减法"（移除不必要的检测层）替代"模块堆砌"（盲目增加结构），以更高效地提升检测性能。

Method: 1) 基于YOLO26构建YOLO26-RD端到端无NMS检测器；2) 设计两个轻量模块：LearnableContrast（494参数的可微分CLAHE模拟，在网络内部按tile自适应对比度）和EdgeSPD（Sobel门控的space-to-depth下采样器，仅比SPD-Conv多2个参数）；3) 在7,618张道路图像数据集上进行数据优先审计，分析实例尺寸分布与几何特性；4) 基于审计结论移除P2检测层但保留P2特征在融合路径中；5) 对瓶颈类裂纹进行失败模式分解，归因残余误差。

Result: (1) 审计推翻了小目标前提：92%实例为COCO-large，线性裂纹为极端长宽比结构（中位10:1），其难点在于敏感性而非定位；(2) 移除P2检测头保留P2融合特征后mAP50提升2.8点，epoch时间减少8%；(3) 最佳配置从零训练（640x640）达到0.787 mAP50，优于项目基线0.771；(4) 最稀有的patching类获得最大单类提升（比基线高2.6点，比未修改的YOLO26-RD对照高9.4点）；(5) 裂纹类的残余误差（约0.74）归因于训练/验证分布偏移（方向与长度）、640x640下的亚像素裂纹宽度及标签不完整，这些是架构改动无法解决的。

Conclusion: 对于路面图像检测，测量驱动的减法（基于数据分析删减冗余结构）优于模块堆砌（盲目新增模块）。作者发布了审计协议和模型，供社区复现与借鉴。

Abstract: Automated pavement-distress detection is commonly framed as a small-object problem, motivating high-resolution P2/4 detection heads and lossless downsampling. We present YOLO26-RD, an end-to-end (NMS-free) detector built on YOLO26 with two lightweight novel modules (LearnableContrast, a 494-parameter differentiable analogue of CLAHE that adapts contrast per tile inside the network, and EdgeSPD, a Sobel-gated space-to-depth downsampler adding only 2 parameters over SPD-Conv), and we subject the design to a data-first audit on a 7,618-image road-survey dataset (alligator crack, linear crack, patching). The audit falsifies the small-object premise: 92% of instances are COCO-large, and linear cracks are extreme-aspect structures (median 10:1) whose difficulty is sensitivity, not localization. Guided by this analysis, we remove the P2 detection level while retaining P2 features in the fusion path, which improves mAP50 by 2.8 points over the full YOLO26-RD model and reduces epoch time by 8%. Trained from scratch at 640x640, our best screening configuration reaches 0.787 mAP50 on the validation split versus a 0.771 project baseline (a stock YOLO26-s of uncontrolled recipe), with the largest per-class gain on the rarest class (patching, 2.6 points over baseline; 9.4 over the unmodified YOLO26-RD control under an identical recipe). A failure-mode decomposition further attributes the residual error of the bottleneck class (crack, approximately 0.74 across all architectures tested) to train/validation distribution shift on crack orientation and length, sub-pixel crack width at 640x640, and label incompleteness, factors no architecture change can address. We argue that for pavement imagery, measurement-driven subtraction outperforms module accretion, and we release our audit protocol alongside the model.

</details>


### [59] [CETalk: Continuous Valence-Arousal Control for Audio-Driven 3D Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15110)
*Peng Jia,Li Dai,Zhen Xiao,Xueliang Liu,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CETalk，一个基于连续Valence-Arousal（VA）表征的音频驱动3D面部动画框架，通过动态情感调制、多尺度时间建模和动态融合机制实现精细的情感控制和准确的唇形同步，并构建了3D-VA-MEAD大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感3D说话头生成方法依赖离散情感类别，无法捕捉情感的连续演变过程；同时忽视了音频发音与情感表达之间的时间频率不匹配问题。

Method: CETalk包含三个关键组件：（1）动态情感调制模块（Dynamic Emotion Modulation Module），利用音频派生的线索自适应地调整情感强度；（2）多尺度时间建模机制（Multi-Scale Temporal Modeling），通过并行分支解耦高频发音运动与低频情感动态；（3）动态融合机制（Dynamic Fusion Mechanism），通过自适应门控网络整合多尺度特征。此外构建了3D-VA-MEAD数据集，包含自动估计的VA标注和重建的3D面部运动。

Result: 在唇形同步准确性和情感表现力方面均优于现有最先进方法，并且能够实现平滑可控的情感过渡。

Conclusion: 基于连续VA表征的情感建模能够比离散情感类别更好地捕捉情感的动态演化，通过频率解耦的多尺度建模可以同时兼顾发音准确性与情感表现力，为情感可控的3D说话头生成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Emotional 3D talking head generation aims to synthesize expressive facial animations with accurate lip synchronization. However, existing methods often rely on discrete emotion categories, which fail to capture the continuous evolution of affect. They also overlook the temporal frequency mismatch between audio articulation and emotional expression. In this paper, we propose CETalk, an audio-driven 3D facial animation framework conditioned on continuous Valence--Arousal (VA) representations for fine-grained emotion control. CETalk predicts a sequence of FLAME parameters through three key components: a Dynamic Emotion Modulation Module that adaptively scales emotional intensity using audio-derived cues; a Multi-Scale Temporal Modeling mechanism that employs parallel branches to decouple high-frequency articulatory movements from low-frequency emotional dynamics; and a Dynamic Fusion Mechanism that integrates these multi-scale features via an adaptive gating network. To support training and evaluation, we construct 3D-VA-MEAD, a large-scale dataset with automatically estimated VA annotations and reconstructed 3D facial motions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CETalk outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both lip-sync accuracy and emotional expressiveness, while enabling smooth and controllable emotion transitions.

</details>


### [60] [CLARA: Clip-Level Multimodal Alignment with VLM-Derived Rationales for Hateful Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15905)
*Yuchen Zhang,Shuang Dai,Zeyu Fu,Yunfei Long,Ravi Shekhar,Haralambos Mouratidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLARA，一种基于片段级（clip-level）的多模态框架用于仇恨视频检测。与将视频视为单实例的传统方法不同，CLARA将视频建模为细粒度片段的序列，通过Mixture-of-Experts片段编码器实现自适应多模态对齐，利用局部-全局片段对比学习目标联合建模短期线索和长程时间依赖，并通过门控Transformer集成VLM派生的推理信息提供高层语义指导。在三个仇恨视频数据集上的实验表明，CLARA一致优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频社交媒体平台的快速增长，仇恨视频检测变得日益重要。与文本或静态多模态内容相比，仇恨视频检测仍未被充分探索且更具挑战性，因为仇恨语义往往来源于语音、音频和视觉内容等多模态线索之间的复杂交互，且这些信号通常是短暂、隐式和时间依赖的，难以通过传统的视频级表示来捕获。

Method: CLARA将视频建模为细粒度片段的序列而非单一实例，以更精确地捕获时间局部化的仇恨信号。核心组件包括：(1) Mixture-of-Experts片段编码器，用于自适应多模态对齐；(2) 局部-全局片段对比学习目标，联合建模短期线索和长程时间依赖；(3) 通过门控Transformer集成的VLM派生推理信息，提供高层语义指导。

Result: 在三个仇恨视频数据集上的大量实验表明，CLARA一致优于现有最先进方法。进一步的消融研究和参数分析验证了每个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: CLARA通过片段级建模和多种创新机制有效解决了仇恨视频检测中多模态交互复杂、信号短暂且时间依赖的难题，为仇恨视频检测提供了更精确有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Hateful video detection has become increasingly important with the rapid growth of video-centric social media platforms, given the serious risks that hate speech poses to both individual well-being and social cohesion. Compared with text or static multimodal content, hateful video detection remains underexplored and significantly more challenging, as hateful meaning often arises from complex interactions among multimodal cues, including speech, audio, and visual content. Moreover, such signals are often brief, implicit, and temporally dependent, making them difficult to capture using conventional video-level representations. In this work, we propose CLARA, a clip-level multimodal framework for hateful video detection. Instead of treating a video as a single instance, CLARA models it as a sequence of fine-grained clips, enabling more precise capture of temporally localized hateful signals. We introduce a Mixture-of-Experts clip encoder for adaptive multimodal alignment, a local-global segment contrastive objective to jointly model short-term cues and long-range temporal dependencies, and VLM-derived rationales integrated via a gated Transformer to provide high-level semantic guidance. Extensive experiments on three hateful video datasets demonstrate that CLARA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Further ablation studies and parameter analyses validate the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [61] [MetaReason: Precise Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning via Editing Meta Information for Solving Geometry Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15006)
*Penghao Yin,Haomin Wang,Qihong Tang,Xiaoye Qu,Hongjie Zhang,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaReason框架，通过将几何图像解析为结构化元信息，利用预定义工具进行可控编辑以合成高保真视觉状态，从而支持平面几何中的辅助线构造与多模态推理。同时构建了TutorGeo数据集（17k图像到元信息转换样本、60k纯文本推理轨迹、60k多模态推理轨迹）和ExamGeo基准，结合监督微调与强化学习训练模型，实验表明该方法显著优于现有开源模型，并与专有模型性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在复杂几何任务中过度依赖纯文本推理，而引入中间视觉状态的方法常因几何表示不准确和渲染保真度低而产生不可靠输出。作者旨在解决这些限制，通过结构化元信息实现准确的辅助线构造与多模态推理。

Method: MetaReason框架包含三个阶段：(1) 将几何图像解析为结构化元信息；(2) 使用预定义工具对元信息进行可控编辑，合成高保真的中间视觉状态（如辅助线添加后的图像）；(3) 基于增强后的视觉状态进行推理。模型通过TutorGeo数据集上的监督微调与强化学习联合训练，并利用ExamGeo基准进行系统评估。

Result: 实验结果表明MetaReason显著优于现有开源视觉语言模型，并且在性能上与传统专有（闭源）模型相当，验证了结构化元信息驱动的多模态推理在平面几何任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化元信息解析和高保真视觉状态合成，MetaReason有效克服了现有方法中几何表示不准确与渲染低保真的问题，为平面几何多模态推理提供了一条可行路径。TutorGeo数据集和ExamGeo基准为后续研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Although visual reasoning is crucial for solving complex geometry tasks, existing vision-language models rely heavily on text-only reasoning. Some recent methods introduce intermediate visual states to facilitate reasoning, but they are often hindered by inaccurate geometric representations and low rendering fidelity, ultimately leading to unreliable outputs. To address these limitations, we propose MetaReason, a framework for multimodal reasoning in plane geometry that leverages structured meta-information to enable accurate auxiliary-line construction. The framework first parses geometric images into meta-information, performs controllable edits with predefined tools to synthesize high-fidelity visual states, and then conducts reasoning based on these augmented views. To support this framework, we construct TutorGeo, a comprehensive dataset containing 17k image-to-meta conversion samples, 60k text-only reasoning traces, and 60k interleaved multimodal reasoning traces. Using this dataset, we combine supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning to develop robust multimodal reasoning capabilities. We also introduce ExamGeo, a benchmark derived from real-world examination problems that enables systematic evaluation across varying difficulty levels. Experimental results demonstrate that MetaReason significantly outperforms existing open-source models and achieves competitive performance against proprietary models.

</details>


### [62] [Binarized High-Efficiency RAW Video Restoration and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16756)
*Tianyu Zhu,Ying Fu,Hesong Li,Gengchen Zhang,Xin Yuan,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BinRVR，一个二值化的RAW视频恢复框架，通过引入二值化信息交互模块（BIIM）和分布感知二值化卷积（DAB-Conv），在减少约96%计算量和参数的情况下仅损失约4%的性能，并支持多比特量化以实现硬件约束下的灵活性权衡。实验证明其在低光增强、去噪、去模糊和超分辨率等RAW视频恢复任务上达到先进水平，并探索了在下游视频应用（目标检测和单目深度估计）中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管二值神经网络（BNN）能够实现图像增强的高效轻量级部署，但它们在建模时间一致性和激活值分布方面存在不足，限制了其在视频场景中的有效性。此外，RAW视频恢复是高质量低级感知的基础，亟需一种既能高效部署又能保持良好性能的二值化方法。

Method: BinRVR框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 二值化信息交互模块（BIIM），以高效统一的方式联合建模空间和时间信息；(2) 分布感知二值化卷积（DAB-Conv），利用全精度激活的统计信息来缓解量化误差。该框架还支持多比特量化，可在不同硬件约束下实现精度与效率的灵活权衡。

Result: 在RAW视频恢复任务（包括低光增强、去噪、去模糊和超分辨率）上，BinRVR相比最先进的二值化方法取得了有竞争力的性能，计算量和参数减少约96%，性能仅损失约4%。此外，在下游视频应用（目标检测和单目深度估计）中也验证了方法的潜力。

Conclusion: BinRVR有效解决了二值神经网络在视频场景中建模时序一致性和处理激活值分布的问题，在保持极低计算开销的同时实现了有竞争力的RAW视频恢复性能，且支持多比特量化以满足不同硬件部署需求，为高效视频低级视觉处理提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: RAW video restoration is fundamental to high-quality low-level perception and serves as the basis for a wide range of downstream vision applications. While binary neural networks (BNNs) enable efficient lightweight deployment for image enhancement, their deficiencies in modeling temporal coherence and activation value distributions hinder their effectiveness when applied to video scenarios. In this paper, we propose BinRVR, a binarized RAW video restoration framework that reduces computation and parameters by approximately 96% while incurring only about 4% performance degradation. Specifically, we present a Binarized Information Interaction Module (BIIM) to jointly model spatial and temporal information in an efficient and unified manner. Moreover, we develop a Distribution-Aware Binarized Convolution (DAB-Conv) that leverages the statistics of full-precision activations to mitigate quantization errors. The proposed framework further supports multi-bit quantization, enabling flexible accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across different hardware constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our BinRVR achieves competitive performance compared with state-of-the-art binarized methods on RAW video restoration tasks, including low-light enhancement, denoising, deblurring, and super-resolution. We further explore the potential of our method on downstream video applications, including object detection and monocular depth estimation.

</details>


### [63] [Geometry-Aware Spatio-Temporal Context Modeling for 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15279)
*Sitao Chen,Zhuangwei Zhuang,Hui Luo,Qingyao Wu,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAST（Geometry-Aware Spatio-Temporal）方法，用于4D占用预测。该方法通过渐进式显式-隐式生成和双路径时空建模，解决了现有方法在静态结构几何失真和时间一致性方面的问题，在Occ3D-nuScenes上以2.84倍速度提升超越了现有最先进方法（mIoU提升7.67%，IoU提升6.44%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D占用预测对自动驾驶至关重要（尤其用于边角案例仿真），但现有基于离散token化和自回归预测的方法存在两个关键缺陷：静态结构存在几何畸变，以及预测时间范围内的时间一致性不足。

Method: GAST方法由两个核心模块组成：（1）生成模块通过位姿驱动变形（pose-driven warping）、运动感知特征调制（motion-aware feature modulation）和基于注意力的特征细化（attention-based feature refinement）逐帧生成具有高几何保真度和语义合理性的占用结果；（2）时空模块通过全局上下文聚合增强空间一致性，并通过时态动态提取捕捉场景演化。该设计实现了历史重建与未来预测的端到端联合优化。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes数据集上的大量实验表明，GAST在mIoU上超越最先进方法7.67%，在IoU上超越6.44%，同时实现2.84倍的推理加速，并在长期预测任务中保持强劲性能。

Conclusion: GAST通过将渐进式显式-隐式生成与双路径时空建模相结合，有效解决了4D占用预测中的几何变形和时间不一致问题，为自动驾驶中的边角案例仿真提供了更准确高效的解决方案，并具有长期预测的鲁棒性。

Abstract: 4D occupancy forecasting models the spatio-temporal evolution of 3D scenes and is crucial for autonomous driving, especially for corner-case simulation. Existing methods often rely on discrete tokenization followed by autoregressive prediction, yet struggle with geometric distortion in static structures and inconsistent temporal coherence over the forecasting horizon. In this work, we propose a Geometry-Aware Spatio-Temporal context modeling method (GAST) for 4D occupancy forecasting, built upon progressive explicit-implicit generation and dual-path spatio-temporal modeling. Specifically, the generation module produces per-frame occupancy with high geometric fidelity and semantic plausibility through pose-driven warping, motion-aware feature modulation, and attention-based feature refinement. Subsequently, the spatio-temporal module enhances spatial consistency through global context aggregation while capturing scene evolution through temporal dynamics extraction. This unified design enables joint optimization of historical reconstruction and future forecasting in an end-to-end manner. Extensive experiments on Occ3D-nuScenes demonstrate the superiority of our method, outperforming the state-of-the-art by 7.67% in mIoU and 6.44% in IoU with a 2.84x speedup, while maintaining strong performance in long-term forecasting.

</details>


### [64] [HistReNeRF: Historic Image Relocalisation within Contemporary Neural Radiance Field Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15420)
*Benjamin T. Hughes,Stuart James*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HistReNeRF提出一种利用NeRF作为场景接口、通过DINOv2特征匹配将历史照片重定位到当代3D场景中的6自由度位姿估计框架。在包含10,545张当代街景图像和230张历史照片的跨时间数据集上，嵌入空间域适应相比像素空间方法平均降低了11%的平移误差和16%的旋转误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史照片在当代场景模型中的重定位具有挑战性，因为历史与现代视角在摄影外观、可见物体和空间布局上存在显著差异。现有方法难以直接处理这种跨时间的领域偏移，需要一种能够在特征空间中进行域适应的方法。

Method: HistReNeRF首先利用NeRF对当代场景进行连续隐式重建，提供可查询的场景接口。然后使用域适应的DINOv2 patch特征，将历史照片的特征与从NeRF中采样的候选射线的特征进行匹配，从而估计6自由度位姿。该方法直接在用于定位的特征表示中实现历史摄影与当代图像之间的域适应，而非在像素空间操作。

Result: 在包含10,545张当代街景图像和230张来自三个欧洲地标的历史照片的跨时间数据集上，嵌入空间域适应相较于像素空间方法平均降低了11%的平移误差和16%的旋转误差，验证了特征空间域适应的有效性。

Conclusion: 神经场景重定位为特征空间的域适应提供了自然接口，能够有效减少跨时间外观偏移，且无需修改查询图像本身。该方法证明了NeRF作为场景表示在跨时间视觉定位中的潜力。

Abstract: Relocalising archival photographs within a contemporary scene model is challenging because historic and modern views can differ in photographic appearance, visible objects, and spatial layout. Therefore, we present HistReNeRF, a framework that estimates the 6-DoF pose of a historic photograph by matching adapted DINOv2 patch features to candidate rays sampled from a contemporary Neural Radiance Field (NeRF) reconstruction. The continuous representation of a NeRF provides a queryable scene interface from which candidate rays can be sampled and matched, enabling domain adaptation between historic photography and contemporary images directly in the feature representation used for localisation. We evaluate embedding-space-based domain adaptation against pixel-space methods on a new cross-temporal dataset comprising 10,545 contemporary street-level images and 230 archival photographs from three European landmarks. Embedding-space adaptation reduces translation and rotation errors by an average of 11% and 16%, respectively, across the three scenes. These results show that neural scene relocalisation provides a natural interface for feature-space adaptation, reducing cross-temporal appearance shift without modifying the query image. Code and dataset at https://github.com/ARTUROLab/HistReNeRF.

</details>


### [65] [Turning spectra into images improves plant trait retrieval with 2D-CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16661)
*Javier Lopatin,Teja Kattenborn,Eya Cherif,Sebastián Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探索将一维高光谱反射光谱转换为二维图像表示是否能够改善多性状预测。在GreenHyperSpectra数据集上，最简单的Reshape变换结合EfficientNet-B0（R²=0.684）超越了1D CNN基线（R²=0.587）。2D掩码自编码器（MAE-2D）的线性探测达到R²=0.646，超过所有1D自监督方法。可解释性分析表明，蛋白质和叶片水分预测与PROSAIL辐射传输模型模拟的波段重要性一致，而类胡萝卜素和叶面积指数则不一致。增益主要来自2D光谱图像的表征优势，而非架构复杂度或ImageNet预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱反射光谱学能够非破坏性地估算植物功能性状，但现有深度学习方法将光谱处理为一维序列，限制了其对长距离波段间依赖关系的捕捉能力。作者提出疑问：将一维光谱转换为二维图像表示是否能改善CNN的多性状预测性能。

Method: 在GreenHyperSpectra数据集（7,897个标注光谱、八个性状、400-2450 nm）上，比较了九种光谱到二维图像的变换方法，使用EfficientNet-B0从零训练，与已发表的1D CNN结果对比。随后在139,000个未标注光谱图像上预训练2D掩码自编码器（MAE-2D），并通过线性探测（冻结编码器、仅训练MLP头）评估。最后使用Integrated Gradients和Grad-CAM解释模型预测，将波段重要性映射回光谱轴，与PROSAIL辐射传输模型模拟的敏感性进行比较。

Result: 最简单的Reshape变换性能最佳（R²=0.684±0.001），较1D SOTA基线提高了0.097。MAE-2D线性探测达到R²=0.646，超过包括微调MAE-1D（R²=0.641）在内的所有1D自监督方法。跨数据集评估中所有模型性能大幅下降，无模型显著超越1D基线。可解释性方面，蛋白质（r=0.45）和叶片水分（r=0.33）的波段重要性排序与PROSAIL模拟一致，而类胡萝卜素（r=0.06）和叶面积指数（r=-0.11）不一致。

Conclusion: 2D光谱图像表示的优势（而非架构复杂度或ImageNet预训练）是超越1D方法的关键驱动力。对于具有尖锐吸收特征（如蛋白质和叶片水分）的植物化学性状，模型确实读取了已确立的叶片化学信息；但对于无尖锐特征或冠层结构性状（如类胡萝卜素和叶面积指数），模型未能捕捉到相同的物理化学信号。跨数据集泛化仍是挑战。

Abstract: Hyperspectral reflectance spectroscopy enables non-destructive estimation of plant functional traits, yet current deep learning approaches process spectra as one-dimensional sequences, which limits how they capture long-range inter-band dependencies. We asked whether transforming 1D spectra into 2D image representations improves multi-trait prediction with convolutional neural networks (CNN). We compared nine transformations using EfficientNet-B0 on the GreenHyperSpectra dataset (7,897 labeled spectra, eight traits, 400-2450 nm), benchmarked against published 1D CNN results on the same split. Trained from scratch, the simplest transformation, a direct Reshape of the spectrum into a 2D grid, performed best ($R^2 = 0.684 \pm 0.001$) and improved on the state-of-the-art 1D baseline ($R^2 = 0.587$, $+0.097$). We then pretrained a 2D masked autoencoder (MAE-2D) on 139,000 unlabeled spectral images. Linear probing, which freezes the encoder and trains only a multilayer perceptron head, reached $R^2 = 0.646$ and exceeded every 1D self-supervised counterpart, including the fine-tuned MAE-1D ($R^2 = 0.641$). Under cross-dataset evaluation all models lost most of their accuracy and none beat the 1D baseline significantly. To identify which wavelengths drive each prediction, we applied Integrated Gradients and Grad-CAM and unfolded band importance back to the spectral axis. Protein ($r = 0.45$) and leaf water ($r = 0.33$) agreed with sensitivities simulated by the PROSAIL radiative-transfer model, while carotenoids ($r = 0.06$) and leaf area index ($r = -0.11$) did not, showing that the model reads established leaf chemistry for traits with sharp absorption features. The representational advantage of 2D spectral images, rather than architectural complexity or ImageNet pretraining, drives the gain over 1D approaches.

</details>


### [66] [X$^2$Localizer: Cross-grained Alignment for Progressive Cross-view Video Geo-localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16658)
*Zichao Zeng,Weijia Fan,Yufan Chen,June Moh Goo,Junwei Zheng,Ruiping Liu,Kunyu Peng,Jiaming Zhang,Rainer Stiefelhagen,Jan Boehm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式跨视角视频地理定位（PCVG）这一部署导向的任务扩展与评估协议，并引入X²Localizer（跨粒度对齐框架）与滑动窗口重定位（SWRL）策略，实现在变化的时间预算、前缀推断、随机起点和长距离中断等实际场景下的渐进式定位，同时保持甚至小幅提升传统全视频性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角视频地理定位（CVG）方法依赖固定长度输入和事后精炼，无法适应部分或动态观测下的在线定位需求，在真实部署场景中（如时间预算变化、前缀推断、随机起点、长距离中断）存在明显局限。

Method: 1) 提出PCVG任务设定与评估协议，涵盖可变时间预算、前缀推断、随机起点评估和带中断的长距离定位；2) 引入X²Localizer，采用跨粒度对齐框架，联合监督全局前缀-航拍检索与token聚合的帧-航拍瓦片匹配，并使用预算依赖的非对称目标函数；3) 提出滑动窗口重定位（SWRL）策略，动态刷新候选区域以实现失败恢复和长距离部署，无需全序列重新处理。

Result: X²Localizer在传统全视频性能上保持并发微小提升（Recall@1 +0.1，Recall@10 +0.3），同时在早期定位上显著改善；在最具挑战性的单帧设置下，粗粒度检索相比先前SOTA提升Recall@1 +4.7、Recall@10 +11.5；结合SWRL后，在随机起点和长距离场景下实现稳健的渐进式定位。

Conclusion: 本文通过PCVG协议和X²Localizer+SWRL方案，缩小了基准评测与实际部署之间的差距，验证了渐进式跨视角视频地理定位在真实世界场景中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Cross-view Video Geo-localization (CVG) aims to localize ground-view videos by retrieving their corresponding geo-tagged aerial images. However, CVG approaches rely on fixed-length inputs and post-hoc refinement, hindering online-oriented localization under partial or dynamic observations. In this work, we formulate Progressive Cross-view Video Geo-localization (PCVG) as a deployment-oriented extension and evaluation protocol of CVG, enabling localization under varying temporal budgets, prefix-based inference, random-start evaluation, and long-range localization with interruptions. To explore PCVG, we introduce X$^2$Localizer, a cross-grained alignment framework that jointly supervises global prefix-to-aerial retrieval and token-aggregated frame--aerial-tile matching with a budget-dependent asymmetric objective. Furthermore, we introduce a Sliding-Window Re-Localization (SWRL) strategy that dynamically refreshes candidate regions for failure recovery and long-range deployment without full-sequence reprocessing. Extensive experiments show that X$^2$Localizer preserves conventional full-video performance, with marginal gains of +0.1 Recall@1 and +0.3 Recall@10, while substantially improving early localization. In the challenging single-frame setting, X$^2$Localizer improves coarse retrieval by +4.7 Recall@1 and +11.5 Recall@10 over the previous state-of-the-art method. With SWRL, our approach further enables robust progressive localization under random-start and long-distance scenarios, narrowing the gap between benchmark evaluation and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [67] [Catching Hallucinated Citations in Video-LLM Question Answering: A Self-Verification Pipeline and Verifier Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15574)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向视频问答系统的幻觉检测与修正管道。该管道由检索增强语言模型生成带时间戳引用的答案，并在展示给用户前对每个被引用的帧进行独立复核。直接询问视觉模型（因谄媚效应完全失效，0%捕获率）、盲重述加通用LLM裁判（不稳定，0%-100%波动）等方法均不可靠，最终采用小型自然语言推理（NLI）模型作为验证器，在对抗性虚假前提问题上捕获79%的捏造说法，且不影响真实说法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉语言模型的视频问答系统常以高置信度输出带时间戳的声明，但这些声明可能并未被所引用的帧所支持。时间戳给人一种"已接地"（grounding）的假象，提升了用户信任却未提升准确性，形成一种具有欺骗性的幻觉。作者旨在构建一个管道来闭环检测并过滤这类幻觉。

Method: 管道包括：(1) 检索增强语言模型起草答案并为每条声明附带时间戳帧引用；(2) 每个被引用帧在展示给用户之前被独立重新检验。作者对比了基线方法，并消融了三种验证设计方案：直接询问视觉模型该帧是否支持声明、盲重述帧内容配合通用LLM裁判、以及用小型自然语言推理（NLI）模型替代LLM裁判。实现同时适配Apple Silicon (MLX)和Google Colab (HF Transformers, CUDA)。
