Result: 直接询问视觉模型（由于谄媚效应）在40条声明上捕获率为0%，完全失败；盲重述加通用LLM裁判虽有改进但极其不稳定，根据提示措辞在被标记率0%到100%之间振荡；用小型NLI模型替换裁判后，得到稳定且可解释的验证器，在对抗性虚假前提问题上捕获79%的捏造声明，且对真实声明零误伤。

Conclusion: 研究发现大型视觉语言模型存在严重的谄媚问题，使得自评式验证不可靠；通用LLM裁判受提示敏感性影响过大；小型NLI模型提供了稳定、可解释、高效的验证方案，能够有效捕获大量捏造声明而不损害真实声明。完整管道、评测框架和双平台实现已开源。

Abstract: Video question answering systems built on vision-language models often produce timestamped claims with high confidence even when unsupported by the cited frame. This deceptive hallucination arises because timestamps imply grounding without ensuring correctness, increasing user trust but not accuracy. We introduce a pipeline that closes this loop. A retrieval-augmented language model drafts answers with per-claim timestamp citations, and each cited frame is independently re-examined before being shown to the user. We compare against a plain baseline and ablate three verification designs, evaluated on both Apple Silicon (MLX) and Google Colab (HF Transformers, CUDA). Directly asking the vision model whether a frame supports a claim fails completely (0% catch rate on 40 claims) due to sycophancy. Blind re-captioning plus a general LLM judge improves results but is unstable, oscillating between 0% and 100% flagged depending on prompt phrasing. Replacing that judge with a small natural language inference model yields a stable, interpretable verifier that catches 79% of fabricated claims on adversarial false-premise questions while leaving true claims untouched. We release the full pipeline, evaluation harness, and implementations for both Apple Silicon and Colab. Code is available at https://github.com/yogesh-iitj/grounded-video-qa.

</details>


### [68] [ENAF: A Multi-Exit Network with an Adaptive Patch Fusion for Large Image Super Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15349)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Tuan Nghia Nguyen,Xuan Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ENAF，一种用于单图像超分辨率的动态网络，通过自适应补丁融合和多个提前退出机制，利用一个小型PSNR估计网络来关联图像纹理与计算成本，从而优化质量-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大图超分辨率动态网络方法依赖手工设计的难度评估指标（如图像边缘），这些指标与SISR模型的实际计算复杂度关联较弱，导致执行路径分配不够准确。

Method: ENAF基于主干网络构建，集成多个提前退出（EEs）以处理过度参数化的SISR模型；引入一个小型网络估计PSNR，将数据纹理与EE处的计算成本相关联，并根据得分将图像补丁有效分配到不同退出点，同时采用自适应补丁融合。

Result: 在常用数据集和流行的SISR骨架上进行的大量实验表明，ENAF在各种设置下均能有效工作，改善了质量-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: ENAF通过数据驱动的PSNR估计替代手工设计的复杂度评估，实现了更优的超分辨率质量与计算复杂度的平衡，并开源了代码。

Abstract: To accelerate single image super-resolution (SISR) networks on large images (2K-8K), many recent approaches decompose an image into small patches and dynamically determine an execution path according to its difficulty (referred to as a dynamic network). To quantify the hardness of a patch, they mainly rely on a handcrafted assessment score, e.g., edge, which weakly associates a patch's texture with the computational complexity of a SISR model. To address the problem, we introduce ENAF - a dynamic network for SISR with an adaptive patch fusion. Built on top of a backbone, ENAF incorporates multiple early exits (EEs) to tackle the over-parameterized SISR model. More importantly, ENAF plugs a tiny network that estimates PSNR to associate data texture with a computation cost at an EE. Based on the scores, ENAF effectively assigns image patches to an exit, enhancing the quality-complexity trade-off. Extensive experiments on common datasets with popular SISR backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of ENAF in various settings. The source code is provided in https://github.com/nmduonggg/ENAF

</details>


### [69] [Counterfactual Sensitivity Is Not Repairability: Auditing Replay Probes for Video Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15685)
*Rama AlHamidi,Rasul Khanbayov,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARVE是一种黑盒反事实探针，通过对比SHAM（匹配的假重放）和DESTROY（销毁检索帧语义内容）两种重放下答案的变化，来评估工具使用视频代理是否真正依赖检索到的视觉证据。在冻结的VideoExplorer风格代理上，DESTROY改变答案的频率比SHAM高29.3个百分点，作为路由信号能提升3.26个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用的视频代理在回答前会检索视觉证据，但最终答案并未被强制依赖检索到的内容。当前缺乏有效的黑盒测试来判断代理是否真正利用了检索到的帧，因此需要一种反事实探针来检验检索证据与最终答案之间的因果依赖关系。

Method: CARVE采用反事实黑盒探针方法：首先让代理检索帧，然后进行匹配的SHAM重放（在原帧上重新执行相同流程）和DESTROY重放（销毁检索帧的语义内容），比较两种条件下答案的变化率。同时探索不同重放预算k（k=3与k=10）的影响，并在LVBench数据集上评估其作为路由信号的效果。

Result: 在冻结的VideoExplorer风格代理上，三次k=3独立运行中，DESTROY比SHAM改变答案的频率高29.3个百分点，具有大而可复现的聚合效应。CARVE从1,258个LVBench问题中选出538个，准确率提升3.26点，且高于大多数匹配的随机子集的回退产出。但问题级分数稳定性较差，k=10虽减少平局却削弱了原始零阈值路由策略。CARVE分数与标注的时间覆盖度仅弱相关。

Conclusion: CARVE应被理解为一种路由信号而非直接的接地（grounding）分类器。其在聚合层面能可靠检测代理对检索证据的依赖，但问题级稳定性有限，需要在实际应用中权衡重放预算与路由策略的选择。

Abstract: Tool-using video agents retrieve visual evidence before answering, but the final answer is not forced to depend on what was retrieved. The natural black box test is counterfactual: destroy the semantic content of the frames the agent retrieved and check whether the answer changes, against a matched sham that re-executes the identical pipeline on those same frames. We introduce CARVE, a black-box counterfactual probe that compares answer changes under matched SHAM and DESTROY replays. Across three independent k=3 runs on a frozen VideoExplorer-style agent, DESTROY changes the answer 29.3 percentage points more often than SHAM, yielding a large and reproducible aggregate effect. Question-level scores are less stable, and increasing the replay budget from k=3 to k=10 reduces ties but weakens the original zero-threshold routing policy. At k=3, CARVE selects 538 of 1,258 LVBench questions and improves accuracy by 3.26 points, with higher fallback yield than most matched random subsets. The score shows only a weak association with annotated temporal coverage, so CARVE is best understood as a routing signal rather than a direct grounding classifier. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/CARVE.

</details>


### [70] [Fast Test-Time Refinement for Robust Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15113)
*Jiaming Liang,Chi-Man Pun,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对学习型图像压缩（LIC）系统的对抗鲁棒性问题，提出了一种快速测试时精炼（FTTR）框架。研究发现LIC系统中存在“非对称对抗轨迹”（AAT）性质：从对抗区域回到良性区域比反向过程容易得多，通常仅需1-2步即可大致恢复。基于该性质，作者提出了FTTR框架，并建立了两维管道模型来解释该现象，证明鲁棒性来源于输入作为标签（Input-as-Label）性质引起的对抗区域收缩，而非梯度混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型图像压缩（LIC）在良性环境下表现优异，但深度神经网络带来的高表征能力使其容易受到对抗攻击，阻碍了其作为可信标准化编解码器的应用。虽然已有的测试时精炼（TTR）方法在灰盒场景中可作防御，但其迭代开销巨大、缺乏理论理解，且未在白盒设置或ℓ2边界之外的攻击（如速率目标、非目标失真）下验证。这些空白促使作者进行系统性研究并提出更高效的防御方案。

Method: 1) 系统性研究LIC系统的对抗行为，揭示非对称对抗轨迹（AAT）性质：从对抗到良性区域的过渡比反向过程容易得多，对抗样本通常1-2步即可大致恢复；2) 提出两维管道模型（Tube Model）从理论上解释AAT现象；3) 基于AAT提出快速测试时精炼（FTTR）框架；4) 通过输入作为标签（Input-as-Label）性质论证鲁棒性来源是对抗区域的收缩而非梯度混淆。

Result: 在多种LIC系统上，经过各类强自适应攻击的广泛评估，FTTR框架展现了良好的鲁棒性，验证了理论分析的正确性。相比传统TTR方法，FTTR显著降低了迭代开销，仅需1-2步即可实现对抗样本的大致恢复，同时保持防御效果。

Conclusion: 作者提出了基于AAT性质的FTTR框架，为LIC系统提供了高效且可解释的对抗防御方案。理论模型和实验验证了该方法的有效性，明确了鲁棒性机理（对抗区域收缩），为LIC系统的安全部署提供了实用且可信的解决方案。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has demonstrated remarkable rate-distortion (RD) performance in benign settings. However, the high representational capacity endowed by deep neural networks (DNNs) comes at the expense of increased adversarial vulnerability. This hinders their adoption as trusted standardized codecs. Recent work has sketched test-time refinement (TTR) as a defense in gray-box scenarios, despite its original purpose of improving benign RD performance. Unfortunately, extensive iterations of TTR incur prohibitive overhead, while the robustness mechanism lacks theoretical understanding. Moreover, TTR has not been evaluated in white-box settings or against attacks beyond $\ell_2$-bounded rate and untargeted distortion objectives. To bridge these gaps, we present a systematic study. Our study reveals an Asymmetric Adversarial Trajectory (AAT) property in LIC systems: transitioning from adversarial to benign regions is significantly easier than the reverse process, where adversarial examples can often be roughly recovered within only 1-2 steps. We provide a two-dimensional Tube Model to explain this phenomenon. Based on AAT, we propose a Fast Test-Time Refinement (FTTR) framework for practical and robust LIC systems. We establish that the robustness arises from the contraction of adversarial regions induced by the Input-as-Label property of LIC systems, rather than from obfuscated gradients. Extensive evaluations with diverse strong adaptive attacks across multiple LIC systems demonstrate the promise of the proposed FTTR framework. The code is available at https://github.com/chinaliangjiaming/FTTR.git.

</details>


### [71] [Gaussian-JEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning for 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15651)
*Bin Ren,Qi Ma,Yue Li,Zongyan Han,Yidi Li,Yuqian Fu,Rao Muhammad Anwer,Theo Gevers,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaussian-JEPA，一种基于潜在预测的自监督方法，用于学习3D高斯泼溅的通用表征。不同于现有重建式自监督方法（其监督与单一采样的原始实现绑定），Gaussian-JEPA通过在线编码器处理可见上下文，并用共享EMA编码器提供多尺度目标特征，在特征空间中预测被遮蔽的高斯token块表征，而无需重建高斯属性。实验表明其在高斯重采样、部分观测、可渲染形状补全、分割与分类迁移中均优于匹配的重建式预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算的编码器消费高斯资产的采样观测，导致同一物体可能通过不同的原始实现（primitive realizations）被观测。现有自监督方法主要重建被遮蔽的高斯属性，这使监督被绑定到单一采样实现，且需要输入空间解码器。潜在预测作为替代方案虽有潜力，但应用于高斯token时需要能兼容耦合属性和异构空间支持的目标，这是之前未被解决的问题。

Method: Gaussian-JEPA采用联合嵌入预测架构：在线编码器处理可见高斯token上下文，共享的指数移动平均（EMA）编码器为被遮蔽的token块提供停止梯度（stop-gradient）的多尺度特征目标。使用互补的目标投影和特征空间接地（feature-space grounding）提供潜在监督，无需重建高斯属性即可训练。

Result: 在多个评测任务上（高斯重采样一致性、部分观测下的实例信息保留、可渲染形状补全，以及部分分割和物体分类迁移），Gaussian-JEPA相比匹配的重建式预训练方法表现更优：对重采样输入更具一致性，部分观测下保留更多实例信息，并为高斯补全提供更强的冻结特征。

Conclusion: 结果表明潜在预测是学习可复用3D高斯表征的有效目标函数，为3DGS基础模型的自监督预训练提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) represents 3D content with anisotropic primitives that jointly encode geometry and appearance. Fixed-budget encoders consume sampled observations of Gaussian assets, so the same object may be observed through different primitive realizations. Existing self-supervised methods mainly reconstruct masked Gaussian attributes, tying supervision to one sampled realization and requiring an input-space decoder. Latent prediction offers an alternative, but its application to Gaussian tokens requires targets that accommodate coupled attributes and heterogeneous spatial support. We introduce Gaussian-JEPA, which predicts representations of held-out Gaussian token blocks from visible context. An online encoder processes the context, while a shared exponential-moving-average encoder supplies stop-gradient features for multi-scale targets. Complementary target projections and feature-space grounding provide latent supervision without reconstructing Gaussian attributes. We evaluate the features under Gaussian resampling, partial observations, and renderable shape completion, together with transfer to part segmentation and object classification. Compared with matched reconstruction pretraining, Gaussian-JEPA is more consistent across resampled inputs, retains more instance information under partial observations, and provides stronger frozen features for Gaussian completion. These results support latent prediction as an effective objective for reusable 3D Gaussian representations. Code is on the project page (https://amazingren.github.io/Gaussian-JEPA/).

</details>


### [72] [Generation of Synthetic Fingerphotos with GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15029)
*Conor Miller-Lynch,Sandip Purnapatra,Syed Konain Abbas,Lambert Igene,Faraz Hussain,Soumyabrata Dey,Stephanie Schuckers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并使用StyleGAN2-ADA和StyleGAN3两种现有图像生成架构来合成接触式（无接触）指纹照片，通过对比真实与合成指纹的生物特征统计、计算匹配分数等指标，评估了合成指纹在真实感、隐私保护和多样性方面的表现，并公开了评估代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于接触式指纹数据量有限，且分享真实个体指纹存在安全风险，因此需要探索能够替代或补充真实数据的合成指纹生成方法，用于开发和评估接触式指纹识别系统。

Method: 使用StyleGAN2-ADA和StyleGAN3生成合成指纹照片；通过三方面进行评估：(1) 比较合成指纹与真实指纹的生物特征统计以评估真实感；(2) 计算真实与合成指纹之间的匹配分数以评估隐私保护；(3) 计算不同合成指纹之间的匹配分数以评估样本多样性。

Result: 论文提供了合成指纹在真实感、隐私保护和多样性三个维度上的定量评估结果，为未来合成指纹评估提供了可比的基准。

Conclusion: 基于GAN的合成指纹生成方法（StyleGAN2-ADA和StyleGAN3）能够生成具有足够真实感、良好隐私保护性和一定多样性的合成指纹照片，可作为真实接触式指纹数据的替代或补充，同时作者开源了评估代码以便未来研究与对比。

Abstract: Contactless fingerprinting is an emerging approach to biometric authentication that allows users to scan their fingerprints without touching a scanner. Due to the limited amount of contactless fingerprint data available and the security risks associated with sharing real individuals' fingerprints, it is valuable to explore methods of generating synthetic data that can be used in place of - or in conjunction with - real data to develop and evaluate contactless fingerprinting systems. In this paper, we present and evaluate synthetic fingerphotos generated using StyleGAN2-ADA and StyleGAN3, existing image generation architectures. We evaluate the realism, privacy preservation, and variety of the synthetic fingerphotos by comparing their biometric feature statistics to those of real fingerphotos, computing match scores between real and synthetic fingerphotos, and computing match scores between different synthetic fingerphotos. This paper provides a quantitative comparison point for future evaluations of synthetic fingerphotos. The evaluation code is made available at https://github.com/cmillerlynch/fingerphoto-gan.

</details>


### [73] [HarmTrace: Anchor-Calibrated Decoupled Optimization for Fine-Grained Target Identification in Harmful Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16622)
*Yujia Li,Yiqun Zhang,Zihan Cheng,Yijie Huang,Tenglong Ye,Zihan Wang,Xiaocui Yang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将多模态有害模因检测从单纯的危害性分类扩展为细粒度的目标识别任务（目标类型、目标实体、文本提及、视觉区域），并引入新数据集Mem3W和严格指标JRA。作者提出HarmTrace框架，通过实体感知的SFT和条件目标识别策略优化（CTPO）实现危害性与目标识别解耦优化，显著提升JRA（从17.58%提升到52.51%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有害模因检测仅关注危害性二分类，模型可能正确预测危害性但错误识别被攻击的目标或其支撑证据，缺乏对"攻击谁、在哪里被攻击"的细粒度理解。这导致模型决策的可解释性和可靠性不足，需要将任务扩展为更细致的目标识别。

Method: 1) 提出Mem3W数据集，统一多个公开有害模因数据集并为有害实例提供人工验证的细粒度标注（目标类别、目标实体、文本提及、视觉区域）。2) 引入联合记录准确率（JRA）严格指标。3) 提出HarmTrace框架：首先通过实体感知的监督微调强化目标实体监督；然后应用条件目标识别策略优化（CTPO），解耦危害性与目标识别优势，仅对标签正确且有害的样本进行目标识别优化；CTPO采用虚拟正锚点（VPA）作为目标识别优势归一化的完全正确参考。

Result: 在多个代表性多模态大语言模型上评估发现危害性准确率与JRA之间存在显著差距。HarmTrace在各评估主干上同时提升了JRA和危害性准确率，其中在Qwen3-VL-8B主干上JRA从17.58%提升至52.51%。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 细粒度的目标识别对于多模态有害模因检测至关重要，仅优化危害性分类是不够的。HarmTrace通过实体感知微调与条件策略优化的解耦设计，有效弥合了危害性准确率与联合目标识别精度之间的差距，展示了在有害模因细粒度理解上的显著潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal harmful meme detection is typically formulated as image--text harmfulness classification. A model may correctly predict harmfulness while misidentifying the attacked target or its supporting evidence. We therefore extend harmful meme detection with fine-grained target identification, asking what type of target is attacked, who is targeted, and where the target appears in the meme. The model predicts harmfulness for every meme and, for harmful memes, outputs the target category, target entity, textual mention, and visual region. To support this task, we introduce Meme3W, which unifies multiple public harmful meme datasets and provides human-verified annotations for harmful instances. We further introduce Joint Record Accuracy (JRA), a strict record-level metric requiring the harmfulness label and all target-identification fields to be jointly correct. Experiments with representative multimodal large language models reveal a substantial gap between harmfulness accuracy and JRA. To narrow this gap, we propose HarmTrace, an anchor-calibrated decoupled optimization framework. HarmTrace strengthens target-entity supervision through entity-aware supervised fine-tuning. It then applies Conditional Target-identification Policy Optimization (CTPO) to decouple harmfulness and target-identification advantages, restricting target-identification optimization to label-correct responses for harmful examples. CTPO uses a Virtual Positive Anchor (VPA) as a fully correct reference for target-identification advantage normalization. HarmTrace improves both JRA and harmfulness accuracy across the evaluated backbones, with JRA on the Qwen3-VL-8B backbone increasing from 17.58\% to 52.51\%. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/llly1234/HarmTrace-for-Harmful-Memes.

</details>


### [74] [On the Adversarial Robustness of Remote Sensing Semantic Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15267)
*Weikang Yu,Yonghao Xu,Pedram Ghamisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对语义变化检测（SCD）任务的任务定制鲁棒性评估框架，将输出端攻击目标与输入端时间扰动访问分离，系统分析组件漏洞和跨时间传播。在四个数据集和六种代表性模型上的实验表明，即便二值变化定位相对稳定，最终语义变化预测仍可被严重破坏，且单时间戳的扰动/攻击目标可传播到另一时间戳的预测；跨模型迁移明显弱于白盒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCD是双时相密集预测任务，同时涉及时间戳级语义预测、变化定位和最终语义变化解码，形成复杂的对抗依赖关系，而传统鲁棒性协议无法刻画这种耦合结构。现有研究缺乏针对SCD任务特性的系统性对抗鲁棒性评估框架。

Method: 提出任务特定的评估框架，分离输出端攻击目标（组件级对象）与输入端时间扰动访问（单/双时间戳扰动），在四个数据集上对六种模型（CNN、Transformer、状态空间模型）进行组件级与时间级目标、单/双时间戳扰动、多种攻击方法和跨架构迁移性的系统实验。

Result: 发现最终语义变化预测可被严重破坏而二值变化定位相对稳定；单时间戳扰动或攻击目标可跨时间传播影响另一时间戳的预测；这些行为在不同架构族中普遍存在，但跨模型直接迁移远弱于白盒攻击。

Conclusion: SCD的对抗鲁棒性取决于完整的双时相预测通路而非单一分支或骨干架构族，本文提供了评估耦合双时相图像分析鲁棒性的结构化协议。

Abstract: Semantic change detection (SCD) is a bitemporal dense-prediction task that jointly identifies changed regions and their semantic states before and after change. Unlike single-image segmentation or binary change detection, SCD couples two temporal inputs with timestamp-wise semantic prediction, change localization, and final semantic-change decoding, creating adversarial dependencies that are not captured by conventional robustness protocols. We present a task-specific evaluation framework that separates output-side attack objectives from input-side temporal perturbation access, enabling systematic analysis of component vulnerability and cross-temporal propagation. Experiments on four datasets and six representative CNN-, Transformer-, and state-space-based models evaluate component-level and temporal objectives, single- and dual-timestamp perturbations, multiple attack methods, and cross-architecture transferability. The results show that final semantic-change predictions can be severely corrupted even when binary change localization remains comparatively stable, and that perturbations or attack objectives associated with one timestamp can propagate to the prediction of the other. These behaviors occur across different architecture families, while direct cross-model transfer remains considerably weaker than white-box attacks. The study demonstrates that adversarial robustness in SCD depends on the complete bitemporal prediction pathway rather than on an individual branch or backbone family, and provides a structured protocol for evaluating robustness in coupled bitemporal image analysis. Code is available at https://github.com/EricYu97/AdvSCD.

</details>


### [75] [Qwen-Video-Edit: Instruction-Based Video Editing by Repurposing an Image Editing Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14790)
*Yunpeng Bai,Yossi Gandelsman,Michaël Gharbi,Qixing Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需大规模训练视频扩散骨干的新思路：将强大的指令式图像编辑模型Qwen-Image-Edit直接应用于视频编辑。通过将Wan 2.1视频VAE的潜在帧排列成一张大型虚拟图像的瓦片（tile），复用图像位置编码，并用轻量级输入/输出投影桥接两个潜在空间，系统在Ditto-1M三元组上微调即可完成视频编辑，辅以Wan 2.2的少量去噪步骤作为时序增强器。实验表明，逐帧视频潜在表示与图像域足够接近，成熟图像编辑先验可经最小化适配迁移到视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于指令的视频编辑通常依赖视频预训练生成骨架（如视频扩散Transformer），训练成本高昂。作者探索另一条路径：能否利用成熟的指令式图像编辑模型直接编辑视频，避免大规模视频模型适配成本。

Method: 1) 将Wan 2.1视频VAE的潜在帧排列为一张大型虚拟图像（tile布局）；2) 复用Qwen-Image-Edit的图像位置编码于每个瓦片；3) 用轻量级输入/输出投影桥接图像与视频潜在空间，投影从编辑器自身的patchify/unpatchify层热启动，使静态视频在初始化时与模型已理解的图像嵌入完全一致；4) 在Ditto-1M编辑三元组上微调；5) 用Wan 2.2的少量去噪步骤作为可选的时序增强器。设计由一系列零训练观察驱动：图像编辑器可直接编辑"联系表"形式的视频；对联合编码与逐帧编码拼接的令牌保持一致；且对真实视频潜在表示的零样本编辑已达可识别程度。

Result: 实验表明，即使在视频潜在空间训练上投入巨大，逐帧视频潜在表示仍与图像域足够接近，成熟图像编辑先验仅需最小化适配即可成功迁移。系统在Ditto-1M数据上微调后即可完成视频编辑，并可通过Wan 2.2去噪步骤增强时序一致性。

Conclusion: 文章证明了另一种视频编辑范式的可行性：无需训练视频专用扩散骨干，通过复用强大图像编辑器的先验并辅以轻量适配，即可实现高质量指令式视频编辑，为视频编辑领域提供了降低训练成本的替代方案。

Abstract: Instruction-based video editing is commonly built on video-pretrained generative backbones: a video diffusion transformer is adapted, at considerable cost, to condition on a source video and an editing instruction. In this report we explore a different route and show that a strong instruction-based image editing model can edit videos by operating directly on video-VAE latents. Starting from Qwen-Image-Edit, we arrange the latent frames of a Wan~2.1 video VAE as tiles of one large virtual image, reuse the editor's image positional encoding for every tile, and bridge the two latent spaces with a pair of lightweight input/output projections warm-started from the editor's own patchify and unpatchify layers, so that at initialization a (static) video is embedded exactly as an image the model already understands. The whole system is then fine-tuned on the public Ditto-1M editing triplets, and a few denoising steps of Wan~2.2 serve as an optional temporal enhancer. We motivate the design with a chain of zero-training observations: the stock image editor already edits a video presented as a contact sheet; it is indifferent to whether the sheet's tokens come from one joint encode or from per-frame encodes stitched in latent space; and it even edits genuine video latents zero-shot to a clearly recognizable degree, leaving fine-tuning only a fidelity gap to close. Our results suggest that, despite the large investment in training video latent spaces, per-frame video latents remain close enough to the image domain that mature image editing priors transfer with minimal adaptation. Project Page: https://yunpeng1998.github.io/Qwen-Video-Edit-Page; Code: https://github.com/yunpeng1998/Qwen-Video-Edit; Model: https://huggingface.co/yunpeng1998/Qwen-Video-Edit.

</details>


### [76] [Anchor-Regularized Adaptation for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection with DINOv3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15196)
*Hyeongjun Choi,Juhun Lee,Davide Cozzolino,Luisa Verdoliva,Simon S. Woo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了锚点正则化自适应（ARA）方法，通过低秩适应捕获像素级伪影，同时利用冻结的锚点分类器保持预训练表示结构，在AI生成图像检测中实现了对齐与非对齐数据监督的互补，在九个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究认为对齐训练数据可以去除虚假相关性以提升泛化能力，但冻结的DINOv3线性探针即使在错配数据上训练也能表现优异。作者希望理解这一泛化现象的根本原因及其局限，并解决朴素混合对齐与错配数据会导致表示扭曲、限制泛化的问题。

Method: 首先分析发现冻结DINOv3依赖忠实表征真实图像空间的特征，但其最终层难以捕捉像素级伪影线索。为此提出Anchor-Regularized Adaptation (ARA)：使用低秩适应（LoRA）捕获像素级伪影，同时引入冻结的锚点分类器作为正则化约束，防止模型偏离原始预训练表示结构，从而在不牺牲泛化能力的前提下利用像素伪影线索。

Result: 该方法在九个多样且具有挑战性的基准上取得了最先进的性能，证明ARA能够实现来自错配和对齐数据的互补监督，从而实现更有效的检测。

Conclusion: ARA有效地调和了对齐与错配数据之间的冲突——一方面通过LoRA增强对像素级伪影的敏感性，另一方面通过锚点正则化保护预训练表示的完整性，最终实现泛化与检测能力兼得。

Abstract: Recent works in AI-generated image detection have shown that careful training data alignment can improve generalization by removing spurious correlations. However, linear probes on frozen DINOv3 representations achieve remarkably strong performance even when trained on misaligned datasets. Motivated by this result, we analyze the underlying rationale and the limits of this generalization. We find that frozen DINOv3 performs well because its decisions rely on features that faithfully represent the space of authentic images. At the same time, its final layer is less effective at capturing the subtle pixel-artifact cues that can be emphasized by aligned training data. We further observe that naively mixing aligned and misaligned data during adaptation improves sensitivity to such cues but at the cost of distorting the pre-trained representation, limiting generalization. To address this issue, we propose Anchor-Regularized Adaptation (ARA). We apply Low-Rank Adaptation to capture pixel-level artifacts while leveraging a frozen anchor classifier to avoid deviations from the original representation structure. This allows the model to exploit pixel-artifact cues without sacrificing generalization. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on nine diverse and challenging benchmarks, indicating that ARA enables complementary supervision from misaligned and aligned data for more effective detection.

</details>


### [77] [Audio-Visual Segmentation via Depth-Guided Collaborative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16285)
*Zhaojin Fu,Yuyang Hong,Qi Yang,Zili Wang,Kun Ding,Shiming Xiang,Bin Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DGCM-AVS，一个三模态（音频-视觉-深度）框架，通过引入估计深度作为空间结构线索来增强音频-视觉分割（AVS）中跨模态对齐的鲁棒性。该框架包含深度感知动态调制器（改善相邻物体分离并保持物体内部特征一致性）和深度引导渐进融合（以深度为桥梁渐进对齐音频与视觉特征）。在AVSS数据集上分别取得了M_J和M_F指标10.2%和8.7%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVS方法大多未显式建模几何线索（如相对距离和遮挡），限制了跨模态对齐的鲁棒性；而人类感知中空间结构自然与视听证据结合以准确定位发声物体。

Method: 提出DGCM-AVS三模态框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Depth-Aware Dynamic Modulator，利用深度信息改善相邻物体的分离同时保持物体内部特征一致性；(2) Depth-Guided Progressive Fusion，将深度作为中间桥梁，渐进性地将音频线索与视觉特征对齐。

Result: 与最先进方法相比，DGCM-AVS在AVSS数据集上的M_J相对提升10.2%，M_F相对提升8.7%。

Conclusion: 深度是AVS任务中一个有前景但尚未充分探索的模态，本文研究可能为该方向后续研究提供启发。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Segmentation (AVS) is a fundamental task in multimodal perception that performs pixel-level segmentation of sounding objects in videos by leveraging both visual and audio cues. It has broad applications in video understanding, human-computer interaction, and autonomous driving. However, most existing AVS methods do not explicitly model geometric cues such as relative distance and occlusion, thereby limiting the robustness of cross-modal alignment. In human perception, spatial structure is naturally integrated with audio-visual evidence to accurately localize sounding objects. Motivated by this, we incorporate estimated depth as a spatial structural cue for AVS and propose DGCM-AVS, a tri-modal framework that jointly models audio, visual, and depth information. Specifically, we design a Depth-Aware Dynamic Modulator to improve the separation of adjacent objects while preserving intra-object feature consistency. Furthermore, we propose Depth-Guided Progressive Fusion, which uses depth as an intermediate bridge to progressively align audio cues with visual features. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, DGCM-AVS achieves relative improvements of 10.2 percent in M_J and 8.7 percent in M_F on the AVSS dataset. We believe our study highlights depth as a promising yet underexplored modality for AVS and may encourage further research in this direction.

</details>


### [78] [Contrastive Energy Fields for Inference-Time Procedure Planning in Instructional Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16457)
*Mohamed Afham,Christoph Reich,Oliver Hahn,Daniel Cremers,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CEFITO是一种程序规划方法，通过学习动作条件化的表示空间，将规划重新表述为任务约束的优化问题，在推理时显式省略不相关动作，在两个基准数据集上达到最先进精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序规划方法（如前馈神经网络或扩散推理）将每个动作都视为可行的，缺乏强制执行任务特定逻辑约束的能力，导致无法排除不相关或不合理的动作。

Method: CEFITO首先学习一个预测器来表达动作条件化的表示空间，然后基于该表示空间将程序规划表述为任务约束的优化问题。与传统方法不同，它在推理时显式地排除不相关动作，对动作空间进行推理。

Result: 在两个已建立的程序规划基准上达到了最先进的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过将规划重构为任务约束优化并显式省略不相关动作，CEFITO实现了更有效的程序规划，验证了在动作空间上进行显式推理的优越性。

Abstract: Procedure planning seeks to estimate a sequence of actions to transition from an observed initial state to a given goal state. Current procedure planning approaches directly predict action sequences from latent representations using feed-forward neural networks or diffusion-based inference. These paradigms treat every action as plausible, lacking the ability to enforce task-specific logical constraints that render certain actions irrelevant or not plausible. We propose CEFITO, a procedure planning approach that learns a predictor to express an action-conditioned representation space. Based on this representation space, we formulate procedure planning as a task-constrained optimization problem. Unlike prior methods, CEFITO explicitly reasons over the action space by omitting irrelevant actions during inference-time planning. This reformulation enables effective procedure planning and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on two established procedure planning benchmarks.

</details>


### [79] [Cross-View Urban Sensing: Mapping Subjective Streetscape Perception via AlphaEarth Embeddings and Urban Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16310)
*Peilin Li,Pengfei Chen,Jingyu Wang,Zhifeng Yang,Tiansheng Chen,Mengjie Gong,Xiao Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CVLNet，一种跨视角学习网络，利用AlphaEarth遥感嵌入和多源城市上下文数据预测街道景观感知质量，无需推理时使用街景图像（SVI），在四个东南亚城市将感知估计从仅13-31%的道路覆盖扩展到完整路网，平均路段级Adjusted R²达0.76，并结合WorldPop人口数据量化环境不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 居民对城市街道景观的感知是公共健康和积极出行的重要因素，SVI虽广泛用于感知评估，但其覆盖不均和更新不规律限制了大规模测量，亟需一种无需SVI即可进行全域街道感知测绘的可扩展方法。

Method: CVLNet采用跨视角学习框架，从AlphaEarth嵌入和多种城市上下文数据中预测街景级感知，通过逐任务自适应门控联合建模五个感知维度，以预训练SVI-Percept模型生成的标签作为训练真值。

Result: 在四个东南亚城市（新加坡、吉隆坡、雅加达、马尼拉）评估中，CVLNet达到中位路段级Adjusted R²为0.76，比基线模型提升5.9-11.3%；消融实验表明两类特征互补；将感知图扩展到完整路网，并利用Deficit Palma Ratio量化不同人口密度、人口统计和土地用途群体间的暴露不平等。

Conclusion: 遥感可作为SVI的可扩展替代方案，实现城市街道景观感知的全域映射，从而更全面地评估城市环境不平等。

Abstract: Residents' perception of the urban streetscape is an important factor in public health, active mobility, and social wellbeing. Street view imagery (SVI) has emerged as a widely used data source for assessing these perceptual qualities, yet its uneven coverage and irregular updating limit large-scale measurement. Here, we present CVLNet, a Cross-View Learning Network that predicts street-level perception from AlphaEarth embeddings and multi-source urban contextual data without requiring SVI at inference. CVLNet applies per-task adaptive gating to jointly model five perceptual dimensions, using labels from the pretrained SVI-Percept model as ground truth. The proposed method is evaluated across four Southeast Asian cities: Singapore, Kuala Lumpur, Jakarta, and Manila. CVLNet achieves a median road-segment-level Adjusted $R^{2}$ of 0.76 and consistently outperforms the baseline models, with gains ranging from 5.9--11.3% across the five perceptual dimensions. Ablation experiments show that AlphaEarth features and urban contextual features contribute complementary information. We further produce citywide road-level streetscape perception maps for five subjective perceptual dimensions across all four cities, extending perception estimation from the 13--31% of the road network directly covered by available SVI to the complete road network of each city. Integrating these maps with WorldPop gridded population data, we quantify exposure inequality across population-density, demographic, and land-use groups using the Deficit Palma Ratio. These results demonstrate that remote sensing can serve as a scalable alternative to SVI for citywide streetscape perception mapping, enabling a more comprehensive assessment of urban environmental inequality.

</details>


### [80] [GRNEdit: Efficient General Video Editing from a New Binary-Evidence Perspective in Generative Refinement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16328)
*Feng Xie,Jiagao Hu,Fuhao Li,Zepeng Wang,Yuxuan Chen,Dahua Gao,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRNEdit 是一个轻量级两阶段视频编辑框架，通过将编辑语义编码为二进制位的保留或翻转决策，仅使用不到3%的条件参数（0.6M训练对），GRNEdit-2B和8B在OpenVE-Bench上分别达到4.03和4.18分，2B模型超越多个14B开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的通用视频编辑方法依赖资源密集型的条件注入方式（如重型分支或昂贵的源拼接），缺乏高效的编辑意图建模方法。作者提出能否用更轻量的方式表达编辑语义的问题。

Method: 提出GRNEdit两阶段框架：受GRN（通过比特组合编码视觉语义）启发，将编辑语义重新定义为二进制位上的局部保留或翻转决策。Stage I使用紧凑编码器将离散源编码转为连续证据信号，供GRN主干在二进制细化中整合；受空提示训练的启发，赋予空条件"不编辑"的含义，通过源重建监督。Stage II直接将编辑状态与其源保持对应状态比较，利用二者的差异修正未解决的目标比特决策。

Result: 仅用0.6M训练对和不到3%的条件参数，GRNEdit-2B和GRNEdit-8B在OpenVE-Bench上分别取得4.03和4.18分。2B模型超越多个14B开源编辑器，8B模型与领先开源编辑器性能相当。

Conclusion: GRNEdit证明了通过二进制位级编辑语义建模实现高效视频编辑的可行性，在极低的参数开销下达到与大型模型相当的性能，同时通过身份路径（identity pathway）增强了证据利用和内容保持能力。

Abstract: Instruction-based general video editing seeks to unify diverse editing operations within a single, intuitive interface. Existing approaches often rely on resource-intensive conditioning, using either heavyweight branches or costly source concatenation. Is there any efficient way to model editing intent? Thus, we introduce GRNEdit, a lightweight two-stage framework. GRN inspires our approach by encoding visual semantics through combinations of bits. Through task-specific fine-tuning, we take this representation further and recast editing semantics as local retain-or-flip decisions over individual bits. Source information is consequently modeled as coordinate-wise evidence supporting the observed binary states, while the GRN backbone remains responsible for resolving their global composition into coherent generative semantics. In Stage I, a compact encoder translates discrete source codes into continuous evidence signals, which GRN assimilates throughout binary refinement. Inspired by null-prompt training for classifier-free guidance, we further assign the null condition an editing-specific meaning: an empty instruction denotes no edit and is supervised through source reconstruction. This identity pathway not only implicitly strengthens evidence utilization and content preservation in Stage I, but also produces a source-preserving state in the same representation space as the edited state. Stage II can therefore directly compare each edited state with its source-preserving counterpart and use their discrepancy to revise unresolved target-bit decisions. Trained on only 0.6M pairs with less than 3\% conditioning parameters, GRNEdit-2B and GRNEdit-8B achieve scores of 4.03 and 4.18 on OpenVE-Bench. The 2B model outperforms multiple 14B open-source editors, while the 8B model performs on par with leading open-source editors.

</details>


### [81] [PersonaShot: Benchmarking Person-Centric Narrative Continuity in Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16717)
*Yuji Wang,Yuheng Chen,Teng Hu,Ran Yi,Yijia Hong,Han Feng,Weijian Cao,Chengjie Wang,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaShot，首个以人物为核心的多镜头视频生成叙事连贯性基准，包含约1000个多镜头片段和16个覆盖物理连续性、情感动态与电影语法的评估指标，并通过从大型多模态教师模型蒸馏出的轻量级专家评估器实现与人类专家判断高度一致的自动化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准主要评估角色外观或单镜头质量，无法衡量跨镜头切换时物理状态与情感状态的连贯性；同时缺乏针对不同指标（物理连续性、面部动态、电影关系）所需的不同证据类型（视觉、时序、关系）的分标准评估方法。

Method: 构建包含约1000个多镜头片段和16个指标的PersonaShot基准；从大型多模态教师模型中蒸馏推理能力到轻量级、按标准定制的专家评估器，每种评估器基于其对应指标所需的视觉、时序或关系证据进行推理，并与人类专家判断对齐；在三个时间层级（镜头内状态、跨镜头过渡、序列级轨迹）评估角色连贯性。

Result: 实验揭示了不同最先进模型在能力上的差异化表现，并显示出感知质量与跨镜头叙事连贯性之间存在的明显差距。即使视觉上具有吸引力的视频也常出现物理状态重置、情感突变和跨镜头电影关系断裂。人类研究证实评估器与专家判断具有高度一致性。

Conclusion: 多镜头视频生成的叙事连贯性与感知质量是两个相互独立的能力维度，现有模型在叙事层面仍有显著不足；PersonaShot可作为该方向系统化评估的基准工具，其蒸馏式专家评估器为缺乏精确标准评估的领域提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Video generation is rapidly evolving from single-shot clips to multi-shot narratives, where the human character serves as the core narrative anchor. However, existing benchmarks mainly assess character appearance or individual-shot quality, without measuring whether physical and emotional states remain coherent across cuts. They also rarely provide criterion-specific evaluation methods, although physical continuity, facial dynamics, and cinematic relations require different visual, temporal, and relational evidence. To address these limitations, we introduce PersonaShot, the first person-centric benchmark for narrative continuity in multi-shot video generation. PersonaShot contains approximately 1,000 multi-shot segments and 16 metrics spanning physical continuity, affective dynamics, and cinematic grammar. \textbf{\textit{1)} Narrative Continuity Benchmark:} We evaluate character coherence across three temporal levels: within-shot states, cross-shot transitions, and sequence-level trajectories. \textbf{\textit{2)} Human-Aligned Specialist Evaluators:} We distill reasoning from a large multimodal teacher into lightweight criterion-specific evaluators, each grounded in the visual, temporal, or relational evidence required by its metric, and align them with expert human judgments. \textbf{\textit{3)} Systematic Evaluation and Insights:} Our evaluation reveals distinct capability profiles across state-of-the-art models and a clear gap between perceptual quality and cross-shot narrative continuity. Even visually compelling videos frequently exhibit physical-state resets, abrupt affective shifts, and broken cinematic relations across shots. Human studies further demonstrate strong agreement between our evaluators and expert judgments.

</details>


### [82] [Perspective-Invariant Attack with Enhanced Transferability of Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15115)
*Kaisheng Liang,Yiming Cao,Bin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视角变换的对抗攻击方法PIA（Perspective-Invariant Attack），通过多自由度顶点采样策略系统地覆盖从2-DOF平移到8-DOF射影映射的透视变换层级，生成几何多样化的输入变体以减少对抗扰动对代理模型的过拟合，从而提升黑盒对抗样本的跨模型迁移性。同时提出PIA-Mix扩展框架，通过互补变换池将其与辅助方法结合进一步提升攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本在代理DNN上生成后可以成功欺骗其他黑盒DNN模型，这种跨模型迁移性对实际应用中的DNN构成严重安全威胁。现有的输入变换技术主要依赖限于自由度（DOF）的局部操作（如分块打乱和缩放），忽略了由视点变化自然产生的全局透视变换，限制了对抗迁移性的进一步提升。

Method: 提出PIA方法，核心是设计一种多DOF顶点采样策略，系统覆盖从2-DOF平移到8-DOF射影映射的完整透视变换层级；通过生成几何多样化的输入变体来缓解对抗扰动对代理模型的过拟合。此外提出PIA-Mix通用扩展，维护互补变换池并将透视变换与辅助变换方法高效结合。

Result: 在不同DNN架构、先进防御机制以及多模态大语言模型上的大量实验中，PIA和PIA-Mix均优于现有的最先进迁移攻击方法。

Conclusion: 透视变换作为一种全局、高自由度的输入多样性手段，能有效提升对抗样本的跨模型迁移性；PIA-Mix的通用扩展框架进一步证明该思路与现有方法具有良好互补性，为改善基于迁移的对抗攻击提供了新方向。

Abstract: Adversarial examples generated on a surrogate deep neural network (DNN) can often successfully fool other black-box DNN models. This cross-model transferability poses serious security threats to DNNs in practical applications. Input transformation techniques are widely used to enhance adversarial transferability by increasing the diversity of input images. However, existing methods primarily rely on local operations with limited degrees of freedom (DOF), such as block-wise shuffling and resizing, overlooking global perspective transformations that naturally arise from viewpoint changes. In this work, we propose a Perspective-Invariant Attack (PIA), which introduces a multi-DOF vertex sampling strategy that systematically covers the perspective transformation hierarchy from 2-DOF translation to 8-DOF projective mapping. By generating geometrically diverse input variations, PIA effectively reduces overfitting of adversarial perturbations to the surrogate model, thereby improving adversarial transferability. We further propose PIA-Mix, a generic extension that maintains a complementary transformation pool and efficiently combines our perspective transformation with auxiliary methods for improved transferability. Extensive experiments involving various DNN architectures, advanced defense mechanisms, and multimodal large language models (LLMs) demonstrate that PIA and PIA-Mix outperform state-of-the-art transfer-based attacks.

</details>


### [83] [Registration-Free Hyperspectral Reconstruction from RGB via a Permutation-Invariant Gram-Matrix Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14994)
*Jiangsan Zhao,Masayuki Hirafuji,Seishi Ninomiya,Jakob Geipel,Wei Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于排列不变性的监督原则，仅通过匹配丰度Gram矩阵来实现RGB到HSI的超分辨率重建，无需空间配准、已知相机响应函数或成对监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HSI融合方法通常假设LR-HSI与HR-RGB之间精确配准，且已知相机响应函数（CRF）。在实际多传感器场景中这两项假设难以满足，导致融合方法失效。

Method: 利用丰度解混后的Gram矩阵对像素排列不变而仅依赖共享材料组成这一特性，通过匹配丰度Gram矩阵学习RGB到HSI的映射；在此基础上使用残差光谱超分辨率函数直接将HR-RGB映射到HR-HSI，无需配准、已知CRF或成对监督。

Result: 在室内、自然场景和遥感三个基准上，该方法达到了与需要配准和CRF假设的现有方法相当的精度；在HR-RGB像素完全随机置换的情况下，现有融合方法崩溃而本方法重建结果不变；消融实验表明重建精度对具体Gram矩阵匹配的差异度量不敏感。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要来源于排列不变的监督原则而非损失函数的调优，该方法在假设被违反时仍保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reconstructing a spatially and spectrally high-resolution hyperspectral image (HR-HSI) from a low-resolution HSI (LR-HSI) and a high-resolution RGB image (HR-RGB) usually assumes precise registration and a known camera response function (CRF). Both assumptions are difficult to satisfy with different sensors. We remove both through a permutation-invariant supervision principle: the Gram matrix of an unmixed abundance map depends on shared material composition but not on pixel ordering. Matching abundance Gram matrices therefore allows RGB-to-HSI mapping to be learned without spatial correspondence and without a predefined CRF. Under a full random permutation of HR-RGB pixels, a state-of-the-art fusion method collapses, whereas our reconstruction is unchanged after inverse reindexing for evaluation. Building on this principle, a residual spectral super-resolution function maps HR-RGB directly to HR-HSI without registration, known CRF, or paired supervision. Across indoor, natural-scene, and remote-sensing benchmarks, the method achieves accuracy comparable to approaches that require these assumptions while remaining robust when they are violated. Loss ablations further show that reconstruction accuracy is largely insensitive to the specific discrepancy used to match the Gram matrices, indicating that performance arises primarily from the permutation-invariant principle rather than loss tuning.

</details>


### [84] [Representation Is Not Enough: Body-Localized Thermal Evidence for Contactless Stress and Craving Sensing in Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16087)
*Sachin Deb,Harshit Sharma,Asif Salekin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FABLE-Therm，一种弱监督的无接触热成像压力感知架构，在去除可穿戴设备后，通过保留身体区域、时间和编码器层面局部证据直到最终决策，在阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）数据上达到0.938 AUROC，并首次证明可以从无接触热视频中恢复自我报告的渴望（craving）。同时通过局部证据实现了部署失败的参与者级归因分析，揭示仅改善表示不足以实现公平部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备在生理监测中提供了压力反应的时空监督信号，但移除穿戴设备后这些监督消失，压力感知退化为弱监督的证据定位问题（clip级标签需追溯至产生它的身体区域和时刻）。此外，OUD患者早期康复期间持续佩戴穿戴设备困难，而压力是其复吸的重要诱因，因此需要无接触的压力感知方案。

Method: FABLE-Therm采用弱监督架构，在嵌入层面融合冻结的基座模型编码器，保留跨身体区域、时间和编码器专属表示的局部证据直到最终决策。理论基础解释了局部融合为何能优于特征拼接和预测平均。该架构用于固定热视频分析，并可进行参与者级局部证据分析。

Result: 在留出参与者的评估中达到0.938 AUROC；所学表示可迁移至自我报告的渴望（首次从无接触热视频恢复渴望的证据）；局部证据支持参与者级失败归因分析，发现仅改善表示不足以实现公平部署——从不充分服务群体补充数据只能恢复约一半的队列差距，其余反映个体间异质性。

Conclusion: 保留局部证据既支持准确感知，也支持对模型失败对象和原因的规范化分析。该模态无关的分解方法适用于具有可识别子群体的模型，此外还发布了首个队列结构化的无接触热成像OUD基准。

Abstract: Removing wearables from physiological monitoring also removes their supervision: the signal indicating where and when a stress response occurred. Contactless stress sensing therefore becomes a weakly supervised evidence-localization problem, where a clip-level label must be traced to the body regions and moments that produced it. We address this with FABLE-Therm, a weakly supervised architecture that preserves localized evidence across body regions, time, and encoder-specific representations until the final decision. FABLE-Therm fuses frozen foundation-model encoders at the embedding level, with theory explaining why localized fusion can outperform feature concatenation and prediction averaging. We study this problem in opioid use disorder (OUD), where stress is a major relapse trigger and sustained wearable use can be difficult during early recovery. Using fixed thermal video, FABLE-Therm achieves 0.938 AUROC on held-out participants, and its learned representation transfers to self-reported craving, providing, to our knowledge, the first evidence that craving can be recovered from contactless thermal video. Localized evidence also enables participant-level analysis of deployment failure. We find that improving representation alone is insufficient for equitable deployment: additional data from the underserved group would recover only about half of the cohort gap, while the remainder reflects person-to-person heterogeneity. This modality-agnostic decomposition applies to models with identifiable subpopulations. Together with the first cohort-structured contactless thermal OUD benchmark, our results show that preserving localized evidence supports both accurate sensing and principled analysis of who a model fails and why.

</details>


### [85] [SA-GEM: Scale-Adaptive and Geospatial Evidence-Modulated Token Pruning for Efficient Remote Sensing Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15075)
*Kexin Ma,Jing Xiao,Bowen Xing,Liang Liao,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-GEM，一个即插即用的框架，通过尺度自适应（任务驱动的分辨率选择）和地理空间证据调制的token剪枝（联合建模任务相关性、空间结构和局部冗余），在遥感视觉语言模型（RS-LVLMs）中实现更高效、更准确的高分辨率图像处理。实验表明，在XLRS-Bench上相比GeoLLaVA-8K在准确率上提升2.3%，同时推理速度提升2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RS-LVLMs在遥感图像多模态理解中面临根本性瓶颈：随着输入分辨率线性增加，视觉token数量呈二次方增长，而重要的视觉证据天然稀疏，在扩大的序列中被进一步稀释。现有token剪枝方法依赖与尺度无关的分辨率策略和孤立的显著性线索，无法实现任务对齐的细粒度自适应和整体证据保持。

Method: SA-GEM包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级路由器，基于查询依赖的token粒度需求选择适当分辨率；(2) token重要性调制器，联合建模任务相关性、空间结构和局部冗余，以保留整体的地理空间证据。该框架是即插即用的，可统一任务自适应的token粒度分配与整体地理空间token重要性调制。

Result: 实验证明更高的分辨率并非普遍有益，一旦达到足够的粒度，token质量比token数量更重要。在多个基准上，SA-GEM在准确率和效率上均优于现有剪枝方法。在XLRS-Bench上，相比GeoLLaVA-8K在准确率上提升2.3%，并实现2.4倍的总推理加速。

Conclusion: SA-GEM揭示了"粒度充分后质量优于数量"的核心洞见，通过尺度自适应和证据调制相结合的策略，有效解决了RS-LVLMs中高分辨率处理带来的token膨胀与证据稀释问题，在保持乃至提升准确率的同时显著降低推理开销，为遥感场景下的高效多模态理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: RS-LVLMs have advanced multimodal understanding of Earth observation imagery, yet their performance is fundamentally constrained by high-resolution processing, as visual token counts grow quadratically with linear input resolution while important visual evidence is inherently sparse and increasingly diluted across the expanded sequence. Existing token pruning methods largely rely on scale-agnostic resolution policies and isolated importance cues, limiting task-aligned granularity adaptation and holistic evidence preservation. To address this, we present Scale-Adaptive and Geospatial Evidence-Modulated Token Pruning (SA-GEM), a plug-and-play framework that unifies task-adaptive token granularity allocation with holistic geospatial token importance modulation. Specifically, a lightweight router selects the resolution based on query-dependent token granularity, while a token importance modulator jointly models task relevance, spatial structure, and local redundancy to preserve holistic geospatial evidence. We show that higher resolution is not universally beneficial and, once sufficient granularity is reached, token quality matters more than token quantity. Experiments across various benchmarks demonstrate that SA-GEM achieves consistent gains in both accuracy and efficiency over existing pruning methods. On XLRS-Bench, it surpasses GeoLLaVA-8K by 2.3% in accuracy with a 2.4 times total inference speedup.

</details>


### [86] [ChainSpace: A Chained-Reasoning Paradigm for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15788)
*Xiaohan Zhang,Feng Gu,Xudong Rao,Xuhao Pan,Tao Wei,Zhou Pan,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ChainSpace，一种链式推理范式，将空间推理组织为状态保持的多轮过程。作者构建了ChainSpace-Bench（人工标注的多轮基准与Chain-Aware评估指标）和ChainSpace-Pipeline（基于模拟器的链式监督生成框架）。实验表明该基准能揭示孤立问题准确率无法捕捉的链级失败，且用小量模拟数据训练的模型在链式基准上达到开源最佳并迁移到多个外部空间智能基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的空间推理方法将空间推理视为独立的问答实例，导致模型可以通过捷径作答，且缺乏对持续性空间理解的监督信号，无法忠实评估和有效训练模型的真空间智能。

Method: 提出ChainSpace链式推理范式：(1) 将空间问题组织为逻辑约束且联合一致的问题链，后续问题依赖前序轮次建立的空间约束；(2) 构建ChainSpace-Bench人工标注真实世界多轮基准及Chain-Aware评估指标；(3) 构建ChainSpace-Pipeline模拟器驱动的链式结构监督数据生成框架用于模型训练。

Result: ChainSpace-Bench能暴露孤立问题准确率无法捕获的链级失败；使用相对少量的模拟器生成链式数据训练的模型在ChainSpace-Bench上达到开源模型最佳性能，并可竞争性地迁移到多个外部空间智能基准。

Conclusion: ChainSpace为空间智能提供了更忠实的评估范式和更数据高效的学习框架，确立了链式推理作为空间基础模型训练与评估的有效范式。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires foundation models to maintain coherent spatial state across interactions with the physical world. However, existing data-centric approaches typically treat spatial reasoning as independent question-answer instances, enabling shortcut-based answering and providing limited supervision for persistent spatial understanding. To address this, we introduce ChainSpace, a chained-reasoning paradigm that structures spatial reasoning as a state-preserving multi-round process. In this paradigm, spatial questions are organized into logically constrained and jointly consistent chains, where later questions depend on spatial constraints established in earlier rounds. Following this principle, we instantiate ChainSpace-Bench, a manually annotated real-world multi-round benchmark with a Chain-Aware Metric, and ChainSpace-Pipeline, a simulator-based chain-structured supervision generation framework for spatial intelligence training. Experiments show that ChainSpace-Bench exposes chain-level failures that are not captured by isolated question accuracy. Additionally, with a relatively small amount of simulator-generated chained data, models trained by ChainSpace-Pipeline achieve the best performance among open-source models on ChainSpace-Bench and transfer competitively to multiple external spatial intelligence benchmarks. These results establish ChainSpace as an effective paradigm for more faithful evaluation and more data-efficient learning of spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [87] [GenRouter: Unified Workflow Routing for Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16721)
*Harold Haodong Chen,Zhiyu Hou,Wen-Jie Shu,Weilin Ruan,Yingjie Xu,Litao Guo,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenRouter是首个面向智能体图像生成的统一工作流路由框架。它通过GenCanvas标准化异构智能体管线为通用原语和可执行模板，并基于需求画像、经验匹配和帕累托过滤将不同提示词自适应路由到最优工作流。实验表明，与重型静态管线相比，GenRouter在保持视觉对齐的同时将执行成本降低95%以上、延迟降低65%，并能通过经验积累实现零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型已解决原始像素合成的基础挑战，研究重心转向满足日益复杂的用户需求。现有的智能体图像生成工作流虽能增强静态推理（如外部知识检索、迭代推理），但大多以固定"一刀切"拓扑独立运行，导致严重的计算不匹配问题——简单查询被迫通过计算密集型管线处理，造成资源浪费。

Method: 1) 提出GenCanvas：将异构智能体管线标准化为通用基础原语和可执行模板的统一抽象空间；2) 在统一空间上构建GenRouter路由框架，通过三个关键步骤实现自适应路由：(i) 需求画像（demand profiling）、(ii) 经验匹配（experience matching）、(iii) 帕累托过滤（Pareto filtering），将异构提示词路由至最优工作流。

Result: 在多个基准上的广泛实验表明，GenRouter实现了优越的视觉对齐效果，同时将执行成本降低超过95%、延迟降低65%（与重型静态管线相比）。此外，系统通过累积经验持续自我进化，实现稳健的零样本泛化，进一步提升性能并减少一半的计算开销。

Conclusion: GenRouter证明了统一工作流路由在智能体图像生成中的有效性，解决了静态"一刀切"管线带来的计算不匹配问题。经验驱动的自我进化机制使系统能够持续优化路由决策，在保持视觉质量的同时显著降低计算和延迟成本，为智能体图像生成提供了可扩展、高效的基础设施。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of text-to-image (T2I) generation models has effectively solved the foundational challenge of raw pixel synthesis, shifting the community's focus toward fulfilling increasingly intricate user requests. While recent agentic image generation workflows enhance static inference with advanced capabilities like external knowledge retrieval and iterative reasoning, they mostly operate in isolated silos with fixed ``one-size-fits-all" topologies. This inevitably leads to severe compute-mismatch, where simple queries are forced through computationally heavy pipelines. To bridge this gap, we present GenRouter, the first unified workflow routing framework for agentic image generation. We first formulate GenCanvas, standardizing diverse agentic pipelines into a universal set of foundational primitives and executable templates. Operating over this unified space, GenRouter adaptively routes heterogeneous prompts to their optimal workflows via (i) demand profiling, (ii) experience matching, and (iii) Pareto filtering. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that GenRouter achieves superior visual alignment while reducing execution costs by over 95% and latency by 65% compared to heavyweight static pipelines. Furthermore, the system continuously self-evolves via accumulated experience, enabling robust zero-shot generalization that boosts performance and halves computational overhead.

</details>


### [88] [AlignJEPA: Predictive Vision-Language Alignment for Remote Sensing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15456)
*Md Aminur Hossain,Omkumar Vaghasiya,Rajeev Ranjan Dwivedi,Vinod Kurmi,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlignJEPA是一个基于JEPA思想的预测式视觉-语言对齐框架，用于遥感基础模型。它使用预训练的AnySat视觉编码器和RemoteCLP文本编码器，仅训练轻量级的预测对齐网络，通过掩码预测和多尺度聚合将视觉token映射到文本空间，实现自然语言检索与图文对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遥感基础模型虽然能够跨传感器、分辨率和地理区域提供可迁移的地球观测表征，但大多数与自然语言的弱对齐限制了其在自然语言档案搜索、图文检索和问题条件分析方面的应用。

Method: AlignJEPA采用JEPA启发的预测式对齐框架，包含预训练的AnySat视觉编码器和RemoteCLP文本编码器，仅训练轻量级预测对齐网络。该网络通过掩码感知的多尺度预测对齐器，在细粒度、区域和全局尺度聚合可见token，用跨尺度Transformer联合建模，并通过学习查询池化将表示投影到文本空间。训练结合了语义预测与双向对比检索损失。

Result: 在BigEarthNet.txt上进行自然语言Sentinel检索训练与评估，在RSICD上验证跨数据集适应能力，并使用RSVQA作为封闭集表示探针进行评估。

Conclusion: AlignJEPA提供了一种参数高效的方式将地球观测基础模型与自然语言对齐，克服了单一全局图文对比对齐的局限性，实现了更细粒度的视觉-语言语义预测。

Abstract: Remote sensing (RS) foundation models provide transferable Earth observation representations across sensors, resolutions, and geographies, yet most remain weakly aligned with natural language, limiting natural-language archive search, image-text retrieval, and question-conditioned analysis. We propose AlignJEPA, a JEPA-inspired predictive vision-language alignment framework for remote sensing foundation models. AlignJEPA uses a pretrained AnySat visual encoder and a RemoteCLIP text encoder while training only a lightweight predictive alignment network. Instead of relying on global image--text contrastive alignment alone, the framework predicts remote-sensing text embeddings from masked visual foundation-model tokens. Its mask-aware multi-scale predictive aligner aggregates visible tokens at fine, regional, and global scales, jointly models them with a cross-scale Transformer, and projects the resulting representation into the text space using learned query pooling. Training combines semantic prediction with bidirectional contrastive retrieval. We train and evaluate AlignJEPA on BigEarthNet.txt for natural-language Sentinel retrieval, evaluate cross-dataset adaptation on RSICD, and use RSVQA only as a closed-set representation probe. AlignJEPA provides a parameter-efficient route for aligning Earth observation foundation models with language.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Accuracy: Assessing Calibration of Geospatial Foundation Models and Their Sensitivity to Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16614)
*Nils Lehmann,Jakob Gawlikowski,Burak Ekim,Isaac Corley,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑了地质空间基础模型（GeoFMs）仅依据标准基准条件下的平均准确率进行排名和选择的常见做法，指出该协议过于狭隘。通过16个冻结编码器、四个分类和五个分割数据集、两个正交压力轴的系统实验，发现所有编码器在腐蚀加剧时性能均下降且排名发生变化；EO预训练编码器与ImageNet预训练编码器在干净准确率和校准上无差异，但在分布偏移下EO模型会更严重地过度自信。CKA分析表明这与表征刚性有关。温度缩放和深度集成无法抵消退化，高斯过程探针只能以三倍恶化干净数据ECE为代价在严重云层下将ECE减半。作者主张评估应在多种条件和指标下进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GeoFMs的排名和选择仅基于标准基准条件下的平均准确率，忽略了实际部署中对地球观测（EO）关键任务至关重要的校准能力（模型置信度与正确性的一致性）。这种过于狭窄的评估协议无法反映模型在真实世界分布偏移下的表现，需要更全面的多角度分析。

Method: 在16个冻结编码器、四个分类数据集和五个分割数据集上，沿两个正交压力轴（腐蚀强度和腐蚀类型）进行系统评估。对比EO预训练与ImageNet预训练编码器在干净准确率、干净校准、分布偏移下的校准与稳定性表现。利用CKA分析表征刚性，并应用三种不确定性量化方法（温度缩放、深度集成、高斯过程探针）进行校准修正实验，同时进行选择性预测实验。

Result: 所有编码器在腐蚀加剧时性能均退化且排名发生变化；EO预训练与ImageNet预训练在干净条件下无显著差异，但分布偏移下EO模型在所有级别和所有腐蚀家族中都更严重地过度自信；CKA表明EO嵌入在腐蚀下移动更少（表征刚性）但丢失同样多的任务信息且保持过度自信；温度缩放和深度集成无法抵消退化，高斯过程探针在严重云层下ECE减半但代价是干净数据ECE增至三倍；基于置信度的弃权机制无法推迟围绕错误自信预测的决策。

Conclusion: 应改变GeoFMs的评估范式，基准排名和评估应在多种条件和指标下进行，以更全面地评估模型发展进展，缩小与现实世界部署场景之间的差距。

Abstract: Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs) are most commonly ranked and selected by accuracy on standard benchmark conditions via averaged ranks. We show that this protocol is too narrow: the promised deployment in critical EO tasks requires further angles of analysis, mainly calibration, the agreement between a model's confidence and its correctness. Across 16 frozen encoders, four classification and five segmentation datasets, and two orthogonal stress axes, every encoder degrades as corruption intensifies, and the ranking changes as well. Across the four classification benchmarks, EO-pretrained and ImageNet-pretrained encoders are indistinguishable on clean accuracy and clean calibration, and EO pretraining provides no more stability under shift than ImageNet pretraining. Under shift the GeoFMs drift further into overconfidence than the ImageNet-pretrained encoders, at every grade and in every corruption family. A centered kernel alignment (CKA) analysis ties this to representational rigidity: EO-pretrained embeddings move less under corruption while losing just as much task information and remaining overconfident. We apply three commonly explored uncertainty quantification methods and find that temperature scaling and deep ensembles cannot counteract the degradation, while a Gaussian-process probe roughly halves ECE under severe cloud only by tripling it on clean data. In selective prediction experiments, we find that confidence-based abstention cannot defer around confidently wrong predictions, and advocate that benchmark rankings and evaluations should therefore operate across a multitude of conditions and metrics to more holistically evaluate model development progress and close the gap to real world deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [Bitstream Action Recognition is Byte Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15695)
*Fangcheng Li,Chaoran Huang,Tianyi Liu,Wenyang Liu,Kejun Wu,Qiong Liu,You Yang,Zhengguo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BRACE框架，用于比特流动作识别（BAR），通过双分支字节建模架构和Intact-Anchored Representation Alignment（IARA）使受损比特流表示对齐到完好比特流的稳定锚点，实现对比特流损坏的鲁棒识别。同时提出四参数RBCS模拟器构建首个大规模BAR数据集BAR-D，并建立包含14种方法的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动作识别依赖比特流的成功像素解码，但存储或传输过程中的比特流损坏会导致严重视觉伪影甚至解码失败，严重影响可靠性。现有方法缺乏对解码依赖性的克服和对损坏鲁棒性的保障，因此需要一种不需要修复比特流、直接在表示空间中处理受损比特流的动作识别方法。

Method: 提出BRACE双分支字节建模架构，将受损比特流和完好比特流视为同一动作的两种字节实现。通过IARA在嵌入层和决策层将受损表示对齐到完好锚点表示，并使用UAS（Unreliable-Anchor Suppression）抑制不可靠锚点的影响，全程在表示空间中进行，无需修复比特流。同时提出四参数RBCS模拟器，模拟传输和存储中的比特翻转和字节丢失错误，构建了BAR-D数据集。

Result: 在BAR-D数据集（包含BAR-Stanford40和BAR-PPMI子集）上对14种像素域、压缩域和比特流域方法进行大规模基准测试，BRACE在比特流损坏鲁棒性上优于所有对比方法。消融实验验证了RBCS数据增强和IARA的有效性。

Conclusion: BRACE通过锚定完好表示来引导受损比特流生成稳定表示，有效解决了比特流损坏下的动作识别问题，为BAR领域提供了新的思路和资源。RBCS和BAR-D为未来研究提供了可复现的模拟工具和大规模基准。

Abstract: Conventional action recognition typically relies on successful pixel decoding of the bitstream. However, bitstream corruption during storage or transmission may cause severe visual artifacts or even decoding failure, posing a significant challenge to reliable action recognition. Bitstream Action Recognition (BAR) aims to overcome the dependency on decoding and the vulnerability to corruption. In this paper, we propose a novel BAR framework, Bitstream Recognition via Anchoring Corrupted Embeddings (BRACE). BRACE is a dual-branch byte-modeling architecture that treats a corrupted bitstream and its intact counterpart as two byte realizations of the same action. This guides the generation of rich and stable representations for robustness to corruption through Intact-Anchored Representation Alignment (IARA). The intact representation serves as a stable anchor, and the corrupted one is aligned to it at the embedding and decision levels under Unreliable-Anchor Suppression (UAS), entirely in representation space and without repairing the bitstream. To address the scarcity of corrupted bitstreams in practice, we introduce the Real-world Bitstream Corruption Simulator (RBCS), a four-parameter simulator that reproduces bit-flip and byte-loss errors arising in transmission and storage. Building on RBCS, we construct the first large-scale BAR dataset (BAR-D), which comprises the BAR-Stanford40 and BAR-PPMI subsets and spans diverse corruption types and severity levels. Finally, we build a large benchmark on BAR-D involving 14 action recognition methods from the pixel, compressed, and bitstream domains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BRACE has superior robustness to bitstream corruption than all comparison methods. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed RBCS augmentation and IARA.

</details>


### [91] [RoofGS: Roofline-Guided End-to-End Acceleration of 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15785)
*Yang Luo,Yan Gong,Yongsheng Gao,Jie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoofGS是一个针对3D高斯溅射的高分辨率渲染优化框架。通过阶段式Roofline分析，识别出前端受全局内存带宽限制、光栅化受指令吞吐量限制两个不同瓶颈。针对内存瓶颈设计了分辨率自适应的32位量化深度排序键；针对计算瓶颈设计了范围感知的位级快速指数近似。在RTX 4090上实现4K分辨率下10.1倍端到端加速（61→616 FPS），仅损失0.028 dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽然能实现实时新视角合成，但在高分辨率GPU上仍然受限。以往方法通常采用通用内核加速，未针对不同硬件阶段的瓶颈进行差异化优化，导致优化效率不高。

Method: 1) 通过阶段式Roofline分析识别前端为内存带宽瓶颈、光栅化为指令吞吐量瓶颈。2) 针对内存瓶颈：设计分辨率自适应的量化深度排序键，压缩至32位。3) 针对计算瓶颈：利用不透明度剔除后的有界指数范围，引入范围感知的位级快速指数近似，并推导出每像素误差上界。4) 补充优化：内核融合、紧凑属性存储、剔除策略、双像素求值。

Result: 在RTX 4090上，4K分辨率下实现10.1倍端到端加速，吞吐量从61 FPS提升至616 FPS，PSNR仅损失0.028 dB。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于Roofline模型识别硬件瓶颈并针对瓶颈类型实施差异化优化（而非泛化加速）的策略，能显著提升3DGS在高分辨率下的渲染性能，且几乎不损失质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel-view synthesis but remains limited on GPUs at high resolutions. Through a stage-wise Roofline characterization, we identify two distinct hardware bottlenecks: global memory traffic dominates the front end, whereas instruction throughput limits rasterization. Guided by this analysis, we develop RoofGS, a rendering framework that applies bottleneck-specific optimizations rather than generic kernel acceleration. For the memory-bound front end, we design a resolution-adaptive quantized depth sorting key that compresses each key to 32 bits. For the compute-bound rasterizer, we introduce a range-aware bit-level fast exponential approximation tailored to the bounded exponent range after opacity culling, with a derived per-pixel error bound. These two core techniques are complemented by additional optimizations (kernel fusion, compact attribute storage, culling, dual-pixel evaluation) that additionally reduce memory traffic and improve instruction-level parallelism. Experiments show that RoofGS achieves a 10.1$\times$ end-to-end speedup over 3DGS at 4K on an RTX 4090, increasing throughput from 61 to 616 FPS, with only a 0.028 dB PSNR loss.

</details>


### [92] [An Empirical Study of Training Pixel-Space Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16887)
*Dengyang Jiang,Ruoyi Du,Zhennan Chen,Dongyang Liu,Zanyi Wang,Mingzhe Zheng,Xiangpeng Yang,Huanqia Cai,Aiming Hao,Yuming Jiang,Peng Gao,Harry Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究提出了一种"潜空间到像素空间"的训练策略，即在潜空间高效预训练获得生成先验，然后在后训练阶段切换至像素空间微调，最终使像素空间扩散模型在性能上匹配或超越潜空间对应模型，同时实现3.18至4.75倍的端到端推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管像素空间扩散模型在生成建模中日益重要，但现有研究多局限于小规模或类别条件设置，缺乏大规模实际训练配方。作者发现直接在大规模像素空间预训练收敛速度显著慢于潜空间，因此需要探索一种结合两者优势的高效训练策略。

Method: 作者首先通过大规模实证研究对比像素空间与潜空间预训练的收敛差异，发现像素空间直接预训练收敛较慢。基于此观察，提出潜到像素（latent-to-pixel）策略：先在潜空间高效预训练以获取生成先验，再在后训练阶段切换至像素空间。随后系统研究了该转换过程中的关键设计选择，包括权重初始化、数据组成、预测目标、解码器架构和噪声调度，最终确定一套实用的配方。

Result: 所提出的配方使得像素空间模型在性能上匹配或超越潜空间对应模型，同时带来3.18到4.75倍的端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: 该研究为像素空间生成提供了实用的经验见解和设计指南，表明通过合理的潜到像素转换策略，像素空间扩散模型可以在效率和性能上同时超越潜空间方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates an increasingly important topic in generative modeling: pixel-space diffusion models. Although numerous studies have explored this topic, most focus on small-scale or class-conditional settings. Consequently, a practical recipe for training pixel-space models that rival or exceed well-established latent-space counterparts remains elusive. Through a comprehensive empirical study, we first observe that direct large-scale pre-training in pixel space converges substantially more slowly than in latent space. This observation motivates a latent-to-pixel strategy that acquires generative priors efficiently in latent space and transitions to pixel space during post-training. We then systematically investigate the key design choices governing this transition, including weight initialization, data composition, prediction target, decoder architecture, and noise schedule, and identify a practical recipe that makes the resulting pixel-space models match or outperform their latent-space counterparts while delivering 3.18 to 4.75 times end-to-end inference speedups. We hope that our findings provide useful empirical insights and practical guidelines for future research on pixel-space generation.

</details>


### [93] [BASeg: Boundary-Aware Remote Sensing Segmentation with Structural Penalties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15683)
*Yuexi Song,Kailai Sun,Zhuoyu Wang,Mingyi He,Paul Pu Liang,Shenhao Wang,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MABL边界损失函数和BASeg分割框架，解决遥感语义分割中细粒度特征和边界细节捕获不足的问题，并构建了全球10个城市的GCD-25k基准数据集，在多个遥感基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感语义分割方法难以捕获细粒度物体特征和边界细节；当前广泛使用的数据集缺乏城市形态多样性；生成图像上的分割问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Mahalanobis-Angle Boundary Loss (MABL)，通过马氏距离加权和角度感知惩罚联合建模结构重要性和边界方向；构建BASeg框架，集成全局视觉状态空间模块(GSM)和跨特征融合模块(CFM)以捕获长距离上下文依赖和细粒度局部细节；建立全球10城市基准数据集GCD-25k。

Result: 在四个遥感基准数据集上，BASeg一致性地优于现有方法，mIoU提升最高达2.8%，并在多样场景中产生更精确的物体边界分割；将MABL集成到多种现有分割架构中均能一致提升性能。

Conclusion: MABL可作为即插即用的模块增强多种分割架构的边界感知能力，BASeg框架在遥感语义分割上展现出良好的鲁棒性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is a core computer vision task in the remote sensing field, accelerating advancements in ur- ban development, agriculture, ecology, water resources, and environmental monitoring. However, recent methods usually struggle to capture fine-grained object features and bound- ary details. Besides, current widely used datasets often lack city morphology diversity and segmentation on generative im- ages remains largely unexplored. To address these issues, we propose a Mahalanobis-Angle Boundary Loss (MABL) that explicitly enhances boundary and shape consistency. MABL jointly models structural importance and boundary orientation through Mahalanobis distance-based weighting and angle- aware penalty. It can be readily integrated into diverse seg- mentation architectures and consistently improves their accu- racy. Built upon MABL, we introduce BASeg, a boundary- aware remote sensing segmentation framework with Struc- tural Penalties. BASeg integrates a Global Visual State Space module (GSM) with a Cross-Feature Fusion module (CFM) to capture both long-range contextual dependencies and fine- grained local details. Additionally, we establish a global 10- city benchmark dataset (GCD-25k) to facilitate accurate build- ing and road segmentation. Extensive experiments on four remote-sensing benchmarks demonstrate that BASeg consis- tently outperforms existing methods, achieving up to a 2.8% improvement in mIoU while producing more accurate object boundary segmentation across diverse scenes. Moreover, integrating MABL into multiple existing segmentation archi- tectures consistently improves performance across datasets, demonstrating its robustness and broad applicability.

</details>


### [94] [Deep Thought Alignment: Trajectory-Level Latent Distillation for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16316)
*Ao Shen,Yongheng Zhang,Yinghui Li,Manning Wang,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Latent-OPD，在现有On-Policy Distillation（输出token分布匹配）的基础上，增加轨迹级别的潜在表示蒸馏，将学生模型中间到后期层与教师模型更深层对齐，实现帧高效的视频推理，在六个基准上优于仅输出级蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型（LMMs）在视频推理中因处理大量视觉信息的高计算成本而受限，需要知识蒸馏将其推理能力迁移到小型高效模型。现有On-Policy Distillation（OPD）仅匹配输出token分布，但视频推理依赖跨多帧的证据累积，输出级监督只能捕获通过token预测表达的信息，无法直接约束推理过程中形成的潜在表示。

Method: 提出Latent-OPD，在OPD基础上增加轨迹级潜在蒸馏。具体包括两部分：(1) 关注每条轨迹末尾位置的隐藏状态，该位置能有效汇总累积的视觉证据和推理上下文；(2) 引入渐进式教师前瞻（progressive teacher-lookahead）策略，将学生中后期层与教师更深层逐步对齐。

Result: 在六个视频推理基准上的实验表明，Latent-OPD一致优于仅输出级OPD。改善在帧数有限、长视频或需要复杂证据聚合的任务中尤为显著。

Conclusion: Latent-OPD被确立为一种高效的帧经济视频推理方法，通过轨迹级潜在蒸馏补充输出级蒸馏，有效解决了视频推理中多帧证据累积的表示学习问题。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) for video reasoning have long been hindered by the high computational cost of processing vast amounts of visual information. This dilemma motivates the transfer of the reasoning capabilities of large models to smaller, more efficient ones. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers a promising solution by matching output-token distributions along student-generated trajectories. However, video reasoning often depends on evidence accumulated across multiple frames. In this context, output-level supervision only captures information expressed through token predictions and does not directly constrain the latent representations formed during reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose Latent-OPD, which augments OPD with trajectory-level latent distillation. Specifically, our method focuses on the position at the end of each trajectory, where hidden states effectively summarize the accumulated visual evidence and reasoning context. Furthermore, we introduce a progressive teacher-lookahead strategy, which aligns middle-to-late student layers with increasingly deeper teacher layers. Experiments on six video reasoning benchmarks show that Latent-OPD consistently outperforms output-only OPD. Notably, the improvements are particularly pronounced in scenarios with limited frames, long videos, or tasks requiring complex evidence aggregation. These results establish Latent-OPD as a highly effective approach to frame-efficient video reasoning.

</details>


### [95] [DRAFE: Domain-Robust Asymmetric Fusion of Heterogeneous Detection Transformers for Cross-City Fine-Grained Traffic Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16632)
*Divine Yao Agbobli,Geoffery Eyram Agorku,Israel Afriyie,Kwadwo Amankwah-Nkyi,Marvin Osei-Kuffour,Richmond Owusu Duah,Bright Seglah,Kelvin Asamoah Terkper,Kwabena Amoako Adjei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRAFE框架，结合LW-DETR和RF-DETR两个独立训练的检测器，通过两阶段训练（公共数据预训练+挑战赛数据微调）和推理阶段的多种融合策略，在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6上实现跨城市细粒度交通目标检测，取得0.4022 mAP、排名第6的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统中的目标检测需要同时满足细粒度车辆识别和跨城市域泛化的要求，而现有方法难以兼顾这两点，因此需要一种域鲁棒的检测集成框架。

Method: DRAFE包含两阶段训练策略：(1)先在多样公共交通数据集上预训练互补的LW-DETR和RF-DETR检测器，使用伪标签扩展和人在回路标注精炼，构建了包含6,049张图像和203,619个标注的语料库；(2)再在Project Hafnia Track 6数据集上进行符合挑战赛要求的微调。推理时采用锚点条件类别一致匹配、可靠性加权坐标融合、一致性感知置信度重校准和互补假设恢复等融合策略。

Result: 在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6上取得0.4022 mAP，在25个参赛队伍中排名第六，比基线初步集成方案提升了0.0553 mAP。

Conclusion: DRAFE通过互补检测器集成和多策略融合，有效改善了跨城市细粒度交通目标检测的性能，但仍有进一步提升空间（仅排名第六）。

Abstract: Deep learning-based object detectors are fundamental to intelligent transportation systems, enabling traffic monitoring, vehicle analytics, and infrastructure management. However, achieving both fine-grained vehicle recognition and robust cross-city domain generalization remains challenging. We present the Domain-Robust Asymmetric Fusion Ensemble (DRAFE), which combines independently trained LW-DETR and RF-DETR detectors for cross-city fine-grained traffic object detection. DRAFE employs a two-stage training strategy that first pretrains complementary detectors on diverse public traffic datasets using pseudo-label expansion and human-in-the-loop annotation refinement, producing a curated corpus of 6,049 images and 203,619 annotations, before challenge-compliant fine-tuning on the Project Hafnia Track 6 dataset. At inference, DRAFE applies anchor-conditioned class-consistent matching, reliability-weighted coordinate fusion, agreement-aware confidence recalibration, and complementary hypothesis recovery. On AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6, DRAFE achieves 0.4022 mAP, ranks sixth among 25 participating teams, and improves by 0.0553 mAP over a preliminary ensemble evaluated under identical benchmark conditions.

</details>


### [96] [Graph Neural Assisted Actor-Critic for Latency-Efficient Edge Vision System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16142)
*Alam Noor,Luis Almeida,Kai Li,Jiyan Wu,Miguel Gutiérrez Gaitán,Eduardo Tovar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种GCN辅助的A2C深度强化学习模型，用于无人机视频传输延迟优化。通过仅传输帧中像素相关子区域而非整个视频帧，结合拉格朗日对偶与梯度下降防止约束违规，实验表明该方法相比其他DRL和最先进模型显著降低了视频帧传输延迟和误检率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视觉系统在禁飞区监控等场景中需要将视频流传输到地面服务器以供操作员协助，但现有视频传输技术的延迟成本过高，严重影响操作员协助的有效性，亟需一种降低视频传输延迟的优化方法。

Method: 提出一种GCN辅助的A2C深度强化学习系统模型。利用GCN探索特征相关像素组的隐藏表示，并监督A2C模型选择像素相关子区域进行传输而非完整视频帧；同时结合拉格朗日对偶形式和梯度下降，防止延迟优化过程中的收敛失效以及过罚和欠罚约束违规问题。

Result: 实验结果显示，与其他DRL方法和最先进模型相比，GCN辅助的A2C模型在无人机视觉系统中同时降低了视频帧传输延迟和误检率。

Conclusion: GCN辅助的A2C深度强化学习框架能够有效优化无人机视频传输延迟，通过选择性传输像素相关区域而非完整帧，在降低传输延迟的同时保持较低的误检率，为无人机实时监控系统提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: UAV on-board vision systems are widely used for different activities, including monitoring in no-fly zones. In this case, the vision-equipped UAV streams a video to a ground server where an operator assists its activities. The latency of video transmission has a profound impact on the effectiveness of the operator assistance. However, most techniques available for video transmission still incur significant latency costs. In this paper, we propose a graph convolutional neural network-assisted (GCN-Assisted A2C) deep reinforcement learning (DRL) system model to find the optimal pixel-correlated area of a suspicious object. We combine the Lagrangian dual form with gradient descent to prevent lack of convergence and over- and under-penalization constraint violation during latency optimization. The proposed system model sends a sub-group pixel-correlated area of the frame from the UAV to the server rather than the transmission of the whole video frame. The proposed framework utilizes the GCN model to explore hidden representations of feature-correlated groups of pixels. Moreover, the GCN supervises the A2C model, which selects a subgroup to enhance transmission latency, thus supervising the training of UAV actions in A2C. Experimental results show that GCN-assisted A2C reduces video frame transmission latency together with false detection rate in UAV vision systems over other DRL and state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [97] [Robust structure from motion for aerial-ground images via detector-free feature matching and multi-view track refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15251)
*San Jiang,Hui Wang,Xing Zhang,Zhongwen Hu,Zhijun Wang,Ruisheng Wang,Wanshou Jiang,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种旋转鲁棒的免检测器匹配网络，结合多视图航迹优化用于增量式运动恢复结构（ISfM），解决航空-地面图像因视角、尺度和旋转差异导致的特征匹配难题。方法包含旋转感知特征提取、多尺度注意力变换、双向特征匹配和多视图航迹优化四个模块，在真实航空-地面数据集上相比LoFTR将5°位姿误差的AUC提升93.9%，ISfM重建精度提升27.6%-32.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空-地面图像的三维重建对高精度城市模型至关重要，但两类图像之间严重的视角、尺度和旋转差异使得鲁棒特征匹配极具挑战性，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出旋转鲁棒的免检测器匹配网络，包含四个模块：(1) 旋转感知特征提取，用全向状态空间块（OSS Block）沿八个对称方向扫描建模长程空间依赖，生成旋转不变特征图；(2) 多尺度注意力变换，利用四叉树注意力构建层级token金字塔，隔离高关联token区域并丢弃无关区域，以线性复杂度捕获长程上下文；(3) 双向特征匹配，采用对称的从粗到细匹配方案，粗匹配在互近邻约束下计算双向Softmax置信矩阵，细匹配用MLP回归亚像素坐标偏移；(4) 多视图航迹优化，利用集成索引结构评估局部空间邻近性并将断裂子航迹链接到最高置信度锚点，确保ISfM流程中的特征重复性。

Result: 在真实航空-地面数据集上，相比LoFTR，5°位姿误差的AUC提升了93.9%，在ISfM重建中达到最高精度，精度提升范围为27.6%-32.7%。

Conclusion: 所提方法为航空-地面图像的集成三维重建提供了可靠解决方案，有效提升了跨视角、跨尺度、跨旋转场景下的特征匹配鲁棒性和重建精度。

Abstract: Integrated 3D reconstruction from aerial-ground images is essential for generating high-precision urban 3D models, yet severe variations in viewpoint, scale, and rotation make robust feature matching highly challenging. To address these limitations, this study introduces a rotation-robust detector-free matching network coupled with multi-view track refinement for incremental Structure from Motion (ISfM). The proposed workflow features four key modules. First, rotation-aware feature extraction replaces traditional convolutions with an Omnidirectional State Space Block (OSS Block) that selectively scans across eight symmetrical directions to model long-range spatial dependencies and synthesize rotation-invariant feature maps. Second, multi-scale attention transformation utilizes quadtree attention to build a hierarchical token pyramid that isolates high-association token regions and discards irrelevant areas, capturing long-range context with linear computational complexity. Third, bi-directional feature matching executes a symmetric coarse-to-fine matching scheme where coarse alignment computes dual-direction Softmax confidence matrices under mutual nearest neighbor constraints, and fine alignment uses a multi-layer perceptron to regress sub-pixel coordinate offsets. Finally, multi-view track refinement employs an integrated indexing structure to evaluate localized spatial proximity and link disjoint sub-tracks to the highest-confidence anchor point, ensuring stable feature repeatability across the ISfM pipeline. By using real aerial-ground datasets, experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method improves AUC at 5° pose error by 93.9% compared with LoFTR and achieves the highest precision in ISfM reconstruction, with the improved accuracy ranging from 27.6% to 32.7%. The proposed method provides a reliable solution for integrated 3D reconstruction of aerial-ground images.

</details>


### [98] [Towards Zero-Shot Domain Generalization for ID Cards Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16591)
*Mario Nieto-Hidalgo,Juan M. Espin,Juan E. Tapia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于国家身份证件呈现攻击检测（PAD）的方法，利用原型网络头部（Prototypical Network head）配合EfficientNet-V2-b0骨干网络，每类仅需四张真实样本即可构建可靠的类原型；同时引入 episodic 训练机制，固定PAD类别而变化卡片域，使网络学习通用的攻击线索。在多国数据集和公开的DLC-2021基准上，该方法平均等错误率（EER）约为9%，优于传统softmax和CLIP零样本基线，且仅需单一来源国家的数据即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家身份证件的呈现攻击检测受限于缺乏公开的真实样本，导致系统难以跨国家泛化。现有方法依赖大量标注数据，而真实证件样本稀少且涉及隐私，需要一种在极少样本条件下仍能有效工作的PAD方案。

Method: 提出两项核心创新：（1）基于EfficientNet-V2-b0骨干网络的Prototypical Network头部，每个类别仅需四张真实样本即可创建可靠的类原型；（2）episodic训练范式，保持PAD类别固定同时变化卡片域，迫使网络学习与具体卡片类型无关的通用攻击线索。

Result: 在大型多国数据集和公开的DLC-2021基准上评估，方法达到约9%的平均等错误率（EER），在仅使用单一来源国家数据的条件下仍优于传统softmax分类器和CLIP零样本基线。

Conclusion: 该方法在保护隐私的前提下实现了准确的呈现攻击检测，同时大幅减少了数据收集需求，为跨司法管辖区的远程注册提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Presentation-Attack Detection (PAD) for national ID cards is limited by the lack of publicly available genuine samples, making it difficult for systems to generalize across countries. This paper introduces two main innovations: (1) a Prototypical Network head using an EfficientNet-V2-b0 backbone that requires only four genuine samples per class to create reliable prototypes; and (2) an episodic training regime that keeps PAD classes fixed while varying the card domain, allowing the network to learn universal attack cues.
  Evaluated on a large multi-country dataset and the public DLC-2021 benchmark, this method achieves an average Equal Error Rate of around 9\%, outperforming conventional softmax and CLIP zero-shot baselines even with data from a single source country. This approach provides accurate, privacy-preserving PAD while minimizing data collection, facilitating scalable cross-jurisdictional remote onboarding.

</details>


### [99] [A Unified Backbone--Expert Framework with Relation-Token and Residual--Classifier Interfaces for Automatic Modulation Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15160)
*Zhixiang Deng,Houbiao Li,Zongyong Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的骨干-专家框架（backbone-expert framework），针对自动调制识别（AMR）在不同观测长度下的表示瓶颈，通过公共卷积状态空间骨干加两个专门接口，分别处理短序列（显式滞后感知复平面关系令牌补偿信息损失）和长序列（门控多尺度残差细化模块+固定平均分类器协作），在RML2016.10b和HisarMod2019上分别达到67.28%±0.14%和87.19%±0.77%的平均准确率，并通过三种子消融、原生长度跨配置测试和受控窗口研究验证了专家接口解耦优于一刀切架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动调制识别面临不同观测长度下的表示瓶颈，单一模型架构难以在不同序列长度下同时取得优异表现，需要针对序列长度差异设计专门的表征方案。

Method: 提出统一的骨干-专家框架：包含一个公共卷积状态空间骨干和两个专门接口。短序列路径在编码前注入显式滞后感知复平面关系令牌以补偿信息损失；长序列路径设计门控多尺度残差细化模块修正特征图，并结合固定平均分类器协作以利用互补证据。

Result: 在RML2016.10b上达到67.28%±0.14%的平均准确率，在HisarMod2019上达到87.19%±0.77%（三次运行的均值±样本标准差）。通过三种子消融、原生长度跨配置测试和受控窗口研究进一步验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: 专家接口解耦（面向不同序列长度分别设计接口）显著优于一刀切（one-size-fits-all）架构，证实了针对不同观测长度进行专门化建模在AMR任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic modulation recognition (AMR) faces distinct representation bottlenecks under varying observation lengths, where a single model architecture often fails to excel. To address this, we propose a unified backbone-expert framework with a common convolutional state-space backbone and two specialized interfaces. For short sequences, we inject explicit lag-aware complex-plane descriptors as relation tokens before encoding to compensate for information loss. For long sequences, we design a gated multi-scale residual refinement module to correct the feature map, combined with a fixed-averaging classifier collaboration to harness complementary evidence. Our framework achieves overall average accuracies of 67.28 \pm 0.14% on RML2016.10b and 87.19 \pm 0.77% on HisarMod2019 (mean \pm sample standard deviation over three runs), respectively. The framework's efficacy is further validated through three-seed ablations, native-length cross-configuration tests, and controlled window studies, confirming the benefit of expert-interface decoupling over one-size-fits-all architectures.

</details>


### [100] [Convolution-Free Holistic Multivariance Decomposition Layer for Efficient Hyperspectral Image Classification Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16241)
*Süha Tuna,Ülker Başar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为全模式分解（Holistic Multivariance Decomposition, HMD）的新型端到端可微神经层，用于高光谱图像分类的特征提取，通过将独立单模式变化与协作高阶交互显式分离，在保持与CNN相当的泛化能力和训练稳定性的同时，大幅减少参数数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的高光谱图像分类特征提取方法存在明显不足：刚性张量分解（如Tucker、CP、Tensor Train）无法捕获复杂的空间-光谱相互依赖关系，而基于卷积神经网络的方法虽然性能好但参数庞大、计算成本高昂。因此需要一种既能捕捉复杂光谱-空间交互结构又具备参数效率的新方法。
